# Analytics Metrics

_which metrics to track, dashboards, benchmarks, reading the data._

Evidence base: 1908 extracted items from 608 videos by #7Phantom, 1Coal, 1_Bruce, Alex Builds Games, AllThingsHow (guide), AlvinBlox, ArTheDev, Arow, BloxxedDev, BubuTheDev, Business of Creators, ByteBlox, CORSO, CaberJaber, CallMeDogey, Creators on the Blox, Crusherfire, Cumin, Daiorg, Deconstructor of Fun, DitchyDevelopments, Duckable, Emancyphur, Erthlord, GDC Festival of Gaming, GameMakers, GamesBeat, Generalist Programmer, GnomeCode, GosuGamers, GuideRealm, ItsZyonic, JustDucksey, JustVeryYellow, Last Level Studios, Lnor Roblox, LovJak, Matt The Method, MonarchDev, MonzterDEV, MoseyTube, Mulch, OtakuKart, Perstile, PineappleDev, Platform Aeronaut (transcript), PocketGamer.biz, Poke, PolarBear, Possible Panda, Quez, Rapstrike GFX, Raul, RoBuilder, RoTrends (tracker), RoWatcher (tracker blog), Roblox, Roblox Creator Docs, Roblox Creators, Roblox DevForum, Roblox Investor Relations, Roblox Newsroom, Roblox public API, Roox4, RoyalDev, Seibo, SimTek Game Development, SmartyRBX, Stewiepfing, StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data), Suphi Kaner, TechSpectrum, ThatGuyEvan, TheDankDeveloper, TheDevKing, ThePurpleGuy, Tizzy, Tubefilter, Ventures, Vindooly, VuukStudios, Where The Money Went, Yann, anomalousDucky, bubDEV, eGamers, janisjanis01, kra_DeZ, rasdev, studio rook., tef, waveshine, {EISABOOS} "RBX" (2016-07-05 to 2026-11-01). Roblox changes quickly: check the date on each source and see Date context below.

## Overview
Adds creator and influencer acquisition measurement as a new area: use a 7-day minimum click window, judge campaigns on day-365 or lifetime installs, and measure organic uplift with a per-market proxy, because tracked links understate impact. Also adds: set the KPI before designing a live event, track gacha and item-level economics, check spend buckets for payer clusters, prioritise backlog by LTV impact divided by effort, keep an owned raw data copy reconciled against MMP and store dashboards, and read the first weeks of a new game as mixed. Most sources are indie and self-reported, mostly mobile rather than Roblox-specific. The influencer figures come mainly from one 2026 source and one client dataset.

## Rules and findings
- **Instrument analytics before launch** (multiple-creators): Set up funnels and event tracking before closed beta or launch, and watch feature engagement and drop-off on launch day to catch game-breaking flows. Define engagement, retention and monetization KPIs before development. A ~50% day-over-day DAU drop was cited as a game-breaking-bug signal. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-07 22:08](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=1328), [Roblox Creators 2021-08 13:25](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=805), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies), [Roblox Creators 2023-06 2:09](https://youtu.be/pWF06iX9_Gg?t=129), [Roblox DevForum 2017-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Prioritise retention (D1/D7/D30) as the core health metric** (multiple-creators): Retention is the key metric; without it you tread water with constant updates. Track D1, D7, D30 and week-later returns to judge updates. Use D1 plus average session time as early indicators of long-term retention. Check D1/D7 against benchmarks before paying to promote a new game. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-07 35:47](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=2147), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 1:00:29](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=3629), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 1:01:09](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=3669), [Roblox DevForum 2022-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794), [Roblox DevForum 2022-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Diagnose retention by window and with first-session data** (multiple-creators): Low D1 means onboarding problems (confused, frustrated, slow to fun). Low D7 means weak progression. Low D30 means missing end-game content. A late big drop-off signals a content problem. Use New User First Session Retention (Tizzy's healthy example, self-reported: ~95% at 30s, 87% at 1m, 65% at 5m; others 20-30%) and time onboarding tasks to find the slow tail. Get D1 and session time to benchmark before driving traffic. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2023-09 0:13](https://youtu.be/LpAU6TheAZ4?t=13), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 26:09](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=1569). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Read monetization through conversion, ARPPU and ARPDAU together** (multiple-creators): Conversion rate (share who ever spend) is a key metric since first purchasers tend to buy again. High conversion with low ARPPU means one purchase and nothing after, so build post-first-purchase content. Low ARPPU suggests unoptimised item value. Low RPPU suggests a bad first purchase or over-generous first offer. ARPDAU = conversion x ARPPU. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-08 21:50](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=1310), [Roblox Creators 2021-08 23:55](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=1435), [Roblox Creators 2022-02 9:20](https://youtu.be/RfpMZZFy8Zw?t=560), [Roblox DevForum 2022-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-monetization-metrics-for-the-analytics-dashboard/1826688), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Build custom monetisation tracking** (multiple-creators): Base analytics don't show how your purchase systems perform. Track time to first and repeat purchase, stage-to-stage conversion, who buys what, earned vs purchased currency, promo-code redemptions, and which gamepasses sell. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/monetisation-101-how-to-effectively-generate-revenue-in-your-roblox-game/246902), [Roblox DevForum 2019-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/monetisation-101-how-to-effectively-generate-revenue-in-your-roblox-game/246902), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/balance-virtual-economies), [Roblox 2018-07 23:34](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=1414), [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Track the economy: sources, sinks and wallet balance** (multiple-creators): Track every currency source, sink and purchase. A constantly growing currency means too few sinks. Wallet spikes mean too few sinks; always zero means players need more currency. Avoid unlimited sources (Adopt Me money tree capped at $100/day). Compare currency bought vs earned during sales. A gold surplus with flat purchases signals unbalanced earning. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 5:56](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=356), [Roblox 2018-07 7:19](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=439), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 25:28](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1528), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 26:02](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=1562), [Roblox Creators 2024-07 3:13](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=193). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use funnels for onboarding and feature flows** (multiple-creators): Fire an event per step (open store, select, buy, equip) to locate drop-off. Start with onboarding, log after the step completes, track micro-steps rather than chunked ones, send the first step explicitly, validate step numbers server-side, and use custom fields to compare variants. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-08 28:00](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=1680), [Roblox Creators 2022-12 20:31](https://youtu.be/0PEBFsguCdw?t=1231), [Roblox Creators 2024-07 1:27](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=87), [Roblox Creators 2024-07 14:49](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=889), [Tizzy 2026-07 9:05](https://youtu.be/FnsIL4zPCQg?t=545). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use level, lesson and section analytics for difficulty and content tuning** (multiple-creators): Track level completion, per-lesson failure rates and tries to pass, time by section and device, win/loss ratio per wave (low for hard modes, ~3-4 for easy), progression speed and heat maps. Rework high first-try-failure lessons and polish where time is spent. Default Roblox analytics alone are not enough. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-08 1:17:49](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=4669), [Roblox Creators 2021-08 1:19:18](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=4758), [Roblox Creators 2022-06 57:44](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=3464), [Roblox Creators 2023-06 0:47](https://youtu.be/pWF06iX9_Gg?t=47), [Roblox Creators 2023-06 1:29](https://youtu.be/pWF06iX9_Gg?t=89). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Compare against same-genre benchmarks and fix the weakest metric first** (multiple-creators): Use the benchmark scorecard (100+ DAU). Read the 50th-90th percentile as a spread of similar games. Focus first on below-benchmark metrics (session time, D1, payer conversion, QPTR); push below-50th metrics above 50th. CallMeDogey says below 75th needs work; Zach (Deconstructor of Fun) targets the 90th. Benchmarks are not used in ranking. A 6-month high can still be below benchmark. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 1:22](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=82), [Roblox 2024-09 30:11](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=1811), [Roblox DevForum 2024-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-new-experience-overview-with-insights-benchmarks-realtime/3108840), [Roblox DevForum 2024-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-new-experience-overview-with-insights-benchmarks-realtime/3108840), [Roblox DevForum 2023-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-similar-experience-benchmarks-broader-access/2210285). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Do not over-optimise every metric individually** (multiple-creators): Build a fun game and monitor the six metrics rather than min-maxing them (Roblox talk). Analytics are one input alongside intuition. Strong stats don't guarantee success because discovery luck matters (1_Bruce). CallMeDogey, by contrast, argues all six in green is the one true way to grow. Sources: [Roblox 2025-09 35:36](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=2136), [Roblox 2025-09 35:36](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=2136), [Roblox Creators 2022-01 21:22](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=1282), [1_Bruce 2025-12 7:44](https://youtu.be/wKI3PBO3bB0?t=464), [CallMeDogey 2026-02 1:21](https://youtu.be/IQm6zE2fKUI?t=81). Newest source 2026-02-20.
- **Judge session length in context** (multiple-creators): Use average session time (total playtime / sessions) to size content and compare before/after features. Social games benchmark 10+ minutes; 15-20 min is strong and 12 decent for large games. A 3-minute session with 33% likes signals weak engagement. Prefer retention over session length for young casual audiences. Model players as active ~75% of session time. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2022-02 58:50](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=3530), [Roblox Creators 2021-11 53:28](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=3208), [Roblox Creators 2022-02 4:03](https://youtu.be/RfpMZZFy8Zw?t=243), [Roblox Creators 2023-09 0:08](https://youtu.be/N2l0BDgSNtQ?t=8), [Roblox DevForum 2022-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Track DAU, MAU and DAU/MAU stickiness** (multiple-creators): DAU/MAU shows how often players return; a declining ratio means new players aren't forming habits, so improve onboarding, events or hooks. Compare MAU year over year, same month, to separate seasonality from decline. Recent decline suggests lack of new content; persistent low engagement points to the core loop. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2023-09 0:08](https://youtu.be/N2l0BDgSNtQ?t=8), [Roblox DevForum 2022-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794), [Roblox DevForum 2025-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-track-new-user-retention-by-daily-and-weekly-cohort/3817030), [Roblox Creators 2022-02 3:21](https://youtu.be/RfpMZZFy8Zw?t=201), [Roblox Creators 2023-09 0:50](https://youtu.be/N2l0BDgSNtQ?t=50). Newest source 2025-07-15.
- **Slice data to find the cause of a change** (multiple-creators): Don't trust aggregates. Break down by platform, device tier, source, version, country and age until you find the smallest affected segment. A drop exactly at publish points to that update. A drop only on low-memory Android points to resources, not design. Use outlier detection and 'Explore this metric'. Tizzy's console-only tutorial bug hid inside 77% overall completion (self-reported). Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 17:53](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=1073), [Roblox 2026-10 18:33](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=1113), [Roblox 2026-10 19:15](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=1155), [Roblox DevForum 2023-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-personalized-insights-for-your-experience/2600369), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/insights). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Break retention and funnels down by acquisition source** (multiple-creators): Compare sources on new-user funnel, 7D playtime, D7 retention and 30D revenue per user. A retention drop may only reflect a traffic-mix shift. Create share links per channel. Tizzy watched D1 by source (search ~31%, friends ~27%, self-reported). Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-retention-by-acquisition-source-and-select-your-benchmark-set/4010157), [Roblox DevForum 2023-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-home-recommendations-conversion-rate-and-recent-icon-updates/2731683), [Roblox DevForum 2023-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-home-recommendations-conversion-rate-and-recent-icon-updates/2731683), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Read retention as first-play-date cohorts** (multiple-creators): Retention charts use first play date on the x-axis, so a change shows in that day's new cohort. D7 and D30 are empty for recent dates. Daily cohort table covers 10 days, weekly 10 weeks. Use Recent or Resurrected views, as All Users can look low. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-track-new-user-retention-by-daily-and-weekly-cohort/3817030), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Mind data latency and thresholds** (multiple-creators): Most signals update within 24h; monetization KPIs lag 2 days; ad metrics need 48h; event charts take ~24h (View Events is near real time). Tizzy says up to ~3 days early in launch. Dashboard needs >10 DAU and 10 play hours for 7 days; benchmarks and charts need 100+ DAU. Playtime/CTR need a few thousand daily visits to be reliable. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-with-the-improved-recommended-for-you-algorithm-and-analytics-for-creators/3587441), [Roblox DevForum 2022-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-monetization-metrics-for-the-analytics-dashboard/1826688), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Account for seasonality and use trend overlays** (multiple-creators): Use 28-day period-over-period overlays and 7-day moving averages. Before blaming your game for an impression drop, check your updates, algorithm changes, platform volume/seasonality (Saturday peaks, summer, holidays) and competitors. Signal changes can precede impression drops by a few days. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard), [Roblox DevForum 2025-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-with-the-improved-recommended-for-you-algorithm-and-analytics-for-creators/3587441), [Roblox Creators 2025-10 8:37](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=517), [Roblox DevForum 2025-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-with-the-improved-recommended-for-you-algorithm-and-analytics-for-creators/3587441), [Roblox Creators 2022-02 3:21](https://youtu.be/RfpMZZFy8Zw?t=201). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Treat every update as a hypothesis and verify afterwards** (multiple-creators): Phrase it as 'if I change X, stat Y improves', cross-reference qualitative, quantitative and competitor data, then check D1, D7, session time and first-time experience. Keep or revert. Change incrementally, avoid feature creep, focus on one weak stat (Tizzy's bounce rate ~21%->~15% reportedly doubled impressions; unverified). Sources: [Tizzy 2026-06 4:43](https://youtu.be/HFcegpPPvpI?t=283), [Tizzy 2026-06 9:08](https://youtu.be/HFcegpPPvpI?t=548), [Tizzy 2026-06 14:12](https://youtu.be/zoz3PSMqets?t=852), [Tizzy 2026-07 9:17](https://youtu.be/EZzztgpSaTU?t=557), [Tizzy 2026-07 12:18](https://youtu.be/EZzztgpSaTU?t=738). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Run experiments correctly** (multiple-creators): Write a hypothesis, check MDE (about +/-100% means significance unlikely; <0.01% means a shorter test works), run the full 14-60 days (min 2 weeks), act only when the CI excludes 0, use 2-3 variants, restart on SRM, ignore first-24h harm metrics for launch decisions. Monetisation/D1 are noisier. Weigh tradeoffs, avoid overlapping tests, document. Under ~500-1,000 DAU or at ~0.5-1M players (Grow a Garden) experiments struggled; use feedback instead. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385), [Roblox DevForum 2025-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385), [Roblox DevForum 2025-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385), [Roblox DevForum 2025-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385), [Roblox Creators 2025-12 14:08](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=848). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use experiments and configs for risky or UI changes** (multiple-creators): Test risky changes on a small segment (e.g. new players), compare session length and D1, then roll out via Configs. Test hint timing, starting currency, button placement, matchmaking (100% rollout). Test configs in Studio first; call GetValue only when enrolling a player. Older advice (2022) was a manual 50/50 one-week A/B. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 4:39](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=279), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/ui-ux-design), [Roblox 2025-09 3:46](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=226). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Set up alerts, error reports and performance monitoring each release** (multiple-creators): Check crash rate, error report ('New errors since', platform/OS breakdown) and Performance by Place Version after each publish. Set a 7-14 day baseline, thresholds above normal (1.2% -> 2.5-3%), duration 5 for volatile metrics, one alert with Breakdown, structured names. Needs 100+ DAU. A crash jump from 3-4% to 45% signals a bad build. Sources: [Roblox 2025-09 20:53](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=1253), [Roblox 2026-10 1:55](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=115), [Roblox 2026-10 3:17](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=197), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Instrument events correctly: server-only, post-success, custom fields** (multiple-creators): Economy, funnel and custom events are server-only in published games, logged after success, validated against exploiters, amounts positive, with itemSKU. Use up to 3 custom fields (CustomField01-03, strings, prefixed values, <8,000 combos) instead of many event names/currencies; batch events. Rate limit 120+20xCCU/min; SKUs beyond 100 group into Other; 100 custom events max. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2024-07 16:10](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=970), [Roblox Creators 2024-07 6:55](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=415), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use custom dashboards and calculated metrics** (multiple-creators): Define your own metrics (wins / match starts, sources - sinks), group dashboards by purpose, put top KPIs in summary cards, set aggregation correctly, publish edits and pin to the sidebar. Revisit periodically as old metrics stop answering current questions. Sources: [Roblox 2026-09 17:19](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=1039), [Roblox 2026-10 6:01](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=361), [Roblox 2026-10 6:41](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=401), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-dashboards). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Analyse updates, live ops and events against baselines** (multiple-creators): Judge updates by earnings vs baseline (weekday average plus weekend), playtime, D1/W1/M1 retention and new users; run a retrospective per release. Falling purchases or completion across releases signals fatigue. Compare events 7 days before, during, 7-14 days after; judge by goal (ARPDAU/revenue vs DAU/returning users); monitor hourly to catch uncompletable events. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2022-01 26:30](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=1590), [Roblox Creators 2022-04 0:05](https://youtu.be/QYOaK1hXN1Y?t=5), [Roblox Creators 2022-04 0:46](https://youtu.be/QYOaK1hXN1Y?t=46), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 49:03](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=2943), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 49:07](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=2947). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Segment by demographics, platform and payer status** (multiple-creators): Segment engagement and monetization by age, country, language, platform and OS. Audience shapes pricing and conversion. If the top 15% of payers give far less than ~50% of revenue, improve top-spender retention. Check older cohorts' share in mature games. Console users retain and monetize better and are older. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2022-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794), [Roblox DevForum 2022-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-monetization-metrics-for-the-analytics-dashboard/1826688), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/demographics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/demographics), [1_Bruce 2026-02 3:10](https://youtu.be/i3RQXxZeVYc?t=190). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use Home Recommendation signals for discovery diagnosis** (multiple-creators): Check Acquisition > Home Recommendations: play-through rate, play days and playtime first, then secondary signals (spend days, co-play, Deep play-through, 7-day qualified sessions, bounce rate where lower is better). Fix the weakest signal. These diagnose organic distribution, not ads. An RFY impressions drop can be a trailing indicator (official). Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery), [Roblox 2026-10 9:06](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=546), [Roblox DevForum 2026-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-we-are-improving-home-this-year/4502571), [Roblox DevForum 2026-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/recommended-for-you-algorithm-improvements-that-better-value-long-term-retention/4684575), [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-by-building-games-people-want-to-play/4779042). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Measure ads by quality, not clicks** (multiple-creators): Use Ads Manager as source of truth (Creator Analytics blends sources). Wait ~24h learning and 48h reporting delay; check ROI at day 30. Track CTR, CPP, play rate, 7D playtime and 30D Robux per converted player. MoseyTube targets ~2.5% CTR and ~$0.008 per play (self-reported); from day 5 stop ads and fix gameplay if retention is poor. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-12 28:00](https://youtu.be/DHvfEB8myyU?t=1680), [Roblox Creators 2025-06 50:08](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=3008), [Roblox Creators 2025-06 50:50](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=3050), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Track rewarded video: opt-in, frequency, impressions, EPM** (multiple-creators): Key metrics are opt-in rate (target 15%+) and daily frequency (3+). Underearning is usually too few impressions. Low opt-in means poor discoverability. Check retention and playtime for harm, test via experiments, tag placements. EPM is a secondary quality signal. Earnings = EPM x impressions / 1000. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/obby), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/tycoon), [Roblox Creators 2026-05 3:57](https://youtu.be/R6HbXwyuHzY?t=237), [Roblox Creators 2026-05 5:44](https://youtu.be/R6HbXwyuHzY?t=344), [Roblox Creators 2026-05 6:25](https://youtu.be/R6HbXwyuHzY?t=385). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use native Roblox analytics; know the GameAnalytics history** (multiple-creators): Built-in Creator Dashboard is free and needs no setup. Older advice (2017-2021) was GameAnalytics (~1 hour setup); a 2023 talk still suggested it as funnels weren't supported. Native funnels and economy now exist. Don't rely on GameAnalytics acquisition data. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 37:16](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=2236), [Roblox DevForum 2017-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083), [Roblox DevForum 2017-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 35:47](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=2147), [Roblox 2023-09 20:09](https://youtu.be/9eaev-ypgWo?t=1209). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Verify revenue, Premium, thumbnails, feedback and misc signals** (multiple-creators): Monetization KPIs omit refunds; use My Transactions and exclude your own purchases. Premium Playtime Score gives daily feature feedback (Robux Earned lags 28 days). Judge thumbnails by QPTR and CTR. Use Feedback chart for downvote spikes; favorites signal retention (MoseyTube). Watch update unfollow rate, subscriptions, avatar item analytics. AI reports can err. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2022-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-monetization-metrics-for-the-analytics-dashboard/1826688), [TheDankDeveloper 2024-07 9:39](https://youtu.be/maRjVb1NAYc?t=579), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/monetization), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/passes), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Read the post-spike baseline, not the spike** (multiple-creators): Ad pushes, updates and viral moments spike concurrency, then fade. Judge a change by where players settle about a week later. Duckable's CCU went 25 to 225 in two days, then dropped to near zero overnight. Compare settled baselines, not peaks. Sources: [rasdev 2026-10 5:19](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=319), [rasdev 2026-10 5:19](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=319), [janisjanis01 2026-09 10:02](https://youtu.be/WJpNCB5J9bg?t=602), [Duckable 2026-05 12:37](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=757), [Duckable 2026-05 11:15](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=675). Newest source 2026-10-17.
- **Use percentiles (P10/P50/P90), not averages** (one-creator-repeated): Averages hide the experience of slow devices and outliers. Read ClientFpsP10 and memory percentiles for low-end players, and session-time percentiles by platform/OS. For lower-is-better metrics (ping, age difference), P10 is best case and P90 is worst case. The Filter By menu offers average, P10, P50 and P90. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alert/metrics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/analytics). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Pair telemetry with qualitative playtests** (multiple-creators): Telemetry shows how many players are affected; small qualitative samples explain why. During prototyping, skip metrics and rely on qualitative feedback. Add analytics close to launch, when most systems exist. Sources: [GDC Festival of Gaming 2022-02 25:16](https://youtu.be/WGRpglQ87XE?t=1516), [Roblox Creators 2022-06 37:44](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=2264). Newest source 2022-06-11.
- **Triage new games with a four-signal scorecard** (one-creator-repeated): bubDEV's scorecard: long session time, players returning, players spending, enough click appeal. Mark each green or red a couple of days after release, aim for all green, and use it to judge viral potential. It is a triage tool, not a final verdict. Sources: [bubDEV 2025-09 0:37](https://youtu.be/kg_h09_x7no?t=37), [bubDEV 2025-09 1:36](https://youtu.be/kg_h09_x7no?t=96), [bubDEV 2026-01 4:21](https://youtu.be/2lPsxPtj2mM?t=261). Newest source 2026-01-28.
- **Judge a game by income per CCU, not concurrency alone** (single-mention): A 100-CCU game with poor income is a weak purchase. When evaluating a game or a monetisation change, compare income per concurrent user against spend. janisjanis's Shorts-promoted game spent 369,000 Robux and earned 64,506 (net -304,494). Sources: [janisjanis01 2026-07 2:45](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=165), [janisjanis01 2026-07 12:10](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=730). Newest source 2026-07-05.
- **Set self-referenced, genre-realistic targets** (multiple-creators): Judge success against your own history, not front-page games. Cited targets: 50-200 concurrent for a new game (rasdev), 300-500 for a niche game (RoBuilder), 500 visits/day with 250 active (tef). Stage goals: Duckable's 10,000 CCU goal ended at 569. Sources: [rasdev 2026-06 1:13](https://youtu.be/U9RGcuxt9m4?t=73), [rasdev 2026-06 1:13](https://youtu.be/U9RGcuxt9m4?t=73), [RoBuilder 2025-06 1:43:15](https://youtu.be/krMTcTvaj_8?t=6195), [RoBuilder 2025-06 1:43:15](https://youtu.be/krMTcTvaj_8?t=6195), [tef 2026-10 14:28](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=868). Newest source 2026-10-23.
- **Fix the game before buying more ads** (multiple-creators): Check the overview benchmark stats first. Yann: if all six are above the 50th percentile on ads you are in good shape; if they are low, fix the game. janisjanis: low algorithm stats mean the game will not be pushed further regardless of budget. Sources: [Yann 2026-05 6:48](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=408), [janisjanis01 2026-04 4:58](https://youtu.be/xqHXDYbuF2w?t=298), [janisjanis01 2025-07 12:14](https://youtu.be/Sgs36l6K5EU?t=734). Newest source 2026-05-30.
- **Let ad campaigns clear the learning phase** (multiple-creators): Day 1 of ads is a learning phase: do not change creatives or stop ads early. Watch error logs, funnel drop-offs and live players instead. Ad data takes up to 48 hours and plays can be credited up to 30 days later, so the first afternoon tells you almost nothing. Sources: [Yann 2026-05 4:46](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=286), [rasdev 2026-10 11:14](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=674), [rasdev 2026-10 11:14](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=674), [rasdev 2026-10 11:14](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=674). Newest source 2026-10-11.
- **Track ad ROI as players and Robux against spend** (one-creator-repeated): Compare visits, active players and Robux earned against ad spend. janisjanis's game (about 1 active player, about 1,000 lifetime Robux) did not cover its ad spend. Use the Traffic Sources and monetization tabs to attribute players and Robux to a promotion. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-07 12:14](https://youtu.be/Sgs36l6K5EU?t=734), [janisjanis01 2025-06 12:57](https://youtu.be/5LMGVfslAYs?t=777), [janisjanis01 2025-07 12:14](https://youtu.be/Sgs36l6K5EU?t=734), [janisjanis01 2026-07 12:10](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=730). Newest source 2026-07-05.
- **Use time spent per active player as the engagement signal** (one-creator-repeated): Roblox's guidance: minutes per active player is your best proxy for love, and time doesn't lie. Pair it with daily actives and return rate. Purchases can mislead because players may regret buys they didn't value. Kill features that move neither retention nor engagement. (2018 guidance.) Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 11:30](https://youtu.be/EDY-8V943IE?t=690), [Roblox 2018-07 12:10](https://youtu.be/EDY-8V943IE?t=730), [Roblox 2018-07 12:10](https://youtu.be/EDY-8V943IE?t=730). Newest source 2018-07-24.
- **Catch server memory leaks by server age** (one-creator-repeated): In Performance > Server > Server memory by age, a script segment that climbs steadily and never drops suggests a leak. Take Lua heap snapshots only in Studio playtests (live snapshots lag players). Focus on items that keep rising while returning to baseline. Server leaks matter more than client leaks because servers live longer. Sources: [Crusherfire 2025-10 2:14](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=134), [Crusherfire 2025-10 4:03](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=243), [Crusherfire 2025-10 9:06](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=546), [Crusherfire 2025-10 2:55](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=175). Newest source 2025-10-06.
- **Query the Benchmarks API with its exact parameters** (one-creator-repeated): Compare metrics against genre, similarity or top-experience groups. Gotchas: Benchmarks endTime is inclusive, Analytics endTime is exclusive (add one day); dates are RFC 3339 midnight UTC; range at most 400 days; at most 10 percentiles; request BENCHMARK_TYPE_SIMILARITY explicitly when needed; read availableTypes when a series is empty; omit benchmarkVariantId for metrics without l7_average; retry 429s at a lower rate. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Export performance and thumbnail data before it expires** (one-creator-repeated): Client/server performance and stability data is kept 28 days; thumbnail metrics 30 days; dashboard charts show only the past 30 days (earlier custom ranges return Request Failed). Engagement, retention and monetisation metrics are kept about 1,468 days. Export or record older data in time. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Alert on specific stability and quota metrics** (one-creator-repeated): Alert on ClientCrashRate15m and OomUnexpectedExits, split by platform and memory group. Alert on DataStore quota per DataStoreRequestSource to catch runaway save loops; act at the yellow 70%-of-quota warning within an hour. Use OneMinute granularity for incidents and daily for trends. Email at most once per day per alert. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alert/metrics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alert/metrics). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Check network stats before optimising data** (one-creator-repeated): Check Developer Console network stats first. Consider buffers only if incoming data consistently exceeds about 50 KB/s. Confirm savings with the Packet Profiler by measuring packet sizes before and after serialization. Sources: [Crusherfire 2025-04 0:40](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=40), [Crusherfire 2025-04 15:47](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=947), [Crusherfire 2025-04 0:40](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=40). Newest source 2025-04-19.
- **Check earnings trackers for competitor revenue before choosing a concept** (one-creator-repeated): rasdev uses a game-earnings tracker to see how much competing games make before picking an idea. Treat the figures as directional: his estimates (Steal an Egg about $3.3M/month at 1.1M players) are unofficial. Sources: [rasdev 2026-10 4:49](https://youtu.be/Weui21kVEIY?t=289), [rasdev 2026-10 2:02](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=122), [rasdev 2026-10 1:10](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=70), [rasdev 2026-10 1:10](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=70), [rasdev 2026-10 1:10](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=70). Newest source 2026-10-20.
- **Log funnel steps server-side at real progress points** (one-creator-repeated): Send AnalyticsService:LogOnboardingFunnelStepEvent from the server at the moment progress happens (e.g. just before teleport logic), so drop-offs reflect real progression. Map linear games as ordered steps: join, lobby, elevator, teleport, each checkpoint. Confirm events arrive in View Events first; charts can take ~24 hours. Sources: [GnomeCode 2025-03 0:40](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=40), [GnomeCode 2025-03 2:42](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=162), [GnomeCode 2025-03 4:23](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=263), [GnomeCode 2025-03 6:17](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=377). Newest source 2025-03-08.
- **Fix the biggest red drop in funnel steps first** (one-creator-repeated): A large red drop in step completion points to the exact spot to fix, such as a hard encounter or bug. The GnomeCode case found its biggest drop at a specific door during a chase, which is the spot to investigate before changing anything else. Sources: [GnomeCode 2025-03 6:17](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=377), [GnomeCode 2025-03 5:37](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=337), [GnomeCode 2025-03 7:00](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=420). Newest source 2025-03-08.
- **Discover funnel steps with a 90-day lookback** (single-mention): Funnel steps only appear on days when a user actually reached them. Use a 90-day lookback to discover every step, otherwise rarely-reached steps look missing. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Exclude exploiters and long-inactive players from analysis** (single-mention): Filter out exploiters and players inactive for 15–30 days before judging engagement or economy, so cheaters and churned accounts do not distort averages or retention. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057), [Roblox DevForum 2020-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057). Newest source 2020-05-10.
- **Track 18+ earnings as a separate line item** (multiple-creators): Creator Analytics lets you see 18+ US earnings separately. 18+ users can be a large share of Robux (one creator reports over 30%), and DevEx excludes 18+ users, so a demographic-blind revenue number can mislead. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-us-18-devex-rate-earn-42-more-on-spend-from-18-us-players/4607091), [VuukStudios 2026-10 0:40](https://youtu.be/0ENzqNB_EUo?t=40), [VuukStudios 2026-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/4vuXAQt0PuQ?t=0), [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-us-18-devex-rate-earn-42-more-on-spend-from-18-us-players/4607091). Newest source 2026-10-11.
- **Use stage-gated playtime targets** (multiple-creators): Judge average playtime against the stage the game is in: aim for 10+ min during ad-driven traffic and 20+ min once on the algorithm. Low playtime predicts a flop, and a 4-minute average was judged failed. Treat 25+ min as excellent. Sources: [VuukStudios 2026-08 13:25](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=805), [VuukStudios 2025-11 15:28](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=928), [Matt The Method 2025-12 1:35](https://youtu.be/MPHPumDvMIs?t=95), [VuukStudios 2026-08 13:25](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=805), [VuukStudios 2026-08 14:09](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=849). Newest source 2026-08-16.
- **Track highly engaged players against the Select threshold** (one-creator-repeated): Highly engaged means any Robux purchase anywhere on Roblox in the last 60 days, not spend in your own game. Qualifying needs 500 in 60 days; falling below 100 loses eligibility. Watch the Select Eligibility dashboard, which notifies you when you near the threshold. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ama-publishing-requirements-roblox-kids-select/4580953), [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/alternate-publishing-requirements-for-roblox-kids-and-select/4630944), [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/alternate-publishing-requirements-for-roblox-kids-and-select/4630944), [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/alternate-publishing-requirements-for-roblox-kids-and-select/4630944). Newest source 2026-05-11.
- **Read bot-removal drops as accuracy correction** (one-creator-repeated): During the bot cleanup, visits and concurrent players fall without any real engagement loss. Do not treat the drop as a retention problem. Compare against real-user trends over weeks, since fluctuations can last several weeks. Contact Roblox Support instead of trying to fix enforcement issues yourself. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/actioning-against-bot-accounts-on-roblox/4820656), [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/actioning-against-bot-accounts-on-roblox/4820656), [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/actioning-against-bot-accounts-on-roblox/4820656), [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/actioning-against-bot-accounts-on-roblox/4820656), [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/actioning-against-bot-accounts-on-roblox/4820656). Newest source 2026-08-19.
- **Plan analytics API queries around history and budget limits** (one-creator-repeated): Create a key with universe.analytics:read, add the experience under Access Permissions, and send it as x-api-key. Rate limit is 60 requests/min per key per endpoint. Ranges over two years allow only OneWeek, OneMonth or None granularity. On 429, shorten the range, coarsen granularity or reduce breakdown series. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/error-reporting), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Don't sum daily uniques into weekly or monthly totals** (single-mention): Weekly and monthly buckets count distinct users across the period, not the sum of daily values. Summing daily DAU will overstate active users. Also convert your reporting day to UTC before comparing against dashboard buckets, which are UTC-aligned. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Ignore unreliable data-point statuses** (single-mention): Projected values may change later. NotStatisticallySignificant values are unreliable, for example crash rate in small countries. Do not act on those points; wait for finalised data or aggregate to a larger segment. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use consecutiveOccurrences so alerts fire only on persistent spikes** (one-creator-repeated): Require a condition to persist: OneMinute interval allows 6–30 occurrences, HalfHour/OneHour/OneDay allow 1–10. Keep to 20 alerts per experience; delete unused ones when MAX_ALERT_REACHED appears. Only one breakdown dimension per alert, and that dimension must also be in filter. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Compare each day with the same weekday last week** (single-mention): Use PeriodOverPeriod with periodOffsetMultiplier 7 on the OneDay interval so each day is compared to the same day last week. This removes weekday seasonality from alert and trend reads. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Group errors by MessageHash before pulling logs** (one-creator-repeated): Query ErrorCount broken down by MessageHash to get error message hashes, then call log-details. Log-details default to a 7-day window before endTime. Incident history covers a 30-day window because incidents are retained about 35 days. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/error-reporting), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/error-reporting), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Watch Scribe health states in production** (single-mention): Use Scribe.GetStatus and OnIssue to watch health state (Healthy, Degraded, Outage) and error logs live, so you see platform-side or service degradation before player reports arrive. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905). Newest source 2026-07-15.
- **Link performance changes to business metrics per update** (one-creator-repeated): Use the Performance Dashboard client and server tabs over date ranges to see how each update changed performance. Compare it with other dashboards so frame or server changes can be linked to session time and retention, not judged alone. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/monitor), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/monitor). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Judge frame time against 15–20 ms, not the 33 ms ceiling** (one-creator-repeated): Use Developer Stats targets: CPU and GPU frame time ideally 15–20 ms, with 33 ms as the ceiling. Realistic memory is 400–600 MB, not the 200 MB official figure. Check Shift+F2 for Heartbeat/Physics (higher is better), network send/receive, and draw calls (lower is better). Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2021-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074), [Roblox DevForum 2021-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074), [Roblox DevForum 2021-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074), [Roblox DevForum 2021-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074), [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258). Newest source 2026-05-10.
- **Stop ads once organic Home Recommendations appear** (one-creator-repeated): Check Statistics > Analytics > Acquisition for a Home Recommendations bar. Once it appears, turn ads off and let organic recommendations run. After first recs, ship one targeted update (a retention fix or better early tutorial) and check whether recs grow. Sources: [VuukStudios 2026-06 18:12](https://youtu.be/catPKgFIZbQ?t=1092), [VuukStudios 2026-08 14:09](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=849), [VuukStudios 2026-08 8:52](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=532). Newest source 2026-08-26.
- **Set a kill criterion for ad campaigns** (one-creator-repeated): If no home recommendations appear after the ad run, cut the game and pivot rather than buying more ads. Do not buy the audience fee if home recs have not appeared by about day 5–8. Expect 1–2 weeks to reach 500 engaged players, since the counter updates about every 2 days. Sources: [VuukStudios 2026-08 7:30](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=450), [VuukStudios 2026-07 7:05](https://youtu.be/Oo3sCq-TQXs?t=425), [VuukStudios 2026-07 7:05](https://youtu.be/Oo3sCq-TQXs?t=425). Newest source 2026-08-26.
- **Check pricing against the demographic before blaming the game** (single-mention): When conversion is poor, first check whether pricing matches the demographic you actually attract, before concluding the game is at fault. Confirm with the age and geo breakdown in Demographics. Sources: [VuukStudios 2026-08 8:04](https://youtu.be/aw0PmBkakb4?t=484). Newest source 2026-08-06.
- **Gate beta exits on age mix, funnel and like ratio** (one-creator-repeated): During a beta, track tutorial completion, drop-off points, D1/D7 retention, session length and age demographics. Check that the actual age mix matches the intended target, since a mismatch signals a design or marketing problem. Aim for at least an 80% like ratio after closing. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Preview matchmaking changes on mock data** (multiple-creators): Track matchmaking analytics per configuration to find optimisation opportunities. Preview weight changes against mock data in Custom Matchmaking before publishing to confirm matching behaves as intended. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking), [Roblox DevForum 2025-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/custom-matchmaking-is-now-available-to-all-experiences-on-roblox/4051419). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Check Video Service dashboard after changing videos** (single-mention): Creator Dashboard > Monitoring compares efficient versus inefficient video usage. Check it after adding or changing videos, since inefficient video use can cost performance without an obvious symptom. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/video-service). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use Observability dashboards for endpoints and data stores** (single-mention): Track request count and response time per request type and status to diagnose failing endpoints. For Data Stores, monitor requests, response status, throughput and storage, and set custom alerts for sustained failures or unexpected growth. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/http-service), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/best-practices). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Judge ads by play-through rate and CPP, not clicks** (multiple-creators): Track CTR (clicks/impressions), CPC, conversion (attributed purchases/impressions) and CPP (Robux spent / attributed purchases) for sponsored items. Read clicks against actual plays, since they can diverge, and use attribution of 1-day view and 28-day click-through. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items), [Raul 2026-08 4:20](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=260), [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597), [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597), [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Judge ratings on recent data after a fix** (multiple-creators): Old dislikes from an early buggy version can permanently depress a game's rating even after it is fixed. Judge the like ratio over a recent window after the fix, not lifetime totals. Poke saw a 29% like rate (7 likes vs 17 dislikes) early and treated it as a signal to fix before scaling spend. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2020-02 46:18](https://youtu.be/_LdpUJ41cso?t=2778), [Poke 2025-12 9:42](https://youtu.be/VSVCfNHK2IE?t=582), [Poke 2025-12 9:42](https://youtu.be/VSVCfNHK2IE?t=582). Newest source 2025-12-12.
- **Convert gross Robux to net before computing ad ROI** (multiple-creators): Gross Robux overstates creator earnings. Mulch multiplies gross Robux by 0.7 before comparing to ad spend (low-advice; verify the current DevEx rate). BubuTheDev's ~2x return excluded conversion fees, so treat it as an upper bound. Compare Robux spent to Robux earned on the same basis. Sources: [Mulch 2023-04 8:17](https://youtu.be/6perjdMg3T4?t=497), [BubuTheDev 2024-12 3:17](https://youtu.be/82HrjFTdPkU?t=197), [BubuTheDev 2024-12 3:17](https://youtu.be/82HrjFTdPkU?t=197), [PolarBear 2024-03 3:44](https://youtu.be/bwMUqu-wWpw?t=224). Newest source 2024-12-03.
- **Export sales CSVs before history rolls off** (multiple-creators): Dashboard history is limited. Mulch reported about two months of developer product history (2023), so export CSV sales and sum them in a spreadsheet for older periods. Last Level Studios noted the Roblox sales summary had no revenue history beyond one year (2020 statement). Check current limits. Sources: [Mulch 2023-04 5:30](https://youtu.be/6perjdMg3T4?t=330), [Last Level Studios 2020-02 49:46](https://youtu.be/_LdpUJ41cso?t=2986), [PolarBear 2024-02 0:41](https://youtu.be/SNauF9OES7E?t=41). Newest source 2024-02-08.
- **Exclude your own test purchases from sales data** (single-mention): Test purchases inflate developer product totals. ThePurpleGuy removed two self-tests from his totals. Filter them out before judging whether a product or ad test paid back. Sources: [ThePurpleGuy 2026-05 1:22](https://youtu.be/JXg2ACdk_wM?t=82). Newest source 2026-05-13.
- **Read ad dashboards as 7-day moving averages over complete windows** (one-creator-repeated): Day-one snapshots of paid traffic were poor and improved by day two. Use 7-day moving averages and wait for full windows: day-7 retention is meaningless before a week has passed and shows 'not yet available' until then. Paid players are cold, so engagement and day-1 retention dip during ads and should be judged over a longer window. Sources: [LovJak 2026-07 4:48](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=288), [LovJak 2026-07 4:46](https://youtu.be/Y602HH_KdOc?t=286), [LovJak 2026-07 4:46](https://youtu.be/Y602HH_KdOc?t=286), [LovJak 2026-05 8:14](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=494), [LovJak 2026-08 6:49](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=409). Newest source 2026-08-07.
- **Log economy transaction type and item SKU when currency changes** (single-mention): When changing currency, pass an economy analytics transaction type and item SKU so flows are logged automatically. This makes sources and sinks analysable by item rather than as aggregate balance changes. Sources: [Possible Panda 2026-09 5:58](https://youtu.be/KVmPyoOPmRI?t=358). Newest source 2026-09-20.
- **Log onboarding funnel steps with stable names via AnalyticsService** (one-creator-repeated): Call AnalyticsService:LogOnboardingFunnelStepEvent(player, stepNumber, stepName) with identical step names across calls so the funnel graph lines up. Log step 1 on Players.PlayerAdded to measure joins versus later steps. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-09 1:22](https://youtu.be/Nqcba6pLfXY?t=82), [RoyalDev 2026-09 1:22](https://youtu.be/Nqcba6pLfXY?t=82). Newest source 2026-09-24.
- **Know the funnel counting rules before reading drop-off** (one-creator-repeated): Each step logs only once per player, so repeat touches do not inflate counts. If a player skips a step, the funnel assumes earlier steps were completed. Drop-off is recorded only when a later step never fires, so a skipped step is not counted as a drop. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-09 2:44](https://youtu.be/Nqcba6pLfXY?t=164), [RoyalDev 2026-09 3:28](https://youtu.be/Nqcba6pLfXY?t=208). Newest source 2026-09-24.
- **Compare abuse-report levels to the 90th percentile of games** (one-creator-repeated): The new safety analytics on the Analytics page break down report-abuse categories (exploiting, profanity, bullying) and compare your game to the 90th percentile of games. Use this as a relative benchmark for moderation health, not an absolute threshold. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2025-11 20:22](https://youtu.be/KNHngRRMPVI?t=1222), [Last Level Studios 2025-11 20:22](https://youtu.be/KNHngRRMPVI?t=1222), [Last Level Studios 2025-11 23:08](https://youtu.be/KNHngRRMPVI?t=1388). Newest source 2025-11-05.
- **Split new versus returning players after ad bursts** (multiple-creators): After a paid burst, check average session time, DAU and returning versus new players. JustVeryYellow saw a spike fade about two days after ads ended. Visits versus unique players also reveal repeat behaviour (LovJak: 2K visits from 1.5K unique players). Sources: [JustVeryYellow 2025-11 8:11](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=491), [LovJak 2026-08 8:55](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=535). Newest source 2026-08-07.
- **Prioritise and roll back features using usage and retention data** (multiple-creators): Direct update effort to features with the strongest usage data, since neglected systems fall behind. Remove or roll back an update that worsens retention and engagement after release, as LovJak did with an ASMR update. Sources: [CORSO 2024-09 13:22](https://youtu.be/lFD1wKdwSUs?t=802), [LovJak 2026-09 4:48](https://youtu.be/VHdyHvHOId8?t=288). Newest source 2026-09-12.
- **Pin graphics quality on every performance test device** (single-mention): Set test devices to manual graphics level 10 so Roblox does not auto-disable lighting effects. Otherwise stress tests are not comparable across runs or machines. Sources: [TechSpectrum 2021-01 0:01](https://youtu.be/-PPg4N5sKXE?t=1). Newest source 2021-01-23.
- **Subtract the platform trend before crediting an update** (multiple-creators): Platform DAU was 123M in Q2 2026 (+10% YoY) and 138M in Q3 2026 (-9% YoY, +12% QoQ), with Q3 hours about 34B (-15% YoY). Compare your DAU and hours growth with the platform's before attributing a change to your update. Q3 2025 was inflated by 2025 hits, so YoY comparisons can mislead. Sources: [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm), [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-07](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026051059/ex991-robloxq22026earnin.htm), [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript), [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript). Newest source 2026-09-30.
- **Segment growth by region and age, not just the platform total** (multiple-creators): Growth is concentrated outside the US. Japan and India DAU grew 67% and 64% YoY in Q2 2026 while US/Canada grew 6%. APAC DAU grew 76-95% YoY by quarter. US 18+ is nearly one third of age-checked DAU. Slice your own DAU by country and age bracket before drawing conclusions. Sources: [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-07](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026051059/ex991-robloxq22026earnin.htm), [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript), [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00](https://rowatcher.com/news/is-roblox-still-growing-in-2026-a-data-driven-look-at-the-numbers), [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00](https://rowatcher.com/news/is-roblox-still-growing-in-2026-a-data-driven-look-at-the-numbers), [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript). Newest source 2026-07-30.
- **Measure monetization per engaged hour by age cohort** (multiple-creators): Q2 2026 monetization per hour came in below forecast, especially for under-13 players. Users 18+ reportedly monetize about 40% higher than younger players. Compare bookings or revenue per engaged hour across age brackets, not only total revenue or DAU. Sources: [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript), [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00](https://rowatcher.com/news/is-roblox-still-growing-in-2026-a-data-driven-look-at-the-numbers), [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript). Newest source 2026-07-30.
- **Pull live CCU and visits from the public API as timestamped snapshots** (one-creator-repeated): The Roblox public API returns players now, lifetime visits, favorites and votes up/down at the moment of the call. Store each snapshot with its timestamp. Derive daily visits by differencing lifetime visits between two snapshots (derived method). Do not compare snapshots taken at different hours without noting the time of day. Sources: [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95168737547508/), [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95168737547508/), [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95082159892680/), [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/114697347887839/), [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/114697347887839/). Newest source 2026-11-01.
- **Prefer official figures over tracker blogs and label sources** (multiple-creators): Tracker and press CCU figures diverge: Steal a Brainrot peak is reported as 25.4M and 25.8M for the same period, and platform peak as 47.3M and 47.4M. Use the public API, Creator Analytics and IR for benchmarks. Use tracker data only as a labelled, approximate estimate. Sources: [PocketGamer.biz 2025-10](https://www.pocketgamer.biz/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-becomes-first-game-to-surpass-25m-concurrent-players), [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-02](https://rowatcher.com/news/is-the-brainrot-trend-dying-what-ccu-data-from-three-top-games-reveals), [PocketGamer.biz 2025-10](https://www.pocketgamer.biz/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-becomes-first-game-to-surpass-25m-concurrent-players), [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00](https://rowatcher.com/news/is-roblox-still-growing-in-2026-a-data-driven-look-at-the-numbers), [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95168737547508/). Newest source 2026-11-01.
- **Discount AFK or auto-mode time when reading session length** (multiple-creators): One tracker reports a 77.25-minute average session attributed to AFK auto-fishing. Another reports Steal a Brainrot average playtime at about one third of Grow a Garden's. Check whether idle or auto features inflate session length before using it as an engagement KPI, and separate active from idle time. Sources: [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00](https://rowatcher.com/news/fish-it-reels-in-270k-players-inside-roblox-s-most-addictive-casual-game-of-2026), [PocketGamer.biz 2025-10](https://www.pocketgamer.biz/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-becomes-first-game-to-surpass-25m-concurrent-players). Newest source 2026-00-00.
- **Judge a CCU reading against the daily trough-to-peak range** (multiple-creators): Roblox concurrency swings 2-3x between the daily trough and the daily peak. A single snapshot can mislead. Use the 7-day average or same-hour comparisons, and record the daily min and max alongside the average. Sources: [StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) 2026-08](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-august-2026/), [RoTrends (tracker) 2026-10](https://www.rotrends.com/game/6739698191/BUTCHER-x-Violence-District). Newest source 2026-10-00.
- **Size server capacity from CCU divided by 30** (single-mention): Max players per server is 30 on the game page and public API. Minimum servers needed is roughly CCU divided by 30, before fill-rate losses. Plan headroom above this figure and check server-age memory behaviour as load rises (derived calculation). Sources: [AllThingsHow (guide) 2026-05](https://allthings.how/animal-hospital-anomaly-on-roblox-how-the-night-shift-and-anomalies-work/), [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/78515283254292/). Newest source 2026-11-01.
- **Track favorites per 1,000 visits with percentile rank** (single-mention): One tracker reports favorites per 1,000 visits (7-day average) at 0.92, ranked at the 48th percentile. Use this as a quality and interest proxy alongside like ratio, and compare against a percentile band rather than an absolute average. Sources: [RoTrends (tracker) 2026-10](https://www.rotrends.com/game/6739698191/BUTCHER-x-Violence-District), [RoTrends (tracker) 2026-10](https://www.rotrends.com/game/6739698191/BUTCHER-x-Violence-District). Newest source 2026-10-00.
- **Do not conflate revenue and bookings when judging platform monetization** (single-mention): Q3 2026 revenue was $1.6B (+16% YoY) while bookings were $1.7B (-12% YoY). Bookings guidance for Q3 was -14% to -18%. Use bookings as the monetization trend line and state which metric you use. Sources: [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm), [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript). Newest source 2026-09-30.
- **Measure concentration of play across top titles** (single-mention): Top-10 games' share of total hours fell from about 30% three years ago to about 20% now. Track your share of your genre's hours or CCU, not only your rank, to judge whether discovery is widening or narrowing. Sources: [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript). Newest source 2026-07-30.
- **Automate post-update regression watch with rollback triggers** (single-mention): After each update, have an agent or alert watch CCU and crash-rate charts. Define in advance the regression threshold that triggers an automatic rollback, so the response does not depend on someone noticing a chart in time. Sources: [Roblox 2026-04 33:54](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=2034). Newest source 2026-04-16.
- **Synthesise player feedback with AI, then act on it** (single-mention): Use AI to summarise in-game player feedback at scale into themes you can act on. Use it alongside qualitative playtests (existing rule), not as a replacement, and check top themes against raw samples before changing the game. Sources: [Roblox 2026-04 40:00](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=2400). Newest source 2026-04-16.
- **Analyse multi-branch flows with branch-aware Sankey funnels** (single-mention): Journey analytics (announced for late 2026) uses Sankey diagrams with custom non-linear funnels to compare conversion across branches and locate drop-offs. Use it for flows with several paths; keep linear funnels for onboarding steps. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208). Newest source 2026-09-00.
- **Use the analytics agent for fast triage, then confirm in dashboards** (single-mention): The analytics agent (late 2026) answers plain-language questions about game metrics and recommends next steps. Use it to get a first read quickly, then confirm the numbers in the underlying dashboards before acting. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208). Newest source 2026-09-00.
- **Trace client sessions with the Observability Platform** (single-mention): The Observability Platform (late 2026) traces client sessions and logs and gives troubleshooting insights from Studio Build. It complements the existing endpoint and data-store dashboards by covering the client side of a session. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880). Newest source 2026-09-11.
- **Use the Play Test Agent to repeat core flows** (single-mention): The single-player Play Test Agent (beta) runs test cases described in plain text, replacing manual replays of the same flow. Use it to re-check funnel and onboarding paths after each change, and keep human playtests for qualitative feedback. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 16:28](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=988). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Read the abuse-report dashboard against similar games** (single-mention): The abuse report analytics dashboard (Oct 2026) shows daily takedown counts, violation reasons and a comparison with similar games. Read it daily and judge a spike by the peer comparison, combined with the existing 90th-percentile check. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 6:50](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=410). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Combine MMP attribution with platform reporting** (single-mention): Attribution is now probabilistic, so last-click is unreliable. Pair platform reporting with an MMP (AppsFlyer, Adjust, Singular) and compare both before reallocating user-acquisition spend. Self-reported by the indie creator; treat as a method to test, not a verified standard. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 42:37](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2557). Newest source 2026-10-26.
- **Track lifecycle decay of hit games** (single-mention): Read CCU and DAU as multi-week or multi-month trends, not single peaks. The host claims top 2025 games such as Grow a Garden fell over 90% in CCU or DAU by late 2026. This is unverified and low-advice, but it supports comparing your own decay curve against the genre rather than the peak. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 13:41](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=821). Newest source 2026-10-29.
- **Log income per second around each big purchase or boost** (one-creator-repeated): For idle or hatch-based games, record income per second before and after each hatch, booster, or large spend. The Mulch creator logged jumps such as 12.2B to 25.8B/s after a 50-egg spend and 504B to 1T/s after a 2x booster. Self-reported figures, but the method isolates which mechanic moved income. Sources: [Mulch 2026-09 2:39](https://youtu.be/WYODms_JMRA?t=159), [Mulch 2026-09 9:14](https://youtu.be/WYODms_JMRA?t=554), [Mulch 2026-09 9:54](https://youtu.be/WYODms_JMRA?t=594), [Mulch 2026-09 9:27](https://youtu.be/LYx9J3W5ZvU?t=567). Newest source 2026-09-20.
- **Benchmark a clone's organic CCU against the original before investing** (one-creator-repeated): A clone can flop even when the original is strong. Alex Builds Games reports his first clone (Power Arena) reached only ~40-60 organic CCU, while the original competitor reached under 5% of Ability Arena's CCU before rebrand. Check organic traction on a small test before scaling a copy concept. Sources: [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 4:46](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=286), [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 11:29](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=689), [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=0). Newest source 2026-10-09.
- **Expect extreme payer and revenue concentration in planning** (multiple-creators): Monetisation is highly concentrated. Only 0.01% of Roblox players have ever earned 1M Robux (Duckable). Deconstructor of Fun estimates ~50% of 2025 top-25 revenue came from steal-style games and ~10x monetisation for the top title versus the next one. These are estimates; set targets from your own payer distribution, not from top-title outcomes. Sources: [Duckable 2026-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/EOUSVENUkr0?t=0), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 10:15](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=615), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 59:37](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=3577). Newest source 2026-10-29.
- **Keep plays, visits and concurrency separate when quoting game scale** (one-creator-repeated): Plays, visits and unique players are different counts. Record each as its own field and never convert between them. The same creator mixed 'plays' and 'visits' across titles, so quote the exact metric named in the source. Sources: [GnomeCode 2025-02 1:18](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=78), [GnomeCode 2025-02 6:59](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=419), [GnomeCode 2025-02 10:45](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=645). Newest source 2025-02-22.
- **Treat a top-title CCU as an outlier ceiling, not a target** (single-mention): A reference game at around 1 million CCU is a rare outlier. Use it only to show what the platform's largest hits reach. Set your own targets from same-genre, self-referenced numbers instead. Sources: [JustDucksey 2025-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/s5nooZlK5Pc?t=0). Newest source 2025-07-27.
- **Do not benchmark revenue on creator earnings guesses** (single-mention): A speaker's rough earnings guess for a single game is anecdote, not data. Keep it out of benchmark tables and check revenue against official Creator Dashboard figures or tracked data. Sources: [Cumin 2026-05 7:39](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=459). Newest source 2026-05-30.
- **Use a 7-day minimum click window for influencer attribution** (one-creator-repeated): Standard UA uses a 24-hour window, which undercounts creator content that is watched for weeks or months. Use at least 7 days. Do not compare influencer results against UA on a 7-30 day window, because the comparison is unfair to creators. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 13:43](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=823), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 10:59](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=659), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 15:05](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=905), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 12:21](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=741), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 45:14](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2714). Newest source 2026-06-10.
- **Judge creator campaigns on day-365 or lifetime installs, not day 7 or 30 CPI** (one-creator-repeated): Tracking links keep converting for years. One client saw about 50% more installs at day 365 than at day 30 over two years of creator content. Plan budgets against long-tail numbers; Q1918 warns lifetime figures may come in well below planning assumptions. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 15:46](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=946), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 12:21](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=741), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 15:46](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=946), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 12:21](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=741), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 12:21](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=741). Newest source 2026-06-10.
- **Measure organic uplift with a per-market proxy model** (one-creator-repeated): Track organic installs in each market before, during and after a campaign, not just tracked installs. Attribution alone understates influencer impact and can hide a path to profitability. The lift is often much larger than attributed installs. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 15:05](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=905), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 8:13](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=493), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 10:59](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=659). Newest source 2026-06-10.
- **Expect about 30% organic uplift and vary it by campaign type** (one-creator-repeated): Typical organic uplift in test markets was about 30% for Match Masters. Giveaway campaigns attribute traffic directly, so uplift is lower (~30%). Non-giveaway posts can have up to ~100% of attributed traffic be organic. Expect variation by game, audience and country. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 10:59](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=659), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 9:36](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=576), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 8:13](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=493), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 9:36](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=576). Newest source 2026-06-10.
- **Compare verticals on CPM, view-to-install, LTV and retention, with minimum sample** (one-creator-repeated): Diagnose which metric is failing before changing creators. Do not trust a vertical comparison below 10+ videos and about 500k combined views.
