Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 39:03](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2343), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 39:03](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2343). Newest source 2026-06-01.
- **Expect delayed whale conversion from creator installs** (single-mention): In a UK creator campaign, players installed about 15 days after publish but converted months later, often after 3-4 months. Short windows will miss this payer timing. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 15:46](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=946). Newest source 2026-06-01.
- **Set the KPI before designing a live event** (single-mention): The KPI may be retention rather than revenue, and the answer changes the event design. Choose the target first, then design the event to move it. Sources: [GameMakers 2025-02 4:20](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=260). Newest source 2025-02-04.
- **Track all KPIs during a push, not only the target one** (one-creator-repeated): Pushing revenue can quietly hurt retention. Track the full KPI set and balance priorities by game stage. Sources: [GameMakers 2025-02 8:21](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=501), [GameMakers 2022-11 38:37](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=2317). Newest source 2025-02-04.
- **Judge gacha and item economies at item level** (single-mention): Track price and volume of each item, equip rate, depth of spend, and the point where gacha payers stop. Use ARPU per live event as the top-line check. Sources: [GameMakers 2024-08 52:49](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=3169). Newest source 2024-08-12.
- **Check spend buckets for payer clusters before setting prices** (single-mention): If payers cluster at fixed totals (e.g. $10, $100, $1000), align price points to those clusters rather than to arbitrary tiers. Sources: [GameMakers 2022-11 42:46](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=2566). Newest source 2022-11-24.
- **Prioritise the backlog by LTV impact divided by effort** (single-mention): Estimate LTV impact for each item, divide by effort on a 1-10 scale, and sort descending. Attack low-hanging fruit first. Sources: [GameMakers 2022-08 8:34](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=514). Newest source 2022-08-30.
- **Evaluate monthly install cohorts against the previous cohort** (single-mention): Roughly monthly cohort review, tied to split tests and releases. Look for each cohort to show higher LTV and lower CPI than the last. Sources: [GameMakers 2022-08 5:09](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=309), [GameMakers 2022-08 4:29](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=269). Newest source 2022-08-30.
- **Keep an owned raw data copy and reconcile with MMP and store dashboards** (single-mention): Set up analytics and a data lake you own before spending on user acquisition. Do not rely only on MMP or store dashboards; verify data consistency first. Sources: [GameMakers 2025-02 10:26](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=626). Newest source 2025-02-04.
- **Check difficulty changes against revenue and engagement together** (single-mention): Making early obstacles easier cut turn rate and lowered revenue in one case. Warning signs before turns lifted turn rate by about 7%. Judge each tweak on both behaviour and revenue, not on a single number. Sources: [GameMakers 2024-03 15:11](https://youtu.be/zvNQHk1hUYc?t=911), [GameMakers 2024-03 15:53](https://youtu.be/zvNQHk1hUYc?t=953). Newest source 2024-03-08.
- **Expect mixed early metrics while the algorithm finds an audience** (single-mention): Monitor six core stats. A mixed picture early is normal while the algorithm finds the right audience. Do not react to a single weak reading. Sources: [janisjanis01 2026-08 6:47](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=407). Newest source 2026-08-02.
- **Check analytics weekly and improve gradually over a 60-day plan** (single-mention): Review analytics weekly and make incremental changes across a 60-day plan rather than large single shifts. Sources: [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 5:35](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=335). Newest source 2025-12-03.
- **Model ad performance when consent limits tracking** (one-creator-repeated): With low opt-in (about 1 in 6 to 1 in 7 users in one mobile source), survey opted-in users and apply results to the wider cohort. Use one methodology across all channels so they compare fairly. Model at campaign and creative level, because blended ROAS alone cannot support granular budget decisions. Sources: [GameMakers 2024-10 15:31](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=931), [GameMakers 2024-10 21:02](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1262), [GameMakers 2024-10 15:31](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=931), [GameMakers 2024-10 22:30](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1350). Newest source 2024-10-24.
- **Optimise campaigns on a frequent event when revenue is sparse** (single-mention): When revenue events fire rarely, choose a frequently firing optimisation event (for example tutorial complete or a CPA-style action) as the campaign target. Sparse purchase events give the ad algorithm too little signal to learn from. Sources: [GameMakers 2024-10 23:51](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1431). Newest source 2024-10-24.
- **Read visits, session length and like ratio together** (one-creator-repeated): Compare visits, average session length and like ratio side by side. One indie launch had about 5,000 first-day visits and a 14.5-minute average session, but only 10 active players the next day with a poor like ratio. Decent visits plus low likes signals a quality problem to diagnose before buying more traffic. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-06 14:29](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=869), [janisjanis01 2025-06 14:29](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=869), [janisjanis01 2025-06 14:29](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=869), [janisjanis01 2025-06 14:29](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=869). Newest source 2025-06-15.
- **Use badges as drop-off markers** (single-mention): Place badges at milestones (day markers, death or fail points) to locate the exact drop-off point instead of guessing whether difficulty is the cause. The source is low-advice, so confirm with server-side funnel events before acting. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-10 15:56](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=956). Newest source 2025-10-11.
- **Log and replicate the causes of a traction spike** (single-mention): After a traction spike, record the game type, packaging, and traffic source that coincided with it. Repeat those elements in the next release or launch, and read the post-spike baseline rather than the spike itself. Sources: [rasdev 2026-05 2:02](https://youtu.be/wGRgITzP18c?t=122). Newest source 2026-05-07.

## Checklist
- [ ] Instrument funnels, economy and custom events before launch (server-side only).
- [ ] Define engagement, retention and monetization KPIs before development.
- [ ] Check Creator Hub > Overview benchmark scorecard weekly (needs 100+ DAU).
- [ ] Identify the weakest metric versus same-genre benchmarks and fix it first.
- [ ] Track D1, D7, D30 retention and map each to its likely cause.
- [ ] Track average session time, DAU, MAU and DAU/MAU.
- [ ] Track payer conversion, ARPPU and ARPDAU together.
- [ ] Build an onboarding funnel with micro-steps and slice by device and source.
- [ ] Check New User First Session Retention for onboarding issues.
- [ ] Track economy sources, sinks and average wallet balance.
- [ ] Log economy events after success, with itemSKU and positive amounts.
- [ ] Use up to 3 custom fields instead of many event names or currencies.
- [ ] Validate event data server-side against exploiters.
- [ ] Compare each release against previous releases (DAU, conversion, playtime, retention).
- [ ] Check error report, crash rate and Performance by Place Version after every publish.
- [ ] Set alerts: baseline 7-14 days, thresholds above normal, duration 5, one alert with Breakdown.
- [ ] Use 28-day overlays and 7-day moving averages; check seasonality.
- [ ] Break down retention by acquisition source before blaming the game.
- [ ] Write a hypothesis before each change and before each experiment.
- [ ] Check MDE and run the full 14+ days; act only on significant results.
- [ ] Review Ads Manager at day 30 and Creator Analytics for retention impact.
- [ ] Track rewarded video opt-in (15%+) and frequency (3+).
- [ ] Use My Transactions for true income and exclude your own purchases.
- [ ] Compare event windows: week before, during and after.
- [ ] Document experiments and learnings; revisit dashboards periodically.
- [ ] After any update, log the peak CCU and the settled CCU a week later; judge the change on the settled number (T6861, T6466).
- [ ] Read FPS, memory and session time as P10/P50/P90 by platform and device category, not averages (T7336, T7343, T9698).
- [ ] Set alerts on ClientCrashRate15m and OomUnexpectedExits by platform and memory group (T7335, T9040).
- [ ] Set a DataStore quota alert per DataStoreRequestSource and watch the Memory Store yellow warning at 70% of quota (T7337, T7518, T8527).
- [ ] Review Creations > Monitoring > Data Stores for request counts and Request Count by Status to find failing API methods (T8597, T8598, T7521).
- [ ] Check the Performance Dashboard after each publish for FPS, memory and crash rate by device category (T8442, T9698).
- [ ] Use the Developer Console Network tab to inspect DataStore and HttpService status codes, timing and failures (T7803).
- [ ] Export profiling data as JSON to compare sessions outside Studio (T8857).
- [ ] Segment engagement by IsNewUser so new-player behaviour is not diluted by returning players (T9196).
- [ ] Segment retention by Platform, Country, AcquisitionSource and payer status, not just blended D1/D7/D30 (T9191).
- [ ] Attribute promo traffic via the Traffic Sources and monetization tabs (T6241).
- [ ] Keep ad budget alerts at 80% and 100% in view; lowering budget below month-to-date usage throttles the service (T8150).
- [ ] Compare periods with the Analytics tab date range (previous day to last 90 days) (T8106, N3338).
- [ ] Ignore the last 3 minutes of chart data, which may be incomplete (T8600, N3496).
- [ ] Set an analytics_data category per notification type to compare notification performance (T10039).
- [ ] Check the Anti-cheat page estimates by trust level before enforcing a policy (T8622).
- [ ] Track CTR, conversion rate and cost per play for each ad (T5859, N2782, N2783).
- [ ] Check the six overview stats against the 50th percentile before adding ad spend (T6536).
- [ ] Set benchmarks by region before judging retention or ARPPU for a player base (T6663).
- [ ] Run the four-signal scorecard a few days after release, then decide after 1-2 weeks (T6319, N2833).
- [ ] Export performance data within 28 days and thumbnail data within 30 days (T9192, T9193).
- [ ] Use Benchmarks endTime one day later in Analytics than in Benchmarks (T7538).
- [ ] Check network stats in the Developer Console; add buffers only above about 50 KB/s (T6159, N2548).
- [ ] Take heap snapshots only in Studio playtests (T6325).
- [ ] Check Learn & Explore eligibility: like ratio above 50% and at least 15 CCU (N3123, N3124).
- [ ] Log funnel steps server-side at the moment progress happens, and confirm arrival in View Events before judging (charts take about 24 hours).
- [ ] Map linear games as ordered funnel steps: join, lobby, elevator, teleport, each checkpoint.
- [ ] Filter out exploiters and players inactive 15–30 days before analysing retention, playtime or revenue.
- [ ] Keep timestamps on every exported dataset so periods can be compared.
- [ ] Use analytics only as far as budget allows; paid analytics services can be costly.
- [ ] Track average playtime per stage: 10+ min during ads, 20+ once on the algorithm.
- [ ] Track D1 and D7 retention together with playtime; D7 is weighted in Home Recommendations.
- [ ] Track highly engaged players (500 in 60 days to qualify; below 100 loses eligibility).
- [ ] Track 18+ earnings as a separate line and watch its share of Robux.
- [ ] During a beta, check the age mix against the target and aim for at least an 80% like ratio after closing.
- [ ] Set alerts with consecutiveOccurrences to avoid firing on one-off spikes.
- [ ] Use PeriodOverPeriod with a 7-day offset to compare each day with the same weekday last week.
- [ ] Keep alerts at or below 20 per experience; delete unused ones before creating new.
- [ ] Group errors by MessageHash before pulling log details.
- [ ] Check the Performance Dashboard after each update and link it to session time.
- [ ] Export performance data within its 28-day history window before it expires.
- [ ] Check the Video Service dashboard after adding or changing videos.
- [ ] Scope the Analytics API key to universe.analytics:read and send it as x-api-key.
- [ ] Stay within 60 requests/min per key per endpoint; on 429, shorten the range or coarsen granularity.
- [ ] Use granularity None with a limit to rank top-N series, then query those values for display.
- [ ] Convert reporting days to UTC before comparing with dashboard buckets.
- [ ] Do not sum daily uniques into weekly or monthly totals; buckets are distinct-user counts.
- [ ] Check Statistics > Analytics > Acquisition for Home Recommendations after an ad run; if present, stop ads.
- [ ] If conversion is poor, check price against the demographic actually attracted before blaming the game.
- [ ] Preview matchmaking weight changes on mock data before publishing.
- [ ] Before scaling ad spend, check day-1 retention, session time and percent still playing at 5 minutes.
- [ ] Check the like ratio over a recent post-fix window, not lifetime totals.
- [ ] Export sales CSVs monthly, since dashboard history is limited.
- [ ] Remove your own test purchases from developer product totals.
- [ ] Convert gross Robux to net (e.g. ×0.7 or current DevEx rate) before comparing to ad spend.
- [ ] Use 7-day moving averages on ad dashboards and wait for full retention windows (D7 needs 7 days).
- [ ] Call AnalyticsService:LogOnboardingFunnelStepEvent with the same stepName strings in every call.
- [ ] Log funnel step 1 on Players.PlayerAdded.
- [ ] Verify funnel events fire by checking the Output window during testing.
- [ ] Open Creator Hub Analytics > Funnels to see which step players drop at.
- [ ] Pass economy transaction type and item SKU whenever currency changes.
- [ ] Review safety analytics report-abuse breakdowns against the 90th percentile.
- [ ] After an ad burst, compare new versus returning players and average session time.
- [ ] Track visits versus unique players to gauge repeat play.
- [ ] Track ARRPPU and PTR alongside ARPPU when reviewing monetisation and discovery.
- [ ] Track percent still playing at 5 minutes as an early first-session gate.
- [ ] Watch the like ratio early as a warning before scaling spend.
- [ ] Roll back an update if retention and engagement worsen after release, then re-test.
- [ ] Prioritise the next feature by usage data rather than intuition.
- [ ] Pin graphics level to 10 on all test devices for comparable performance runs.
- [ ] Ask a new player to join your game and watch their first minutes alongside the stats.
- [ ] Open the Creator Analytics dashboard and compare per-game signals against the published signal list and relative weights to see where the game is strong or weak.
- [ ] Store a timestamped public API snapshot (players now, lifetime visits, favorites, votes) every reporting day.
- [ ] Compare your DAU and hours growth with platform DAU and hours for the same quarter before crediting an update.
- [ ] Break DAU growth down by country and region, especially US/Canada vs Japan/India and APAC.
- [ ] Compute monetization per engaged hour for each age bracket (under-13 vs 18+).
- [ ] Check whether session length includes AFK or auto-mode time before reporting it.
- [ ] Log daily min and max CCU alongside the average, not a single snapshot.
- [ ] Compute minimum server count as CCU divided by 30 and check headroom.
- [ ] Track favorites per 1,000 visits and like ratio weekly, and compare against a percentile band.
- [ ] Estimate DAU/MAU for your game and compare with platform DAU/MAU (about 38%, derived from Q4 2025 figures).
- [ ] Label every tracker or press figure with its source and date before using it in a report.
- [ ] Compare quarter-on-quarter and year-on-year figures with awareness of 2025 hit-driven base effects.
- [ ] After every update, set up a CCU and crash-rate watch and write the rollback threshold down before release (T17610).
- [ ] Each cycle, generate an AI summary of in-game player feedback, then check the top themes against raw comments (T17612).
- [ ] Check the abuse-report analytics dashboard daily for takedown counts and violation reasons, and compare with similar games (T17729).
- [ ] If you have Play Test Agent beta access, write test cases for core flows and run them before each release instead of replaying flows by hand (T17745).
- [ ] When Journey analytics is available, rebuild multi-path funnels as branch-aware Sankey views to find drop-offs per branch (T17631).
- [ ] When the analytics agent is available, ask it a plain-language question about the last update and check the answer in the raw dashboards (T17632).
- [ ] When the Observability Platform is available, enable client session tracing for the release build and check logs for errors after launch (T17661).
- [ ] Compare platform-reported acquisition with MMP data (AppsFlyer, Adjust, or Singular) before moving UA budget.
- [ ] Plot CCU and DAU weekly or monthly for at least 8 weeks before judging a game's trajectory.
- [ ] For idle or hatch-based games, log income per second before and after each hatch, booster, or large spend.
- [ ] Before launching a clone, test organic CCU on a small audience and compare against the original's CCU.
- [ ] Check the original's CCU trajectory, not only its peak, before choosing a competitor to copy.
- [ ] Label every figure as official, third-party tracker, or creator self-reported when you cite it.
- [ ] Verify third-party estimates (Newzoo, Sensor Tower, creator-exchange) before quoting them.
- [ ] Record plays, visits and CCU as separate fields when noting a game's scale (N7791, N7792, N7794).
- [ ] Tag every creator-reported figure as self-reported in the knowledge file (N7791–N7794, N7799, N7811, N7840, N7841).
- [ ] Use the 10–30 minute quit window only as a rough sanity check against your own session-length median, after discounting AFK time (N7840).
- [ ] Keep speaker earnings guesses out of benchmark tables (N7841).
- [ ] Set a minimum 7-day click window on all creator and influencer tracking links.
- [ ] Report creator installs at day 30, day 90, day 365 and lifetime, not only day 7 or 30.
- [ ] Log organic installs per market for the weeks before, during and after each campaign to build a proxy uplift model.
- [ ] Tag every creator post as giveaway or non-giveaway before reading uplift.
- [ ] Require 10+ videos and about 500k combined views per vertical before comparing verticals.
- [ ] Compare verticals on CPM, view-to-install, LTV and retention, and name the failing metric before changing creators.
- [ ] Write the target KPI down before designing any live event.
- [ ] Track KPIs beyond the target during any revenue push, including retention.
- [ ] Build a payer histogram by spend bucket and compare it to current price points.
- [ ] Track price, volume, equip rate and spend depth for each gacha or item SKU.
- [ ] Score the feature backlog as estimated LTV impact divided by effort (1-10) and sort descending.
- [ ] Keep a raw event copy you own and reconcile it against MMP and store dashboards.
- [ ] Review analytics weekly across a 60-day improvement plan.
- [ ] Log the game type, packaging and traffic source that coincided with any traction spike, so the cause can be repeated.
- [ ] Track visits, average session length (minutes) and like ratio side by side each day after launch.
- [ ] Investigate when visits look healthy but the like ratio is low, before spending on ads.
- [ ] Place badges at milestone and fail points to find where players drop off, then confirm with funnel events.
- [ ] When revenue events are sparse, set the ad optimisation event to a frequent action such as tutorial complete or CPA.
- [ ] Where tracking is consent-limited, survey opted-in users and apply results to the cohort using one method across all channels.
- [ ] Model ROAS at campaign and creative level rather than relying on blended ROAS.
- [ ] Re-measure consent and opt-in rates each quarter instead of reusing old figures.

## Where creators agree
- Retention (D1/D7/D30) is the most important health metric (Roblox Creators, DevForum posts, Tizzy, 1_Bruce, CallMeDogey, AlvinBlox).
- Compare to same-genre benchmarks; benchmarks are for comparison and not used in ranking (Roblox Creators, DevForum, 1_Bruce).
- Only trust statistically significant, full-duration experiment results (Roblox Creators, DevForum, Roblox Creator Docs).
- Low D1 means onboarding; use funnels to find drop-off (Roblox, DevForum, Tizzy, SmartyRBX).
- Slice by platform/device/source to find causes (Roblox, DevForum, Tizzy).
- Economy sources and sinks must be tracked and balanced (Roblox, Creator Docs).
- Conversion rate with ARPPU is the core monetization pair (Roblox Creators, DevForum, Creator Docs).
- Tizzy, MoseyTube and Roblox all say to wait 24h-3 days before judging changes or ads.
- Judge changes against a settled baseline after waiting; peaks from ads or updates fade (rasdev, janisjanis, Roblox docs).
- Use data as a guide: don't ignore it, don't obsess over it (rasdev T6649; janisjanis T6502 on studying stats instead of guessing).
- Time spent or engagement is a stronger signal than purchases alone (Roblox 2018, bubDEV scorecard).
- Read performance through percentiles and per-device breakdowns, not averages (Roblox docs).
- Ad quality (CTR, cost per play, active players) matters more than volume; fix the game when its own stats are weak (Yann, janisjanis, Roblox docs).
- Playtime and early retention are the leading signals for whether a game will gain traffic (VuukStudios, Matt The Method, PineappleDev).
- Analytics data lags: funnels take about 24 hours, benchmarks and acquisition data lag a day to several days after launch (GnomeCode, Raul, official docs).
- Judge trends against real-user baselines over time, not single-day snapshots, especially after platform-side changes.
- Paid visits do not guarantee retention; fix the game before buying more ads (VuukStudios, Erthlord and Raul cases).
- Read day-1 retention and session time before scaling ad spend (RoyalDev, LovJak, Poke).
- Judge ratings on recent data rather than lifetime totals (Last Level Studios, Poke).
- Record or export sales data before dashboard history windows close (Mulch, Last Level Studios).
- Use funnels to locate drop-offs instead of guessing why players leave (RoyalDev).
- Concurrency is hit-driven and decays sharply after launch peaks; several sources show 90-99% declines within months (N7140, N7141, N7097, N7142).
- Platform-level DAU growth is concentrated outside the US and Canada, with Japan, India and APAC leading (N7122, N7205, N7221).
- Session length must be read against game mechanics; AFK or auto modes and genre differ widely (N7081, N7085, N7180).
- Roblox's late-2026 posts (T17610, T17612, T17632) agree that agents and AI should handle routine watching and summarising, leaving developers to act on the output.
- GamesBeat (T17598) matches the existing core set: track acquisition, playtime, retention and monetisation after publishing.
- Hit-game CCU peaks can mislead, so trends over time matter (T17979 aligns with existing peak and baseline rules).
- Track several KPIs together rather than one target, including retention alongside revenue (GameMakers T18464, T18573; Deconstructor of Fun T18663).
- Do not decide on a single data reading; verify with other signals before acting (GameMakers Q1890, T18503).

## Where creators disagree
- **How aggressively to chase benchmarks**
  - CallMeDogey: improve anything below 75th percentile; all six in green is the one true way to grow.
  - Deconstructor of Fun (Zach): aims for the 90th percentile on all six.
  - Roblox (official): push below-50th metrics above 50th for most surfacing gain; don't min-max the six metrics.
  - 1_Bruce: stats don't guarantee success due to discovery luck.
- **Whether A/B testing is reliable**
  - Roblox Creators/Docs: run experiments; they control for seasonality and confounds.
  - Roblox (Grow a Garden talk): at ~0.5-1M players, A/B analytics became too expensive and unreliable, so they used player feedback.
  - Roblox DevForum: games under ~500 DAU should skip experiments; docs say 1,000 DAU may struggle.
- **Ads vs fixing benchmarks first**
  - CallMeDogey: fix benchmarks below 75th first, ahead of advertising.
  - MoseyTube: run ads early, then from day 5 stop them if retention is poor.
  - Tizzy: his ad-driven plays (~45K) dwarfed home-recommendation plays (~950) in one case (self-reported).
- **Audience targeting**
  - 1_Bruce: target high-income countries and 18+ and avoid children's games for revenue (self-reported).
  - Roblox Creators: pick one clear audience; a broad 'under 18' target hit no one.
  - Roblox (2018): Under-9 segment is growing fast, so child audiences are large though they monetise less.
- **Session length target**
  - Roblox 2022: 10+ minutes for social games, but prioritise retention.
  - Roblox 2021: 15-20 min is strong, 12 decent for large games.
  - 1_Bruce: incremental games average >3 hours (self-reported), so genre matters.
- **When to judge a new game or a change**
  - bubDEV (T6319): read four signals a couple of days after release to decide viral potential.
  - rasdev (T6645, N2833): wait at least 1-2 weeks before judging a change.
  - janisjanis (T6829): judge player count stability and retention over a week.
- **Ad CTR target**
  - Yann (N2782): 2.5% or better, with niche simulators around 2.5% and towers up to 8%.
  - janisjanis (N2675): target 3%+ (first ad reached 2.26%).
- **Playtime threshold**
  - VuukStudios: 10–15 min ideal, 12+ target, 10+ during ads, 20+ on algorithm, 25+ excellent (N5019, T13702, N5375)
  - PineappleDev: ~13 min benchmark, 15+ min for good Premium payouts (N4596)
  - Matt The Method: ~22 min as key promotion signal (N5036)
- **First-session or D1 retention benchmark**
  - Seibo: 71% first-session retention judged excellent by Gemini (T13840); low-advice, AI-judged
  - VuukStudios: D1 target 12–15% (T13704)
- **Paying-user conversion scale**
  - Seibo reports conversion rate 0.32 (N5522), units unclear, possibly percent
  - VuukStudios targets 2–3% (N5378)
- **Platform daily user count**
  - Roblox Jumpstart page: 144M daily active users (N5664, low-advice)
  - Roblox Creator Hub (Sept 2026): 123M average per day last quarter (N5439)
- **Ad attribution method**
  - 2020 DevForum: 1-day view, 28-day click-through (N4209)
  - 2026 official: CTR, CPC, conversion and CPP from attributed purchases (T14837); attribution window not restated
- **How to read engagement drops during a paid campaign**
  - LovJak (T16546, Q1596): drops are normal because paid players are cold; judge over a longer window and they should improve.
  - RoyalDev (T16616): check day-one retention and session length before spending on ads; poor numbers mean the game loop must be fixed first.
- **Like-ratio threshold for action**
  - Poke (T16362): a 29% like rate early was a stop-and-fix signal.
  - Mulch (N6397): ~70–71% mid-campaign was read as acceptable despite early downvote bombing when the game was broken.
- **Steal a Brainrot peak CCU**
  - PocketGamer.biz reports 25.4M peak (October 2025) (N7078, N7127 via RoWatcher)
  - RoWatcher reports 25.8M peak (October 2025) (N7140, N7148), and StudioKrew agrees with 25.8M (N7179)
- **Platform peak concurrents**
  - PocketGamer.biz and RoWatcher list 47.4M (August 2025) (N7082, N7144)
  - RoWatcher lists 47.3M in August 2025 (N7114)
- **Roblox monthly hours in 2025**
  - StudioKrew cites analyst figures of roughly 10.25B hours per month (N7186)
  - Company quarterly figures of 35-39.6B per quarter (N7115, N7160) imply about 11.7-13.2B per month
- **Fish It! CCU versus Fisch**
  - RoWatcher earlier reported Fish It! peak at 500K+ versus Fisch at about 100K (5:1) (N7090)
  - RoWatcher later reported Fish It! peak at 2.7M (December 2025) (N7154); the 5:1 ratio is outdated
- **Tracker figures versus their own sidebar (RoWatcher)**
  - RoWatcher (tracker blog) reports a like ratio of 0.8402 (N7557), but its sidebar shows 83.1%.
  - RoWatcher reports 71,835 concurrent players (N7554), but its sidebar shows 26K online.
  - Treat RoWatcher figures as unreliable snapshots and do not use them as benchmarks without an official cross-check.
- **Cohort evaluation window**
  - GameMakers (2022-08, N7892, Q1878) reviews install cohorts roughly monthly against the previous cohort.
  - Deconstructor of Fun (2026-06, T18651, T18678) judges creator campaigns on day-365 or lifetime installs, not short windows.
- **Number of core stats to watch**
  - janisjanis01 (2026-08, T18713) monitors six core stats and expects a mixed early picture.
  - The existing knowledge file's four-signal scorecard rule for triaging new games uses four signals.

## Date context (what may be outdated)
- GameAnalytics (2017-2021, 2023) was the recommended tool; native Creator Analytics now provides funnel, economy and custom events (2024+). Older GameAnalytics advice is superseded, though a 2023 official talk still suggested it because funnels weren't yet supported.
- The 2017 note that GameAnalytics acquisition data is inaccurate is old and refers to Roblox traffic.
- Dashboard eligibility thresholds dropped over time: 600 DAU (Mar 2022) -> 500 (May 2022) -> 300 engagement (Jun 2022) -> >10 DAU/10 hours (current docs). Use current docs.
- Benchmark cadence changed: quarterly (2022-03), daily (2022-10), and the scorecard moves to 7-day averages (2025-10). Monthly Stats report retired Sept 2022.
- Default date range changed from 7 to 28 days in May 2022.
- Retention charts now use first-play-date cohorts (2025-07).
- A/B test advice moved from a manual 50/50 one-week split (2022) to the built-in experiments platform with 14-60 day runs, MDE and SRM checks (2025).
- Home recommendation signal set updated (2026-03/06): Deep play-through, 7-day qualified sessions, three windows, 21 RFY charts. Official 2025-10 says 20+ signals are weighted with none dominant.
- Playtime and CTR are reliable only above a few thousand daily visits (2024-08).
- Platform-scale numbers (MAU/DAU, mobile share) are time-stamped, 2018 to 2026, and conflict across years.
- Indie CCU, revenue, visits and ad stats are self-reported and unverified; recheck 2026 items against current docs.
- Roblox 2018 guidance (T4987, T4989, Q878) on time spent and decision razors predates the current percentile and Benchmarks tooling; use the 2026-10 Creator Docs for tooling and parameters.
- Creator Docs entries (2026-10) are the newest official source. Retention windows (28/30 days) and dashboard limits may change; recheck before relying on them.
- Benchmark example values (N3148) are illustrative for 2026-01-01, not current norms.
- Indie CTR and cost-per-play targets span 2023 (T5859) to 2026 (N2782, N2783); units differ, so do not compare directly.
- Revenue estimates (N2948-N2951) are 2026-09/10 speaker estimates, not official.
- Learn & Explore thresholds (N3123, N3124) are as of 2026-10 and may change.
- Earlier Shorts-era view and subscriber figures (2025) are about YouTube promotion, not in-game analytics.
- 2017–2018 GameAnalytics advice (T10479, T10508) predates native Creator Hub analytics; use native tools first and treat third-party trackers as optional.
- 2020 items on sponsored reporting (T10850, T10852, N4209, N4210): old reporting was deprecated after 12/31/2020; export or ignore those reports and verify attribution in current tools.
- 2021 Developer Stats targets (T10925, N4233–N4236) predate the Performance Dashboard; prefer current Performance Dashboard and MicroProfiler data.
- 2024 ad and revenue examples (GnomeCode, PineappleDev, ByteBlox) are self-reported and reflect older Creator Dashboard layouts; UI paths changed to Creator Hub in 2026 (e.g. Statistics > Analytics > Acquisition).
- Home Recommendations weight day-7 retention and playtime from Nov 2025 onward (T13030); earlier advice did not reflect this.
- August 2026 bot-removal cleanup makes visits and CCU before/after comparisons unreliable; treat the affected period as a correction window.
- Analytics API docs and alerts documentation are dated Oct 2026 and are the most current platform reference.
- Funnel logging API (LogOnboardingFunnelStepEvent) is cited from Mar 2025; confirm it still exists before implementing.
- Creator Hub Analytics > Funnels and the onboarding funnel API are described as current in Sept 2026 (T16679, T16698); prefer them over ad hoc event tracking where available.
- Safety analytics (report abuse vs 90th percentile) appeared around Nov 2025 (T16312); older guidance does not cover it.
- Sales history limits conflict by date: Last Level Studios reported one year of revenue history (2020, N6123) while Mulch reported about two months of developer product history (2023, T16003). Check current limits before relying on either.
- The 0.7 gross-to-net Robux multiplier is from 2023 (T16000) and fee structure may have changed; verify the current DevEx rate.
- Most campaign figures (2024–2026) are one-off self-reported snapshots and may be stale; use them as examples, not benchmarks.
- The 5-minute retention target (>18%) is a single 2026 creator's self-set target, not a platform standard.
- As of November 2026, platform growth has reversed YoY: DAU was +10% in Q2 2026 but -9% in Q3 2026, with hours -15% (N7201, N7213, N7267). Q3 2026 may partly reflect a tough comparison against 2025 hit peaks (inference).
- The 2026 bookings guidance of $8.28-8.55B (22-26% growth) (N7124) has been superseded by the Q2 low-end result and the Q3 guidance of -14% to -18% YoY (N7206, N7266). Prefer the latest official figures.
- Peak CCU figures for Grow a Garden and Steal a Brainrot (2025) are historical records; current figures are much lower (N7178, N7140). Do not use 2025 peaks as current benchmarks.
- Tracker figures dated 2026-10 and 2026-11, and public API snapshots taken on 2026-11-01, are the most recent data points. Items marked 2026-00 have no month and are approximate.
- RIVALS and other mid-tier games show large swings within two months (N7152, N7182), so refresh benchmark snapshots at least monthly.
- Late-2026 features (Journey analytics, analytics agent, Observability Platform) were announced in Sept 2026 posts as 'Late 2026'. As of 2026-11-01 their availability is unconfirmed; check current docs before relying on them.
- The single-player Play Test Agent is in beta (Oct 2026 post).
- The abuse report analytics dashboard was announced in Oct 2026. If it replaces the older 90th-percentile check, use the dashboard's peer comparison instead.
- Platform DAU: over 100M (Aug 2025) was superseded by ~150M (Jan 2026). Platform hours: 13B per month (Feb 2026) and 29B last quarter (Sept 2026) use different windows.
- Pre-existing advice to build observability dashboards for endpoints and data stores still applies. The Observability Platform adds client-session tracing on top.
- As of 2026-10, attribution is probabilistic; avoid relying on last-click alone for acquisition analysis.
- Grow a Garden decay claim (over 90% CCU or DAU drop by late 2026) is a host assertion, unverified; re-check before using.
- Roblox PC/console H1 2026 comparison and the steal-style revenue share are 2026 estimates from third parties; check for updates.
- All figures in this batch are from 2026-09 to 2026-10 and may age quickly for live games.
- New figures span Feb 2025 to May 2026. Player counts, plays and visits change fast, so the 2025 figures (N7791–N7794, N7811) may be stale as of 2026-11-01. Re-check live numbers before relying on them.
- The May 2026 session-length and earnings items (N7840, N7841) are the most recent in this batch but are informal estimates, not measured data.
- Deconstructor of Fun (2026-06) gives the most recent influencer attribution guidance; prefer its 7-day click window and long-tail install windows over the standard 24-hour UA default.
- GameMakers 2021 ad completion and feed watch figures (N7875-N7877) are dated and tied to Audience Network and Facebook formats, not Roblox ad formats.
- GameMakers 2022 prioritisation and cohort guidance (T18446, N7892, Q1878) is older; the method still applies but the examples are mobile.
- Temple Run (2024) and League of Legends (2022) figures are older and cross-genre; use as illustrations only.
- Mixed early-game metrics (janisjanis01, 2026-08 and 2026-10) describe a specific game trajectory, not a general rule.
- ATT and consent opt-in rates (N8070, 2024-10) are mobile-platform specific and change with OS policy; re-measure rather than reuse.
- Indie Roblox scale figures span 2024-06 to 2025-09; concurrency and visit benchmarks for late 2026 may differ.
- N8038 (2021 GameMakers, Playtika organic gap) predates the current Roblox context; treat as historical.
- The 2025 janisjanis01 flop-case figures are low-advice and dated; do not use them as current targets.

## Instructions for an agent using this file
- If a metric is below benchmark, then fix the weakest below-50th metric first using same-genre benchmarks.
- If D1 is low, then audit onboarding with a micro-step funnel and New User First Session Retention; D7 low means review progression, D30 low means add end-game.
- If conversion is high but ARPPU is low, then add post-first-purchase content; if wallets keep growing, add sinks or cap faucets.
- If a metric drops, then check timing against publishes, then slice by platform/device/source/version before changing design.
- If impressions fall, then check own updates, algorithm changes, seasonality and competitors, allowing for signal-to-impression lag.
- If DAU is under ~500-1,000, then skip experiments and use qualitative feedback; otherwise check MDE first.
- If an experiment is not significant, then treat it as no change; never ramp a significantly negative result or decide on day-one data.
- If sending analytics events, then do it from the server in a published game, after success, with validation and custom fields rather than extra event names.
- If running ads, then wait 24h learning and 48h delay, use Ads Manager for ROI at day 30, and check Creator Analytics for retention harm; for rewarded video check impressions, opt-in (15%+) and frequency (3+).
- If quoting indie CCU/revenue numbers or after any release, then label indie claims unverified, verify income with My Transactions, and check error report, crash rate and alerts.
- If a metric spiked after an ad push or update, wait about 7 days and compare the settled baseline before calling the change a win or failure.
- If judging a new game, use the four-signal scorecard at about 2 days post-release for triage, then decide after 1-2 weeks.
- If reading FPS, memory or session time, use P10 for the slowest players and P90 for the worst case on lower-is-better metrics; never rely on averages alone.
- If an ad campaign is under 48 hours old, do not decide anything; plays can be credited for up to 30 days.
- If ad CTR or cost per play is weak and the overview stats are below the 50th percentile, fix the game before raising spend.
- If a DataStore or Memory Store metric reaches 70% of quota, act now rather than waiting for the critical overage.
- If data is older than 28 days (performance) or 30 days (dashboards, thumbnails), it is gone; export promptly.
- If the Benchmarks API returns empty or 400, check availableTypes, benchmarkVariantId, the percentile count and the date range, in that order.
- If comparing a game for purchase or as a benchmark, use income per CCU and spend vs earned, not concurrency alone.
- If setting targets, use your own history and genre-realistic numbers, not front-page games.
- If funnel charts are empty, check View Events first to confirm events arrive; charts can take about 24 hours.
- If a player-count or visit drop falls within the August 2026 bot-removal period, flag it as an accuracy correction and compare against real-user trends over weeks, not as an engagement loss.
- If the API returns a NotStatisticallySignificant or Projected status, do not make decisions from that point.
- When computing weekly or monthly totals, use distinct-user buckets, not sums of daily values.
- When comparing against dashboard figures, convert the reporting day to UTC.
- When an ad campaign produces no Home Recommendations after its run, recommend stopping ads and pivoting rather than buying more.
- When playtime during ads is under 10 minutes, warn that the game is unlikely to reach the algorithm; under 20 minutes on the algorithm is a weak sign.
- Prefer 2026 official Roblox figures over 2020–2021 indie figures and speaker estimates; label low-advice and self-reported numbers as such.
- Do not recommend specific monetisation or traffic decisions from speaker revenue estimates (e.g. per-game income claims) without verified data.
- Keep alert configurations within 20 per experience and use consecutiveOccurrences to avoid noise.
- If judging a game's like ratio after a bug fix, use only the post-fix window and say so.
- If computing ad ROI, convert gross Robux to net first (×0.7 or current DevEx rate), then compare with ad spend in USD.
- If the dashboard's history window is short, export sales CSVs on a regular cadence and sum them externally.
- If a paid campaign is under 7 days old, do not judge D7 retention; use 7-day moving averages and say the window is incomplete.
- If a funnel shows a red drop, confirm the step fires in the Output window before concluding the design is at fault.
- If new-player data shows low engagement after ads, split new versus returning players before cutting features.
- If changing currency, always include an economy transaction type and SKU so sinks and sources can be analysed.
- If computing revenue, remove your own test purchases first.
- If reviewing moderation, compare report levels to the 90th percentile of games rather than an absolute number.
- If performance tests disagree across runs, check that graphics level is pinned to 10 on every test device.
- If a game's DAU or CCU rises or falls, check the platform-wide trend for the same quarter (Q3 2026 DAU -9% YoY) before attributing the change to an update.
- When citing a CCU peak, state the source, date and whether it is official or tracker data. Do not mix 25.4M and 25.8M for the same title.
- Use the Roblox public API for live CCU, visits, favorites and votes, and store each reading with a timestamp.
- If reporting session length, first check and disclose any AFK or auto-mode features that inflate it.
- For age and region analysis, always compute monetization per engaged hour and not only totals.
- When recommending server count, divide CCU by 30 and show the calculation.
- Do not use 2025 hit-game peaks as current benchmarks; prefer 2026 and official figures.
- Treat any figure marked claimed or self-reported as low-confidence and label it accordingly.
- If the analytics agent, Journey analytics or the Observability Platform is not yet available to your account, use the existing funnel, dashboard and alert methods and log step names consistently so you can migrate later.
- If an update causes a major CCU or crash-rate regression and you have pre-set rollback thresholds, trigger the rollback rather than waiting for a manual review.
- Use AI summaries and the analytics agent to generate hypotheses and triage; confirm numbers in the raw dashboards before making a release decision.
- For abuse-report questions, compare against similar games in the dashboard rather than judging absolute takedown counts alone.
- Prefer official Roblox figures; label tracker and indie figures as self-reported and do not mix them with official totals.
- When citing platform scale, use the most recent official figure and state its date.
- If platform attribution and MMP data disagree, report both and do not reallocate spend on one source alone.
- If a game's CCU has risen sharply, compare it to its own multi-week trend before calling it a breakout.
- If a user asks about idle-game income, request income per second before and after each major purchase or boost.
- If a user plans to clone a competitor, ask for the original's CCU trajectory and suggest a small organic test first.
- Label self-reported and third-party figures explicitly; never present a creator estimate as a measured benchmark.
- Do not cite the disputed 20% top-spender statistic as fact.
- When quoting market-share or revenue-concentration figures, name the source and its estimate status.
- If a user asks for scale or benchmark figures from these sources, state that they are creator-reported and undated sampling, not official data.
- If a figure names plays, visits or CCU, keep that metric label; do not convert plays to visits or visits to CCU.
- If a figure is an estimate or guess (N7793, N7841), flag it as an estimate and do not use it as a target.
- Skip non-analytics items from this batch (leaderboard setup T18049, DataStore debugging T18055, YouTube video views N7799) rather than adding them to the analytics knowledge base.
- If evaluating an influencer or creator campaign, use a minimum 7-day click window and never compare it with UA on a 7-30 day window.
- If judging creator value, use day-365 or lifetime installs plus a per-market organic uplift proxy, not day 7 or 30 CPI.
- If a campaign is giveaway-based, assume lower organic uplift (~30%) than non-giveaway posts (up to ~100% of attributed traffic).
- If a vertical comparison has fewer than 10 videos or about 500k views, flag it as insufficient before recommending a change.
- If designing a live event, write the target KPI first and choose the event design to move it.
- If payers cluster at fixed spend totals, align price points to those clusters before making other pricing changes.
- If evaluating gacha or item economies, report price, volume, equip rate, spend depth and drop-off point per item.
- If prioritising features, score each by estimated LTV impact divided by effort (1-10) and sort descending.
- If early metrics are mixed, monitor the full set over time rather than acting on one reading.
- Label all indie figures as self-reported and prefer official Roblox data where available.
- If consent or attribution coverage is low, model results from an opted-in survey and apply them to the cohort, using one method across all channels.
- If revenue events are too sparse to optimise on, switch the optimisation event to a frequently firing action such as tutorial complete or CPA.
- If visits are healthy but the like ratio is low, diagnose game quality before buying more traffic.
- If a traction spike appears, record its game type, packaging and traffic source before the next update.
- Treat all figures from this batch as self-reported unless an official Roblox source confirms them.

## Not covered here (research elsewhere)
- Concrete D7/D30, DAU/MAU and ARPDAU benchmark values by genre (only a few example figures are given).
- How the ranking algorithm weights each signal numerically (only that 20+ are weighted and none dominates).
- Privacy and policy limits on analytics data beyond RTBF notes.
- Detailed methods for external tools (webhooks, BI, PlayFab) beyond mentions.
- Studio-side profiling workflows (MicroProfiler and memory) are mentioned only briefly.
- Statistical basics (CI, SRM, power calculations) beyond the official explanations.
- Verified evidence for indie revenue and CCU claims.
- Current (late 2026) behaviour of the analytics agent and custom dashboards, which were only announced.
- No genre-level ARPDAU, ARPPU, D1 or D7 benchmark values beyond the illustrative N3148 example.
- No official Premium payout or Engagement payout figures in these notes.
- No concrete GameAnalytics coverage; the existing rule's history is not expanded here.
- No event schema or funnel tooling specifics beyond existing rules.
- Indie revenue, CCU and income figures are unverified; no official revenue or earnings API is covered.
- Home Recommendation and algorithm thresholds are not quantified; janisjanis's six algorithm stats and Yann's six overview stats are not named.
- Regional benchmark values are not given (T6663 is qualitative only).
- No Benchmarks API response schema or how to compute your game's percentile rank.
- No seasonality or trend-overlay numbers.
- No Robux-to-USD conversion rate; revenue figures in Robux and USD cannot be compared directly.
- Anti-cheat page estimate values and their accuracy are not specified (T8622).
- No source covers how Home Recommendation ranking actually works beyond D7 retention and playtime weighting; verify with official Roblox documentation.
- No verified benchmarks for D30 retention, ARPDAU or ARPPU by genre in these notes; the Quez ARPDAU comparison (T12807) is unquantified.
- Relationship between the speaker's '500 engaged players' audience counter (T13613) and Roblox's official highly-engaged definition (N5181) is unclear; confirm before relying on either.
- Definition of QPTR (play-through rate) and whether it equals the 2–3% benchmark used for conversion is not stated.
- No guidance on A/B experiment statistics beyond the earlier rule; these notes do not cover sample size or significance.
- No official source on how long bot removal affects retention metrics; the docs only say weeks.
- Analytics API authentication for non-developer accounts and pricing/quota for paid tiers are not covered.
- Attribution window and max-attributed-plays rules come from a 2020 DevForum convention; confirm they still apply to the current ad reporting.
- No official Roblox documentation in this set confirms the AnalyticsService funnel signature or economy argument order; verify against the Creator Hub API docs.
- No current DevEx conversion rate or fee schedule is provided; the 0.7 factor is unverified.
- No genre-specific benchmarks for 5-minute retention or session length beyond two creators' self-set targets.
- Definition and timing of the safety analytics percentile calculation are not documented in these sources.
- Revenue history retention limits are inconsistent across sources and dates; current official limit is unconfirmed.
- Sample sizes are tiny (hundreds to a few thousand players); no statistical significance testing is described.
- Survivorship and self-selection bias: creators mostly share wins or dramatic failures.
- Rating reset mechanics (whether old dislikes ever fade) are not explained beyond one creator's anecdote.
- Definitions of 'player conversion rate' and 'engaged player' vary between creators and are not standardised.
- No genre-level D1/D7/D30 retention or conversion benchmarks are given in these sources.
- No ARPDAU or ARPPU values are given; the bookings figures are platform-level only.
- No ad metrics (EPM, rewarded-video opt-in, impression rates) are covered.
- The actual signal list and relative weights in the Creator Analytics dashboard are not reproduced (T17490 points to them but does not list them).
- Methodology for tracker CCU estimates (RoWatcher, RoTrends, GosuGamers via Rolimon's, GGAID) is undocumented, so their accuracy is unknown.
- Public API field definitions and update frequency for 'players now' are not explained.
- The Q3 2026 DAU drop (138M, -9% YoY) is not reconciled with the Q2 2026 +10% figure in these sources; the official quarterly report should be checked.
- No data on how session length or hours are measured by Roblox for the purpose of game-level comparison.
- No thresholds or examples for setting automatic rollback criteria (T17610 gives no numbers).
- No information on availability, pricing, or access tiers for the analytics agent, Journey analytics, the Observability Platform or the Play Test Agent.
- No evidence on the accuracy or bias of AI feedback summaries (T17612); validate against raw samples.
- No coverage of how Journey Sankey funnels define branches or handle loops and re-entries.
- No per-genre CCU, retention or conversion benchmarks in these items; platform-scale numbers are not targets for individual games.
- Q1778 describes recording action-reaction pairs with state for training world models. The item does not explain how a developer would use it for game analytics, so it is not turned into a rule.
- The GamesBeat item (T17598) gives no specific metric targets or thresholds.
- No MMP setup or configuration details (AppsFlyer, Adjust, Singular) beyond the high-level recommendation.
- No verified lifecycle decay curves or benchmarks for CCU or DAU decay by genre.
- No methodology guidance for income-per-second tracking beyond the Mulch case.
- No official Roblox data on clone performance or conversion rates.
- No verified source for the Grow a Garden decay figure.
- No official Roblox data on concentration of revenue by title or payer.
- How the Cumin 10–30 minute sample was chosen, its size, and which games it covered (N7840).
- Official, dated CCU and visit counts for the reference games cited (N7791–N7794, N7811); check the public API or Creator Dashboard.
- Genre and platform tags for the GnomeCode and JustDucksey example games, needed to make them genre-realistic benchmarks.
- Whether 99 Nights in the Forest's 1M CCU is a peak, average or daily figure (N7811).
- Sources on DataStore event debugging (T18055) and leaderboard setup (T18049) are outside the analytics-metrics topic and are not covered here.
- No Roblox-specific influencer or creator attribution data; the influencer guidance comes from mobile-game sources.
- No official Roblox guidance on influencer tracking windows or organic uplift measurement.
- Organic uplift figures (~30%, up to ~100%) come from one client and one campaign and are not independently verified.
- No Roblox gacha, item-level or spend-bucket benchmarks in these sources.
- No official Roblox ad completion or watch-time benchmarks; the 2021 figures are third-party and dated.
- The League of Legends ARPU figure is an estimate from one speaker and is not a same-genre benchmark.
- No validation of the 'six core stats' list in janisjanis01 (T18713) against the four-signal scorecard in the existing file.
- No details on how the 'Q1918' lifetime install comparison was measured, so its direction should be checked against the source.
- No [OFFICIAL] Roblox sources in this batch; every figure is indie or speaker self-reported.
- No Roblox-native guidance on consent, opt-in or attribution workarounds; research platform measurement documentation directly.
- No genre-level thresholds for like ratio, CTR or session length.
- No minimum sample size guidance for survey-based attribution modelling.
- No details on badge limits or on logging badge events server-side.
- Mobile-only items (ATT rates, Playtika, Jam City, Game of War) do not transfer directly to Roblox.

## Quotes (near-verbatim)
- "N.B Because of Roblox, acquisition is not accurate so uhhh, don't use that." ([Roblox DevForum 2017-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083))
- "this is totally fake chart we don't actually have data about how much fun people are having" ([Roblox 2018-07 6:15](https://youtu.be/4rvzuYMtSlA?t=375))
- "I track every individual purchase I track all the flow of my currencies" ([Roblox 2018-07 5:56](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=356))
- "it takes about an hour and all of a sudden you have retention statistics you have new users versus returning users" ([Roblox 2018-07 37:16](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=2236))
- "The second you think that you know which of your ideas is going to be great is the beginning of the end for you as a creator." ([Roblox 2018-07 11:30](https://youtu.be/EDY-8V943IE?t=690))
- "Minutes spent or engagement is your best proxy for love. Time doesn't lie." ([Roblox 2018-07 12:10](https://youtu.be/EDY-8V943IE?t=730))
- "Give it a few months in order to see how much Premium Payouts benefits you." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-02 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-premium-payouts/455347))
- "Do not only make choices based on data!" ([Roblox DevForum 2020-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057))
- "Without retention you're constantly treading water having to put out more updates to keep your game where it is." ([Roblox Creators 2021-07 1:01:09](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=3669))
- "It definitely helps because I was just using the default Roblox ones." ([Roblox Creators 2021-08 1:17:49](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=4669))
- "Avoid items that create a new source of resources and might unbalance things, or at the very least, build in limits." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 25:28](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1528))
- "certain competitions, if a user plays them, they're more likely to come back the next day." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 31:55](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=1915))
- "a sprint button... actually like massively reduced day one retention so maybe people were running past each other" ([Roblox Creators 2022-02 28:17](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=1697))
- "A player who spends any amount in your game is much more likely again in the future to spend in your game." ([Roblox Creators 2022-02 9:20](https://youtu.be/RfpMZZFy8Zw?t=560))
- "Understanding just how much time my players are actually playing my game so I can tune my features." ([Roblox Creators 2022-02 4:03](https://youtu.be/RfpMZZFy8Zw?t=243))
- "Building an experience without analytics is like driving a car to an unknown place without navigation." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794))
- "ultimately it's about getting people to play longer rather than just getting them to maybe spend more" ([Roblox Creators 2022-04 0:46](https://youtu.be/QYOaK1hXN1Y?t=46))

## Verified additions

_Hand-added, labelled and sourced rows kept in `youtube-learn/supplements/topics/analytics-metrics.md`; they survive rebuilds._

- **D30 definition and gap fixes** (official): Docs define D30 as players returning 30 days after first play; a low D30 usually means missing end-game content or goals. No numeric target is published; use Similar Experience Benchmarks (50th-90th percentile) in Analytics or the GameAnalytics medians in the numbers table. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-11](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started). Newest source 2026-11-01.
- **ARPDAU unit is not specified by Roblox and no official range exists** (official): Docs define ARPDAU as the average amount players spend and give no unit or benchmark. DevForum anecdotes (unit unclear): 0.5-1.5 called low, 3-5 decent, top games above 2 and up to 8; the ratio is driven by returning long-term players. Treat as single-source and compare only with your own Analytics benchmark. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-11](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started), [Roblox DevForum 2026](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-game-struggling-with-monetization-lf-advice-arpdau/4319347). Newest source 2026-11-01.
- **Kids/Select Highly Engaged Player (HEP) thresholds: entry 250, maintenance 25; older 500/100 figures are superseded** (official, 2026-11-01): Docs say a game completes evaluation at 250 unique plays by highly engaged age-checked users in a 60-day window. Staff cut entry 500 -> 250 on 2026-08-19 and maintenance 100 -> 25 on 2026-07-16. Creators who said 500 (Tizzy, LovJak, VuukStudios) or 'below 100 loses eligibility' were right for their date only. RDC26 staff post announces a further drop to 100 in Nov 2026 (announced, unconfirmed live). Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs kids-and-select 2026-11-01](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/kids-and-select), [DevForum staff 2026-08-19](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/highly-engaged-player-threshold-drops-to-250/4820164). Newest source 2026-08-19.

| Metric | Value | Context | Sources |
|---|---|---|---|
| Roblox retention, games over 1M MAU (GameAnalytics 2026) | D1 10.3% median / 22.2% top 1%; D7 1.6% / 9.1%; D30 0.5% / 4.7% | 500+ games, Aug 2025-Jul 2026, 4.76B sessions; excludes smaller games, so smaller games will likely be lower. Secondary summary of the original report. | [GameAnalytics 2026-09](https://gamedevreports.substack.com/p/gameanalytics-key-roblox-and-roblox) |
| Roblox session length (GameAnalytics 2026) | median 9.8 min; top 5% 16.6; top 1% 21.1; sessions/day median 1.56, top 10% 3.22 | Same sample. | [GameAnalytics 2026-09](https://gamedevreports.substack.com/p/gameanalytics-key-roblox-and-roblox) |
| Roblox ARPPU and conversion (GameAnalytics 2026) | ARPPU median $0.70, top 10% $2.33, top 5% $4.80, top 1% $55.31; payer conversion 3.80% (4.2% two years earlier) | USD per payer per the summary; ARPDAU not reported. Conversion is player-level. | [GameAnalytics 2026-09](https://gamedevreports.substack.com/p/gameanalytics-key-roblox-and-roblox) |
| ARPDAU anecdotes (DevForum community) | 0.5-1.5 low; 3-5 decent; top games above 2, up to 8 | Unit unclear, anecdotal, genre dependent. | [Roblox DevForum 2026](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-game-struggling-with-monetization-lf-advice-arpdau/4319347) |
| Likes ratio example | 80.6% described as stable good feedback | One DevForum anecdote; no official target. | [Roblox DevForum 2024](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/is-it-possible-to-get-very-high-arpdaus/2950843) |

## Appendix: every extracted tip on this topic

- 2016-07-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-basic-optimization/26756): Time code by storing tick() before a loop of repeated calls and printing tick() minus start afterward, to compare versions.
- 2017-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083): Set up GameAnalytics with ServerInit once on the server and ClientInit once per client, as early as possible after join, to get retention and playtime data.
- 2017-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083): Lower the module's send-interval setting (around line 5) to about 1 while debugging so events appear quickly instead of waiting a minute.
- 2017-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083): Do not rely on GameAnalytics acquisition data for Roblox games; the author says it is inaccurate due to Roblox.
- 2017-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083): Verify a test session in GameAnalytics Realtime; expect processing of about 15 minutes for small games, up to 45 during slowdowns.
- 2017-11-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838): Add an analytics tool such as GameAnalytics to see where players spend time and money.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [5:56](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=356): Track every purchase and every currency source and sink; compare currency bought versus earned, especially during sales.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [7:19](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=439): Compare sales where players spend from balance with sales that trigger new Robux deposits; the latter signals stronger demand.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [37:16](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=2236): Use GameAnalytics (free, about an hour to set up) for retention, new versus returning users, and play times.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [11:30](https://youtu.be/EDY-8V943IE?t=690): Set decision razors before launch: retention (daily actives, return rate) and engagement (minutes per active player). Kill features that don't move them.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [12:10](https://youtu.be/EDY-8V943IE?t=730): Treat time spent as the truest signal of love; purchases can mislead because players may regret buys they didn't value.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [23:34](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=1414): Use a game analytics module to tie promo code redemptions to players and measure campaign effectiveness.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [3:14](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=194): Target join time: median around 5 seconds is good; over 10 seconds is not great. Measure up to the interactive state, including loading screens.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [6:03](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=363): Check the Developer Console client and server memory breakdown per device type; memory categories differ between platforms, so measure on the target device.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [7:29](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=449): Use Microprofiler (Ctrl+Alt+F6 on desktop, enabled in settings on mobile) to find why frames are slow, including your own script timings.
- 2018-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-resources-mega-thread/177780): Use GameAnalytics (retention and monetisation) to track player metrics, per the 'Using GameAnalytics' thread.
- 2019-03-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/monetisation-101-how-to-effectively-generate-revenue-in-your-roblox-game/246902): Track time to first purchase, time to repeat purchase, and the percentage of users converting between player stages.
- 2019-03-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/monetisation-101-how-to-effectively-generate-revenue-in-your-roblox-game/246902): Build custom monetisation tracking; Roblox base analytics do not show how your specific purchase systems perform.
- 2019-03-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-simple-global-leaderboard/256754): Change the OrderedDataStore key to create separate leaderboards or reset data; use date-based keys (day/month/year) for periodic resets.
- 2020-02-14 Last Level Studios [46:18](https://youtu.be/_LdpUJ41cso?t=2778): Judge ratings using recent data only; old dislikes from an early buggy version can permanently hurt a game that was later fixed.
- 2020-02-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-premium-payouts/455347): Use the Premium Playtime Score to get daily feedback on new features; a sustained rise usually precedes payout increases.
- 2020-02-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-premium-payouts/455347): Judge feature impact over months, not days; the Robux Earned figure aggregates 28 days of Premium behaviour.
- 2020-03-02 AlvinBlox [3:22](https://youtu.be/oh89BQkBvBM?t=202): Check the Premium tab in game Developer Stats (game page three-dot menu) to see your Premium score and Robux earned per day; only available for your own games.
- 2020-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057): Chart average cash by level bracket (1-10, 11-20, etc.) and sales per weapon to see real economy health before changing it.
- 2020-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057): Filter analytics to exclude exploiters and players inactive for 15-30 days, and keep timestamps on all data.
- 2020-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057): Use analytics only as far as budget allows, since analytics services can cost significantly.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805): During launch research, track average session time and which gamepasses or developer products sell best.
- 2020-10-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597): Check attributed play stats in the new reporting to measure how many plays each ad drives; attribution is 1-day view, 28-day click-through.
- 2020-10-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597): Export or save old sponsored reports before the old reporting is deprecated (after 12/31/2020, later the week of Jan 13th).
- 2021-01-23 TechSpectrum [0:01](https://youtu.be/-PPg4N5sKXE?t=1): Set every test device to manual graphics level 10 so Roblox doesn't auto-disable lighting effects; this keeps stress tests comparable.
- 2021-01-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074): Use Developer Stats targets: Memory 400-600 MB realistic, CPU and GPU frame time ideally 15-20 ms, not the 33 ms ceiling.
- 2021-01-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074): Diagnose network with Sent and Received stats; ping depends on the player's connection, so investigate high ping only after those are fine.
- 2021-07-16 Roblox Creators [34:15](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=2055): Instrument each feature: track level progression, pet and dungeon usage, and use the data to decide future content and whether to remove features.
- 2021-07-16 Roblox Creators [35:47](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=2147): Prioritise retention as the key metric, with ARPU and ARPPU for monetisation health; Red Manta used GameAnalytics SDK for tracking.
- 2021-07-16 Roblox Creators [1:00:29](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=3629): Watch long-term retention (week-later returns) to judge an update's success; it is slower to read but shows whether the update kept players.
- 2021-07-29 Roblox Creators [22:08](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=1328): Implement analytics before launch and watch feature engagement and drop-off points on launch day to catch game-breaking flows.
- 2021-08-05 Roblox Creators [13:25](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=805): Set analytics and feature funnels before closed beta so you can track how far players get into the feature.
- 2021-08-12 Roblox Creators [21:50](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=1310): Track conversion rate (share of players who ever spend) as the most important monetisation metric; a first purchase predicts repeat spend.
- 2021-08-12 Roblox Creators [23:55](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=1435): Use RPPU (revenue per paying user) to gauge spend depth; if low, first purchase was bad or first offer was too generous.
- 2021-08-12 Roblox Creators [28:00](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=1680): Build feature funnels by firing an event per step (open store, select, buy, equip, feed) to find drop-off points and fix UX there.
- 2021-08-26 Roblox Creators [1:17:49](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=4669): Use analytics on specific levels played, levels escaped, and shop items bought; polls alone miss players who never use Discord or Twitter.
- 2021-08-26 Roblox Creators [1:19:18](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=4758): Do not rely only on default Roblox analytics; track level completion rates to balance difficulty.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [13:35](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=815): Use metrics and player feedback to spot fatigue: falling purchases or quest completion across several releases signals a pullback.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [25:28](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1528): Avoid unlimited resource sources (Adopt Me money tree printed $5/10min, later capped at $100/day); build in limits and track inflation.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [49:03](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=2943): Run a retrospective after each release and compare DAU and payer conversion against previous releases to guide future themes and timing.
- 2021-09-09 Roblox Creators [31:14](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=1874): Track which competitions players do and compare with next-day retention to measure each competition's quality more accurately than feedback alone.
- 2021-09-09 Roblox Creators [51:59](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=3119): Measure UI success with player counts and analytics, since successful UI generates no direct feedback. Beware a vocal minority of dedicated players.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [36:13](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=2173): Assume players are active only about 75% of logged-in session time (and that paying players are more engaged); tune from real analytics after first season.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [49:03](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=2943): Instrument analytics in your game; use percentile player profiles (P30 to P100) for session length and sessions per day, split by PC and mobile.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [38:15](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=2295): After the first season, use completion data (tiers reached, percent finishing, average dailies completed) to rebalance the next season.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [1:34:20](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=5660): Use A/B tests to tailor mission difficulty per player segment if resources allow, but watch for player outrage when segments see unequal tasks.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [26:02](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=1562): Watch for inflated currencies; release sink items to pull resources out, and cap faucets (Adopt Me money tree was limited to $100/day).
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [29:17](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=1757): Track completion, purchases and engagement per release; a downward trend across releases signals fatigue and need for a new system.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [49:07](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=2947): Run a retrospective after each release focused on process; compare DAU and payer conversion against previous releases.
- 2021-11-18 Roblox Creators [49:23](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=2963): Track purchases and playtime with GameAnalytics and Roblox's built-in analytics; deeper tracking of drop-offs pays off for big updates.
- 2021-11-18 Roblox Creators [53:28](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=3208): Expect playtime per session to shrink once a game gets many players; around 15-20 minutes average is strong, 12 minutes is still decent.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [28:00](https://youtu.be/DHvfEB8myyU?t=1680): Track impressions, clicks, CTR, cost per click, attributed visits (post-click and post-impression), conversion rate and cost per play in sponsored reporting.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [15:44](https://youtu.be/wrf6DRlfoO8?t=944): Track main demographic, engagement per product (visits for games), and followers/members joining and leaving as community health signals.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [16:35](https://youtu.be/wrf6DRlfoO8?t=995): Watch for quote tweets far exceeding likes/retweets; it signals people discuss you negatively rather than positively.
- 2021-12-09 Roblox Creators [26:09](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=1569): Use retention by day to locate progression problems: D1/D7 for early grind, D30 for the late progression tail.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox Creators [21:22](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=1282): Track weapon and attachment usage, map voting rates and spending in PlayFab; avoid letting analytics drive every decision, use them as one input alongside intuition.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox Creators [26:30](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=1590): Judge live ops success by baseline earnings (weekday average plus weekend), engagement/average playtime per update, D1, W1 and M1 retention, and new users.
- 2022-02-01 GDC Festival of Gaming [17:22](https://youtu.be/WGRpglQ87XE?t=1042): Tag each onboarding step as an event to build a funnel; the funnel pinpoints the exact churn points to fix.
- 2022-02-01 GDC Festival of Gaming [25:16](https://youtu.be/WGRpglQ87XE?t=1516): Pair qualitative findings with telemetry. Small samples explain why something happens; telemetry shows how many players are affected.
- 2022-02-17 Roblox Creators [27:36](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=1656): Check day-one retention after shipping features; a sprint button cut D1 retention because it reduced social encounters.
- 2022-02-17 Roblox Creators [58:50](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=3530): Benchmark social games at 10+ minute sessions, but prioritise retention over session length for young casual audiences.
- 2022-02-24 Roblox Creators [0:52](https://youtu.be/RfpMZZFy8Zw?t=52): If Day 1 retention is below benchmark, check onboarding and first-time experience first; players are likely confused or frustrated.
- 2022-02-24 Roblox Creators [9:20](https://youtu.be/RfpMZZFy8Zw?t=560): Treat conversion rate as the key monetization metric; compare it to competitor benchmarks and review whether offers feel worth buying.
- 2022-02-24 Roblox Creators [4:03](https://youtu.be/RfpMZZFy8Zw?t=243): After a major feature update, compare average session length before and after to judge whether the feature helped or hurt.
- 2022-02-24 Roblox Creators [3:21](https://youtu.be/RfpMZZFy8Zw?t=201): Compare monthly active users year over year, same month, to separate seasonal dips (e.g. October, December) from real decline.
- 2022-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794): Use Day 1 and Day 7 retention against benchmarks before spending money promoting a newly launched experience; fix onboarding and retention first.
- 2022-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794): Use average session time together with D1 retention as an early indicator of long-term retention.
- 2022-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794): Calculate DAU/MAU ratio to measure how often an average player returns within a month; a high ratio means frequent engagement.
- 2022-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794): Compare KPIs against the benchmark shown for the next tier up to set realistic improvement goals; benchmarks update quarterly.
- 2022-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794): Segment engagement KPIs by age group, country, language, platform, and OS to find where to iterate content.
- 2022-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794): Export chart data as CSV for offline analysis beyond the dashboard.
- 2022-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794): Measure average session time per session by using total playtime divided by number of sessions; teleports between places count as one session.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [45:02](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=2702): Track which items, creatures or features players ignore or over-use to spot weak systems and economy skew; act on what data shows.
- 2022-04-28 Roblox Creators [0:05](https://youtu.be/5N84f1ZuwRc?t=5): A/B test each core feature: 50% control without the feature, 50% with it, then compare results over one week.
