- 2022-04-28 Roblox Creators [0:05](https://youtu.be/5N84f1ZuwRc?t=5): Measure retention, money spent, time played and session count per group to judge whether a feature helps.
- 2022-04-28 Roblox Creators [0:05](https://youtu.be/QYOaK1hXN1Y?t=5): Judge each live-ops update by earnings vs a baseline: average weekday income plus weekend average, compared across updates for consistency.
- 2022-04-28 Roblox Creators [0:46](https://youtu.be/QYOaK1hXN1Y?t=46): Track average play time per update; rising playtime is a good sign, since live ops should make people play longer, not only spend more.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox Creators [1:08:49](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=4129): Use brand demographic data where provided and supplement with your own research; tools exist for tracking awareness and affinity.
- 2022-06-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-monetization-metrics-for-the-analytics-dashboard/1826688): Use ARPPU alongside conversion rate: high conversion with low ARPPU means players buy once but don't spend again; focus on post-first-purchase content.
- 2022-06-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-monetization-metrics-for-the-analytics-dashboard/1826688): Compare monetization KPIs across Age Group, Country, Language, Platform and OS to find which segments monetize best, then tailor content to them.
- 2022-06-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-monetization-metrics-for-the-analytics-dashboard/1826688): Don't rely on monetization KPIs for financial reporting; refunds aren't tracked. Check My Transactions for actual income.
- 2022-06-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-monetization-metrics-for-the-analytics-dashboard/1826688): Expect a two-day latency on monetization KPIs due to system limitations.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [37:44](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=2264): Skip metrics during prototyping. Use qualitative feedback early and add analytics close to launch, when most systems exist.
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [44:03](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=2643): Target one clear audience; a broad 'under 18' audience ended up hitting everyone and no one.
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [57:44](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=3464): Track per-lesson failure rates, tries to pass, drop-off points and time by device; rework lessons with high first-try failure.
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [1:02:09](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=3729): Track which activities and pets players choose, then A/B test onboarding (forced vs optional) and compare attention between groups.
- 2022-08-30 GameMakers [8:34](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=514): Prioritise by ROI: estimate LTV impact divided by effort (1-10), sort backlog descending, and attack low-hanging fruit first.
- 2022-10-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-benchmarking-insights-analytics-dashboards/2025790): Use the Engagement, Retention and Monetization dashboard benchmarks to compare your experience's KPIs against others; benchmarks update daily.
- 2022-10-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-benchmarking-insights-analytics-dashboards/2025790): Improving a KPI can move your benchmark tier higher, so track each KPI's tier as a progress signal.
- 2022-10-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-benchmarking-insights-analytics-dashboards/2025790): Switch from the Monthly Game Stats Data report to the new dashboards, which have more timely data and better benchmarks.
- 2022-11-22 Roblox Creators [35:06](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=2106): Give players in-game graphics toggles to disable heavy props and lighting, letting them trade visuals for performance.
- 2022-11-24 GameMakers [38:37](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=2317): Track D1/D7/D30 retention, spend per day, conversion and spend per user together. Changing one monetization lever forces changes in the others.
- 2022-11-24 GameMakers [42:46](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=2566): Check spend buckets: if payers cluster at fixed totals (e.g. $10, $100, $1000), adjust your price points to fit those clusters.
- 2022-12-01 Roblox Creators [20:31](https://youtu.be/0PEBFsguCdw?t=1231): Track player progress through the tutorial funnel to see where players drop off or struggle; it points you in a direction even if it doesn't explain why.
- 2023-01-11 LovJak [5:39](https://youtu.be/ZO-YioPJaIo?t=339): Add a leaderboard via the Toolbox (leaderstats) so click progress is visible and persists in the server.
- 2023-03-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-similar-experience-benchmarks-broader-access/2210285): Compare D1, D7, D30 retention, session time, payer conversion, ARPPU and ARPDAU against similar-genre benchmarks, not all-Roblox averages, to judge performance.
- 2023-03-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-similar-experience-benchmarks-broader-access/2210285): Read benchmarks as percentiles: 50th–90th range shows where your experience sits among similar experiences.
- 2023-04-12 Mulch [8:17](https://youtu.be/6perjdMg3T4?t=497): Multiply gross Robux by 0.7 to get real earnings before comparing against ad spend.
- 2023-04-12 Mulch [5:30](https://youtu.be/6perjdMg3T4?t=330): Creator dashboard shows only about two months of developer product history; export CSV sales and sum in Excel for older data.
- 2023-06-08 Roblox Creators [0:47](https://youtu.be/pWF06iX9_Gg?t=47): Track where time is spent by section and device to decide what to polish or rework, e.g. tutorials vs. a multiplayer section with little time spent.
- 2023-06-08 Roblox Creators [1:29](https://youtu.be/pWF06iX9_Gg?t=89): Track per-lesson failure rates, average tries to pass, lessons completed per session, and where players leave, to find spots that are too hard or demotivating.
- 2023-06-08 Roblox Creators [2:09](https://youtu.be/pWF06iX9_Gg?t=129): Instrument detailed analytics for almost everything; without knowing what happens in the game you cannot know what to iterate on.
- 2023-06-19 Suphi Kaner [2:22](https://youtu.be/4rNva5qXj-c?t=142): Debug DataStore behaviour by connecting to its StateChanged, Saving, Saved and AttemptsChanged events and printing them, then simulate offline play to test.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox Creators [2:00](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=120): Use the Creator Hub dashboard to spot problems and compare against peers. Low D1 retention suggests dedicating updates to onboarding.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox Creators [5:56](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=356): Healthy cadence shows as a wave on the dashboard: each new release pulls in players, then activity dips as they finish the content.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox Creators [17:40](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=1060): Measure every release in analytics: player engagement and revenue impact. Use results to iterate or change course.
- 2023-08-25 Roblox Creators [16:01](https://youtu.be/lOOvOIJoMi4?t=961): Find friction via user stories, playtests and analytics; small UX fixes can raise engagement, monetization and player happiness.
- 2023-09-06 Roblox Creators [0:08](https://youtu.be/N2l0BDgSNtQ?t=8): Track daily active users (DAU), monthly active users (MAU), and average session length as core engagement health metrics; higher DAU/MAU is better.
- 2023-09-06 Roblox Creators [0:08](https://youtu.be/N2l0BDgSNtQ?t=8): Use average session length to size content so most players can finish a meaningful chunk within a typical play session.
- 2023-09-06 Roblox Creators [0:50](https://youtu.be/N2l0BDgSNtQ?t=50): When engagement drops, ask: how are players interacting with the core loop, where do they quit (difficulty, missing info, technical issues), and when did the decline start.
- 2023-09-06 Roblox Creators [0:50](https://youtu.be/N2l0BDgSNtQ?t=50): Recent engagement decline suggests lack of new content; persistently low engagement suggests the core loop or primary features are missing something.
- 2023-09-08 Roblox Creators [0:13](https://youtu.be/LpAU6TheAZ4?t=13): Track day 1, day 7 and day 30 retention. Low D1 suggests onboarding problems, low D7 suggests weak progression, low D30 suggests lacking end-game content.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [22:53](https://youtu.be/9eaev-ypgWo?t=1373): For lagging monetization metrics, log each update and review the trend retroactively; review weekly with a monthly oversight.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [24:56](https://youtu.be/9eaev-ypgWo?t=1496): Use a real-time player-location map to spread players across the map so monetized vehicles are needed to reach earning spots.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [20:09](https://youtu.be/9eaev-ypgWo?t=1209): Use third-party analytics such as GameAnalytics plus webhooks to track custom events, since Roblox funnels were not yet supported.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [34:41](https://youtu.be/JjkAaXVULEk?t=2081): Track engagement rate per platform weekly; RB-style benchmark was at least 1 like or reply per 10 views, and a drop signals losing the audience.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [32:38](https://youtu.be/JjkAaXVULEk?t=1958): Judge creator videos by views over likes; post consistently, since consistency helped view counts and streams drive followers and recommended views.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [18:24](https://youtu.be/S8ORVEKec_4?t=1104): Iterate on small changes using analytics rather than building big unvalidated features; avoid feature creep.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [20:16](https://youtu.be/S8ORVEKec_4?t=1216): Measure partner-specific KPIs: interactions with brand NPCs and time spent in game, compared against the partner's other channels.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [21:39](https://youtu.be/S8ORVEKec_4?t=1299): Without platform benchmarks, benchmark against your own store or e-commerce engagement and social media time to see where audiences engage.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [25:25](https://youtu.be/Z-ZKqBPdl44?t=1525): Build or use A/B tooling to split players into experiences and compare metrics per cohort; hard to test everything in social/teleport games.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-personalized-insights-for-your-experience/2600369): When a metric drops or spikes week over week, use chart breakdowns (e.g. by platform) to find the cause.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-personalized-insights-for-your-experience/2600369): A metric at a 6-month high can still be below benchmark; keep improving it rather than stopping.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/create-and-manage-subscriptions-within-your-experience/2600664): Use subscription analytics to track sales, revenue, cancellation trends, and breakdowns by subscriber type (new, renewing, previously canceled) and platform.
- 2023-10-23 janisjanis01 [1:16](https://youtu.be/5E5pj7CB4mM?t=76): Track CTR, conversion rate and cost per play for each ad; here CTR was 1.1% and cost per play about 62 Robux.
- 2023-12-06 Roblox Creators [2:07](https://youtu.be/dXqZC4x2Vn8?t=127): Find struggling players by timing how long most players take each onboarding task and flagging the slow tail.
- 2023-12-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-home-recommendations-conversion-rate-and-recent-icon-updates/2731683): Monitor Home recommendations conversion rate (percent of users who play after viewing on Home) on the Acquisition page, filterable by platform.
- 2023-12-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-home-recommendations-conversion-rate-and-recent-icon-updates/2731683): Check icon-change annotations on acquisition charts to see whether an icon update affected impressions or conversion; icon data only exists since Oct 9, 2023.
- 2023-12-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-home-recommendations-conversion-rate-and-recent-icon-updates/2731683): Use the new users funnel (cumulative) table to find which source drives the most new users and which has the highest new-user conversion rate.
- 2023-12-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-home-recommendations-conversion-rate-and-recent-icon-updates/2731683): Compare new vs returning users per source: returning users now have plays and impressions per source charts.
- 2024-01-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lets-teach-an-ai-to-catch-exploiters/2783460): Normalise run lengths to a fixed point count (here 15) via linear interpolation so run duration does not leak into the classifier; choose the count based on your longest run and avoid over-interpolating.
- 2024-02-08 PolarBear [0:41](https://youtu.be/SNauF9OES7E?t=41): Check Creator Dashboard analytics for developer product total sales to measure whether a small ad test paid back.
- 2024-02-28 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/discovery-on-roblox-past-present-and-future-vision/2859111): Use Creator Analytics to track Home recommendation conversion rate, engagement, progression and monetisation trends to find improvements.
- 2024-03-01 MonzterDEV [13:54](https://youtu.be/em15yK5RjBk?t=834): Use Creator Analytics plus off-platform analytics to track engagement, progression and monetization trends and decide what to change.
- 2024-03-08 GameMakers [15:11](https://youtu.be/zvNQHk1hUYc?t=911): Don't decide on data alone: making Temple Run's early obstacles easier cut turn rate and lowered revenue; warning signs before turns improved turn rate about 7%.
- 2024-03-25 PolarBear [3:44](https://youtu.be/bwMUqu-wWpw?t=224): Check the monetization overview after a sponsor run to compare ad spend against Robux earned; here ~5,000 Robux spent yielded ~535 Robux earned.
- 2024-07-11 PolarBear [8:03](https://youtu.be/yYp23GK9RI4?t=483): Check Creator Dashboard revenue and game pass sales to see what monetised; the speaker sold a game to a friend for about 5,000 Robux.
- 2024-07-16 TheDankDeveloper [9:39](https://youtu.be/maRjVb1NAYc?t=579): Check the Creator Dashboard revenue summary and exclude your own purchases to see true earnings from players.
- 2024-07-24 Roblox Creators [1:27](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=87): Track onboarding funnel steps; fixing a big drop-off (e.g. plant-seed step) can raise day-1 retention and session time, which feed discovery.
- 2024-07-24 Roblox Creators [3:13](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=193): Keep sources and sinks balanced: a constantly growing currency means not enough places to spend it; add sinks.
- 2024-07-24 Roblox Creators [4:34](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=274): Watch average wallet balance: spikes mean too few sinks; always at zero means players need more currency.
- 2024-07-24 Roblox Creators [16:10](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=970): Validate events server-side before sending to analytics, since exploiters can fire client events and corrupt charts.
- 2024-07-24 Roblox Creators [14:49](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=889): Send the first funnel step explicitly (not automatic on join); repeated steps collapse, and skipped steps are assumed completed.
- 2024-07-24 Roblox Creators [6:55](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=415): Log economy events after the transaction is saved; pass enum names as strings for transactionType fields.
- 2024-07-24 Roblox Creators [19:56](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=1196): Item SKUs beyond the top 100 group into 'Other'; keep item SKU counts manageable for readable sales charts.
- 2024-07-30 ItsZyonic [4:47](https://youtu.be/Cd6mpD4doGs?t=287): Check like-to-dislike ratio and average session time in the analytics overview; a 3-minute session and 33% likes signalled weak engagement.
- 2024-08-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-new-experience-overview-with-insights-benchmarks-realtime/3108840): Focus on the key metric groups: engagement (average session time, D1, D7 retention), monetization (payer conversion rate, ARPPU), and acquisition (qualified play-through rate).
- 2024-08-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-new-experience-overview-with-insights-benchmarks-realtime/3108840): Compare your metrics to similar experiences via the benchmark scorecard to find which metric is lagging, rather than optimizing everything at once.
- 2024-08-12 GameMakers [19:24](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=1164): Track trailing 30-day currency held by players; adjust liveops sinks or sources if it drifts too high or too low.
- 2024-08-12 GameMakers [52:49](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=3169): Track price and volume of each item, equip rate, depth of spend, and where gacha payers stop; ARPU per live event is the top-line check.
- 2024-08-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791): Only trust playtime and CTR data once you have at least a few thousand daily visits; smaller samples are unreliable.
- 2024-08-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791): Collect good playtime and CTR data early, since Roblox decides how much to promote a game in its first 10-25 days.
- 2024-09-05 CORSO [13:22](https://youtu.be/lFD1wKdwSUs?t=802): Prioritise updates using feature-success analytics; the team focuses on features with the strongest usage data, which is why neglected systems fall behind.
- 2024-09-06 PineappleDev [0:42](https://youtu.be/UBs1bebPu0s?t=42): Check earnings in Creator Dashboard monetization overview with custom date range, split by gamepasses, developer products and engagement (premium) payouts.
- 2024-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-track-any-custom-event-you-want-and-use-custom-fields-for-segmentation/3163688): Track custom events for UI clicks, player preferences, and game balance (e.g. win/loss ratio per class) that don't fit funnel or economy categories.
- 2024-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-track-any-custom-event-you-want-and-use-custom-fields-for-segmentation/3163688): Use funnel events for sequential actions like onboarding, store purchase, and level progression; use them to measure step-to-step conversion.
- 2024-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-track-any-custom-event-you-want-and-use-custom-fields-for-segmentation/3163688): Use economy events for actions that change in-game currency flow, such as gaining or spending currency.
- 2024-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-track-any-custom-event-you-want-and-use-custom-fields-for-segmentation/3163688): Use custom fields (up to three) to break down funnel, economy, and custom event charts by segment, e.g. level, player class, or weapon type.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [7:40](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=460): Use Roblox's native analytics: retention, engagement, acquisition, revenue, plus economy events (top sinks, average wallet balance) and funnel events for onboarding drop-off.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [10:25](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=625): Track win/loss ratio per wave/difficulty: keep it low for hard modes and around 3-4 for easy modes to tune difficulty.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [11:06](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=666): Generate heat maps from in-game placement events to see where players build and rebalance or redesign maps accordingly.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [1:22](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=82): Use the Experience Overview benchmarks; focus on metrics below benchmark (session time, D1 retention, payer conversion, QPTR) first.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [9:42](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=582): Follow the user journey from join to drop-off to find what to fix; Walmart Discovered found confusion causing 3.8-min session time versus benchmarks.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [14:45](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=885): If payer conversion or ARPPU is below benchmark, check the economy: if sources exceed sinks and wallets keep rising, add items or improve shop UX.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [30:11](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=1811): Push below-benchmark KPIs above the 50th percentile; that move drives the most extra surfacing.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [34:27](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=2067): Console users tend to be more retentive, monetize better and are older; optimize for console and desktop to reach a complementary cohort.
- 2024-10-24 GameMakers [15:31](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=931): Without fingerprinting, survey opted-in users and apply their results to the cohort; use one consistent methodology across all channels so they compare apples to apples.
- 2024-10-24 GameMakers [21:02](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1262): Model performance at campaign and creative level even with imprecise data; blended ROAS alone doesn't support granular budget decisions.
- 2024-10-24 GameMakers [23:51](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1431): Pick a frequently firing optimization event (e.g. tutorial complete or CPA) rather than sparse revenue events when signal is limited.
- 2024-10-28 waveshine [0:45](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=45): Check Creator Hub > your game > Overview; aim for green or yellow bars and fix each red one individually.
- 2024-11-07 GnomeCode [9:44](https://youtu.be/g9UbIrR0RbU?t=584): Calculate ad cost per new player and compare it to your actual revenue per player; a game with low monetisation loses money on each ad-acquired player.
- 2024-11-20 ByteBlox [18:09](https://youtu.be/Jl0SdXksQsI?t=1089): Estimate revenue as sessions times average session length times price per interval. Use real playtime data, not guesses, before pricing anything.
- 2024-11-28 Suphi Kaner [2:23:21](https://youtu.be/SVLFkdeh6c8?t=8601): Benchmark plugin cost with os.clock: analysing a 500-line script took about 0.06s, and module updates about 0.005s.
- 2025-02-04 GameMakers [4:20](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=260): Decide the KPI before designing a live event. It may be retention rather than revenue; the answer changes the event design.
- 2025-02-04 GameMakers [8:21](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=501): Track all KPIs, not just the target one. Pushing revenue can silently hurt retention, so balance and prioritise per game stage.
- 2025-02-04 GameMakers [10:26](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=626): Set up analytics and a data lake you own before spending on UA. Don't rely only on MMP or store dashboards; verify data consistency first.
- 2025-02-04 GameMakers [12:52](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=772): Find the biggest bottleneck first: store conversion, ad impressions/eCPM, or a broken purchase economy. Fix that before incremental tweaks.
- 2025-03-08 GnomeCode [0:40](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=40): Map your game as ordered funnel steps (join, lobby, elevator, teleport, each checkpoint) and log each with AnalyticsService:LogOnboardingFunnelStepEvent; best for linear games.
- 2025-03-08 GnomeCode [2:42](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=162): Log funnel steps server-side from the point where the player actually progresses, e.g. just before teleport logic, so drop-offs reflect real progress.
- 2025-03-08 GnomeCode [4:23](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=263): Expect ~24 hours before funnel charts appear; check View Events first to confirm events are arriving.
- 2025-03-08 GnomeCode [6:17](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=377): Look for big red drops in step completion; they point to the exact spot to fix, such as a hard encounter or bug.
- 2025-03-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-with-the-improved-recommended-for-you-algorithm-and-analytics-for-creators/3587441): Check Analytics > Acquisition > Home Recommendations tab; signal changes usually show in impressions a few days later, so compare signal dips to impression drops with a lag.
- 2025-03-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-with-the-improved-recommended-for-you-algorithm-and-analytics-for-creators/3587441): Treat signals below benchmark as opportunities; if 7-day playtime and play days are low, improve the onboarding funnel to bring players back.
- 2025-03-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-with-the-improved-recommended-for-you-algorithm-and-analytics-for-creators/3587441): Most signals update within 24 hours in analytics and the algorithm, so you can measure the effect of changes quickly.
- 2025-03-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-with-the-improved-recommended-for-you-algorithm-and-analytics-for-creators/3587441): Account for seasonality (weekly peaks on Saturdays, summer/back-to-school, holidays) before blaming your game for an impression drop.
- 2025-04-16 Roblox [20:05](https://youtu.be/vTc2wciWukE?t=1205): Use the creator dashboard time-series for metrics in the ranking objective, such as co-play and friend invites, to see which actions move your discovery.
- 2025-04-19 Crusherfire [0:40](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=40): Check Developer Console network stats first; only consider buffers if incoming data consistently exceeds about 50 KB/s.
- 2025-04-19 Crusherfire [15:47](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=947): Use the Packet Profiler plugin to measure exact packet sizes before and after serialization to confirm savings.
- 2025-04-28 AlvinBlox [4:50](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=290): Focus on session length, retention, and monetisation, the metrics Roblox's algorithm uses to judge games.
- 2025-04-28 AlvinBlox [4:50](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=290): Track session length, retention and monetisation; the speaker says Roblox's algorithm judges games on these three.
- 2025-05-02 Duckable [11:25](https://youtu.be/Wc8wjiy1oYk?t=685): Read dislike and feedback comments closely; the speaker found most dislikes were about unfair boulder knockdowns that did not touch the player.
- 2025-05-11 janisjanis01 [6:21](https://youtu.be/sM9btPAvb28?t=381): Check average view duration and per-video view curves; rising views per video over days signal momentum even when subscribers lag.
- 2025-05-18 Arow [0:56](https://youtu.be/_fVivJ4DtsA?t=56): Check the Economy, Demographics, and Retention tabs in Creator Dashboard to see in-game currency, where players are, and whether retention beats average.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [30:45](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1845): Check D1, D7 and D30 retention in Creator Analytics after running Drive Retention campaigns to measure their effect.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [50:08](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=3008): Ads Manager Robux earnings attributed to a campaign keep updating for 30 days; check ROI at day 30, not day 1.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [50:50](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=3050): Treat Ads Manager reporting as source of truth for ad impact; Creator Analytics blends all sources, so it understates ads.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [57:57](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=3477): If players join and leave immediately, the quality fix should show up in rising 7-day and 30-day retention and Robux spend; watch those rather than plays alone.
- 2025-06-15 janisjanis01 [14:29](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=869): Compare visits, average play time (14.5 min) and likes; low like ratio with decent visits signals a quality problem to diagnose.
- 2025-06-21 Quez [1:32](https://youtu.be/WOG3Rttf-V4?t=92): Benchmark ARPDAU against genre peers; this game's was low versus the simulation genre benchmark, signalling monetisation headroom.
- 2025-06-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-creator-rewards-earn-more-by-growing-the-community/3777628): Track Daily Engagement, Audience Expansion and Share Link Analytics in Creator Analytics to see which actions drive earnings.
- 2025-06-24 SmartyRBX [11:27](https://youtu.be/skcp4P68i84?t=687): Read your analytics after release: watch where players drop off, which gamepasses sell, and when players stop returning (e.g. after 7 days).
- 2025-06-26 RoBuilder [1:43:15](https://youtu.be/krMTcTvaj_8?t=6195): Set a realistic CCU target for niche games; the streamer targets roughly 300-500 players for a niche motorcycle game.
- 2025-06-29 janisjanis01 [12:57](https://youtu.be/5LMGVfslAYs?t=777): Check traffic sources and monetization tabs to attribute players and Robux to Shorts promotion.
- 2025-07-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-track-new-user-retention-by-daily-and-weekly-cohort/3817030): Use the daily/weekly new-user cohort table to check whether big events or holidays brought in players who retain as well as your typical cohorts.
- 2025-07-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-track-new-user-retention-by-daily-and-weekly-cohort/3817030): Check the New User Funnel tab for 7D playtime per user, 7D payer conversion, and 7D/30D revenue per user to see if event cohorts monetise better.
- 2025-07-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-track-new-user-retention-by-daily-and-weekly-cohort/3817030): Read D1, D7 and D30 charts on the same date as one cohort: they now use first-play date, so the same date shows the same players over time.
- 2025-07-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-track-new-user-retention-by-daily-and-weekly-cohort/3817030): Track DAU/MAU stickiness; a declining ratio means new players aren't forming daily habits, so improve onboarding, use experience events, or add engagement hooks.
- 2025-07-21 SmartyRBX [3:45](https://youtu.be/exOJsFcer1Q?t=225): Read funnel signals: if players join but don't buy gamepasses, examine why the pass is unappealing and revise or add passes.
- 2025-07-27 janisjanis01 [12:14](https://youtu.be/Sgs36l6K5EU?t=734): Track visits, active players and Robux earned against ad spend; a game with ~1 active player and ~1,000 Robux lifetime revenue did not cover ad spend.
- 2025-08-07 PineappleDev [2:34](https://youtu.be/tsmsTtrlT8Y?t=154): Check Monetization Overview and Engagement/Creator Rewards together; total earnings include all three, not just gamepass sales.
- 2025-08-15 Roblox Creators [17:08](https://youtu.be/KSez8Ecq50A?t=1028): Use the creator analytics page to dig into trends. Break sales down by gender, age and where they sell, and check the catalog avatar creator share.
- 2025-08-15 Roblox Creators [17:49](https://youtu.be/KSez8Ecq50A?t=1069): Renaming an underperforming item to match a current pop-culture trend (K-pop) revived its sales. Test renames when an item stalls.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [37:16](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=2236): Watch opt-in rate and impression rate: low opt-in means players can't find the ad or don't find the reward compelling.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [38:24](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=2304): Check downstream impact: engagement, playtime and retention in normal creator analytics alongside ad metrics; any negative effect is a warning sign.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [30:34](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1834): Treat unvalidated ad data as provisional; it is still being debotted for a couple of days after events.
- 2025-09-05 Roblox [46:33](https://youtu.be/6iB5__RCQDc?t=2793): A/B testing breaks at scale: at about 0.5-1M players, analytics queries became too expensive and data unreliable; Grow a Garden switched to player-feedback-driven decisions.
- 2025-09-05 Roblox [43:33](https://youtu.be/6iB5__RCQDc?t=2613): Use built-in experiments in Creator Hub and Studio to run A/B tests that control for confounding variables when changing things serially.
- 2025-09-13 bubDEV [0:37](https://youtu.be/kg_h09_x7no?t=37): Judge a new game by four signals: long session time, players returning, players spending, and enough click appeal. Aim to make all four green.
- 2025-09-13 bubDEV [1:36](https://youtu.be/kg_h09_x7no?t=96): Check a game's analytics a couple of days after release. Read each of the four signals as green (good) or red (bad) to decide whether it has viral potential.
- 2025-09-21 janisjanis01 [12:31](https://youtu.be/uNLDi_GO2MA?t=751): Check monetisation and retention stats after 72 hours; creator found only 31 Robux earned in that window and used this to diagnose the flop.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [4:40](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=280): Multi-device users reportedly spend over 2.5x more per hour and engage over 20% longer, so cross-platform support is a revenue lever.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [3:46](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=226): Test matchmaking configs on a subset of players with the experimentation platform, measuring retention and play time before full rollout.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [29:37](https://youtu.be/YgyfcMfxQcI?t=1777): Watch analytics, YouTube videos and new-player sessions to see the first-time experience; designers lose fresh perspective over time.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [35:36](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=2136): Do not optimise the six metrics individually; build a fun game and monitor them. Qualified playthrough rate was the best growth metric for Ninja Challenge.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [6:13](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=373): Only call a result when the confidence interval excludes zero; more sample size shrinks intervals and makes results precise.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [8:22](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=502): Run experiments longer and at larger rollout to detect small lifts (e.g. 1% D1 retention) instead of only 5%+.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [9:03](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=543): Don't act on day-one results; wait until metrics are static (ideally ~2 weeks) to avoid novelty effects.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [9:45](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=585): When metrics trade off, decide by the dominant gain; ramp to 100% only once the key metric is clearly and statistically up.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [14:09](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=849): Let tests run until stat-sig, including long-horizon metrics like D7 retention; Brookhaven ran 28 testing days total with two public playtests.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [20:10](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=1210): If results don't make sense, investigate or run another test rather than trusting the surprising number.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [29:51](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=1791): Starter-pack tests on new-user spenders can take over a month to reach stat-sig; don't react to day-1 or day-2 swings.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [20:53](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=1253): Check client crash rate in creator analytics after each publish; a jump from 3-4% to 45% signals a bad build.
- 2025-10-06 Crusherfire [2:14](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=134): Check Performance > Server > Server memory by age; if the script segment climbs steadily and never drops, you likely have a server memory leak.
- 2025-10-06 Crusherfire [4:03](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=243): Take Lua heap snapshots only in Studio playtests; snapshotting on a live server with players causes lag.
- 2025-10-06 Crusherfire [9:06](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=546): Compare snapshots: red means memory increased, green means decreased. Focus on items that keep rising while returning to baseline.
- 2025-10-11 janisjanis01 [15:56](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=956): Add badges to track where players die or fail (e.g. day milestones) to find the exact drop-off point instead of guessing difficulty.
- 2025-10-11 janisjanis01 [16:18](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=978): Judge CTR and retention separately: high CTR with near-zero Robux showed the problem was after the click, in the game itself.
- 2025-10-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-retention-by-acquisition-source-and-select-your-benchmark-set/4010157): Break D1/D7/D30 retention down by acquisition source to check whether an overall drop is caused by a shift in traffic mix (e.g. more 'Other' external-link users).
- 2025-10-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-retention-by-acquisition-source-and-select-your-benchmark-set/4010157): Use the Home recommendation signals analytics to diagnose organic distribution, not ad performance, since ads don't affect Home recommendation distribution.
- 2025-10-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-retention-by-acquisition-source-and-select-your-benchmark-set/4010157): Benchmarks (similar experiences vs genre) are for comparison only and don't change recommendation impressions; the scorecard will move to 7-day averages to smooth daily noise.
- 2025-10-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-understand-the-success-of-your-experience-events/4019126): For pre-post event analysis, compare 7 days before, the event period, and 7 or 14 days after the event.
- 2025-10-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-understand-the-success-of-your-experience-events/4019126): Judge events by goal: track ARPDAU and revenue for monetization; DAU and returning users for engagement and reactivation.
- 2025-10-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-understand-the-success-of-your-experience-events/4019126): Instrument funnels and custom events to track how users progress through an event.
- 2025-10-30 Roblox Creators [8:37](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=517): When impressions drop, check four factors: your own updates, Roblox algorithm changes, overall platform user volume/seasonality, and competitors improving their signals.
- 2025-10-30 Roblox Creators [32:07](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=1927): Expect a spike in new RFY users after high retention can lower KPIs temporarily, since new cohorts may be less engaged.
- 2025-10-30 Roblox Creators [11:32](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=692): Use the Creator Analytics comparison benchmarks (similar experiences or same genre) but note benchmarks are not used in ranking.
- 2025-11-05 Last Level Studios [20:22](https://youtu.be/KNHngRRMPVI?t=1222): Check the new safety analytics in the developer Analytics page: report-abuse breakdowns (exploiting, profanity, bullying) and comparison to the 90th percentile of games.
- 2025-11-06 VuukStudios [15:28](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=928): Watch average playtime (aim 10-15 min minimum, 25+ min is excellent) and day-7 retention, which Roblox now weights for home page recommendations.
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385): Define a clear cause-and-effect hypothesis with expected metric direction and reasoning before running an experiment, e.g. grow D1 retention via onboarding change.
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385): Check the minimum detectable effect (MDE) before running. MDE around ±100% means significance is unlikely; very low MDE (<0.01%) means a shorter test or less traffic works.
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385): Games under 500 DAU likely need to grow their user base before running experiments; use qualitative feedback instead.
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385): Wait for the full pre-defined experiment duration (at least two weeks suggested) before deciding; early swings can be novelty effects.
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385): Only decide on statistically significant (shaded) metrics; treat non-significant changes as no change, even if the lift looks large.
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385): Expect metric tradeoffs; weigh statistically significant positive vs negative signals and secondary metrics that shift with the primary goal.
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385): Avoid running overlapping experiments on the same features; users can be enrolled in both and interaction effects can distort results.
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385): Document setup, learnings, decisions and next steps for each experiment to build a body of knowledge.
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385): Test config changes in Studio before publishing; published changes reach all players within 5 minutes (or 15-minute rollout optionally).
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/custom-matchmaking-is-now-available-to-all-experiences-on-roblox/4051419): Preview weight changes against mock data in the Custom Matchmaking page to confirm matching behaves as intended before publishing.
- 2025-11-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rewarded-video-ads-are-now-available-to-all-ads-eligible-creators/4063278): Use the experimentation platform to test ad impact on ARPU, ARPPU and payer conversion. Those revenue metrics exclude ad revenue.
- 2025-11-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rewarded-video-ads-are-now-available-to-all-ads-eligible-creators/4063278): Use the new reporting filters on R$ EPM and fill rate, plus per-placement breakdowns, to tune ad placement.
- 2025-11-16 JustVeryYellow [8:11](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=491): Check the analytics tab for average session time, DAU and returning vs new players after an ad burst; the spike faded about two days after the ads ended.
- 2025-12-01 Roblox Creators [14:08](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=848): Check minimum detectable effect before launching an experiment; if the required effect is too high you won't measure anything.
- 2025-12-01 Roblox Creators [14:32](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=872): Only trust statistically significant (shaded) metrics; non-significant ones can be positive or negative per the confidence interval.
- 2025-12-01 Roblox Creators [12:32](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=752): If an experiment is non-significant across all metrics, go with informed intuition, but don't ramp a change that is significantly negative.
- 2025-12-01 Roblox Creators [13:13](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=793): Monetisation and D1 retention metrics are noisier with wider intervals; run monetisation experiments longer or with larger samples.
- 2025-12-01 Roblox Creators [17:34](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=1054): Use 2-3 variants rather than many fine-grained ones; splitting users into tiny buckets makes significance nearly impossible.
- 2025-12-01 Roblox Creators [20:18](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=1218): Run experiments rather than guessing; without them you can't tell if a metric change came from your update or from a weekend or holiday.
- 2025-12-01 Matt The Method [1:35](https://youtu.be/MPHPumDvMIs?t=95): Track average play time first; creator says Roblox promotes games that keep kids on platform longer, and low playtime kills traffic.
- 2025-12-03 {EISABOOS} "RBX" [5:35](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=335): Check analytics weekly and improve gradually across a 60-day plan.
- 2025-12-12 Poke [9:42](https://youtu.be/VSVCfNHK2IE?t=582): Watch like-to-dislike ratio early; 7 likes vs 17 dislikes (29% like rate) signalled problems that needed fixing before scaling spend.
- 2025-12-24 1_Bruce [7:44](https://youtu.be/wKI3PBO3bB0?t=464): Strong day-1 and day-7 retention and session time did not guarantee success; discovery luck also matters, so do not over-trust stats alone.
- 2026-01-28 bubDEV [4:21](https://youtu.be/2lPsxPtj2mM?t=261): Check stats and analytics to judge whether your game has a good chance of growing in the Roblox algorithm.
- 2026-02-04 Roblox Creators [2:56](https://youtu.be/5HcH-9E7USc?t=176): Do not judge a new campaign immediately; wait about 24 hours for the learning/calibration period before reading metrics.
- 2026-02-20 CallMeDogey [1:21](https://youtu.be/IQm6zE2fKUI?t=81): Check the six benchmarks in Creator Hub stats; improve any below the 75th percentile, which the speaker says is the priority over advertising.
- 2026-02-20 1_Bruce [0:59](https://youtu.be/i3RQXxZeVYc?t=59): Track the overview page stats: average playtime, D1 retention, payer conversion rate, qualified playthrough rate, and D7 retention. Keep them green relative to similar games.
- 2026-02-20 1_Bruce [2:19](https://youtu.be/i3RQXxZeVYc?t=139): Benchmark stats against same-genre games, not absolute numbers. QPTR runs lower for adult-targeted games than kids' games.
- 2026-02-20 1_Bruce [3:10](https://youtu.be/i3RQXxZeVYc?t=190): Study your audience demographics. They shape pricing and conversion, and they can make or break a game.
- 2026-02-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-analytics-dimensions-get-insight-into-your-players%E2%80%99-spending-behaviors/4438963): In Monetization > Overview, break the Revenue Chart down by active payer status to see which segments drive revenue.
- 2026-02-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-analytics-dimensions-get-insight-into-your-players%E2%80%99-spending-behaviors/4438963): Review ARPPU, payer conversion, playtime and Retention charts broken down by active payer status to check whether spending is holding steady over time.
- 2026-03-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-we-are-improving-home-this-year/4502571): Check Creator Analytics > Acquisition > Home recommendations for the new Deep play through rate and 7-day qualified play session signals; check Overview tab for 'not interested' counts.
- 2026-03-09 GamesBeat [page](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/): Track acquisition, playtime, retention and monetisation in Roblox analytics after publishing.
- 2026-03-15 1_Bruce [2:30](https://youtu.be/zgq6L4ZzCHs?t=150): Target high-income countries and 18+ audiences; speaker credits this demographic as the main reason for high spending.
- 2026-03-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/voice-chat-is-now-supported-in-servers-of-up-to-100-users/4534031): Monitor performance with the MicroProfiler, Creator Hub performance analytics, and a community bug-report channel after raising server size.
- 2026-04-06 Deconstructor of Fun [8:43](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=523): Track the six Roblox benchmarks: avg playtime per user per day, D1 and D7 retention, payer conversion, ARPPU. Zach aims for the 90th percentile on all.
- 2026-04-12 janisjanis01 [4:58](https://youtu.be/xqHXDYbuF2w?t=298): Check the six algorithm stats in the dashboard before spending more on ads; low stats mean the game will not be pushed further regardless of budget.
- 2026-04-16 Roblox [33:54](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=2034): Have an agent watch CCU and crash-rate charts after updates and trigger automatic rollbacks on major regressions.
- 2026-04-16 Roblox [40:00](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=2400): Use AI to synthesize in-game player feedback at scale into summaries you can act on.
- 2026-04-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ama-publishing-requirements-roblox-kids-select/4580953): Track the Select Eligibility dashboard on Creator Hub; you get notified when the game nears falling below the highly engaged user threshold.
- 2026-04-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-us-18-devex-rate-earn-42-more-on-spend-from-18-us-players/4607091): Track 18+ US earnings as a separate line item in Creator Analytics to see what drives payouts.
- 2026-05-01 LovJak [8:14](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=494): Monitor CCU, session time, crash rate, FPS and server memory in the dashboard while the ad runs; day-7 retention is meaningless before a week has passed.
- 2026-05-01 Duckable [11:15](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=675): Track concurrent players after each update; he saw CCU rise from 25 to a 225 peak within two days of launch before dropping.
- 2026-05-07 rasdev [2:02](https://youtu.be/wGRgITzP18c?t=122): After a traction spike, identify what caused it (game type, packaging, etc.) so you can repeat and replicate it.
- 2026-05-10 janisjanis01 [8:37](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=517): Study player stats and make changes based on them rather than guessing.
- 2026-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258): Check Shift+F2 for Heartbeat/Physics (higher is better), network send/receive, and draw call count (lower is better).
- 2026-05-13 ThePurpleGuy [1:22](https://youtu.be/JXg2ACdk_wM?t=82): Exclude your own test purchases from sales data; the speaker removed two self-tests from his developer product totals.
- 2026-05-13 Tizzy [2:30](https://youtu.be/RQe-MLgY_Zc?t=150): Use a Robux revenue estimator tool to ballpark a competitor's daily earnings; it underreports, so treat as a floor.
- 2026-05-20 Roblox Creators [3:57](https://youtu.be/R6HbXwyuHzY?t=237): Main reason rewarded video underearns is too few ad impressions, not lack of demand. Check impressions first.
- 2026-05-20 Roblox Creators [5:19](https://youtu.be/R6HbXwyuHzY?t=319): Run a test: add rewarded video, then watch retention and playtime to confirm it isn't hurting engagement.
- 2026-05-20 Roblox Creators [5:44](https://youtu.be/R6HbXwyuHzY?t=344): Check opt-in rate; if low or below benchmark, make the ad placement more discoverable.
- 2026-05-20 Roblox Creators [6:25](https://youtu.be/R6HbXwyuHzY?t=385): Track EPM (earnings per thousand ad impressions) as a quality signal, though impressions matter more for earnings.
- 2026-05-23 Tizzy [13:20](https://youtu.be/owMnhg3fn_M?t=800): Don't panic about stat delays; Roblox data updates roughly every 3 days, so judge ads after several days.
- 2026-05-30 Yann [4:46](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=286): Day 1 of ads is a learning phase: do not change creatives or stop ads; watch error logs, funnel drop-offs and live players.
- 2026-05-30 Yann [6:48](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=408): Read the six overview benchmark stats; if all are above the 50th percentile on ads you are in good shape, low stats mean fix the game.
- 2026-06-00 Roblox Newsroom [page](https://about.roblox.com/newsroom/2026/06/optimizing-discovery-great-games-reach-millions-players-roblox): Check the Creator Analytics dashboard for per-game performance against the published signal list and relative weights to see where your game is strong or weak.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [13:43](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=823): Use at least a 7-day click window for influencer attribution, since creator content lives far beyond 24 hours. Standard UA 24-hour windows undercount it.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [15:05](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=905): Add a proxy uplift model: track organic installs in a market before, during and after a campaign, not just tracked links.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [15:46](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=946): Judge creator campaigns on long-tail installs (day 365 or lifetime), not day 7 or 30 CPI, since tracking links keep converting for years.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [10:59](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=659): Expect roughly 30% organic uplift in test markets, but expect it to vary by game, audience and country. Giveaway-driven campaigns show lower uplift because traffic is already attributed.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [39:03](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2343): Compare verticals on CPM, view-to-install conversion, LTV and retention. Diagnose which metric is failing before changing creators.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [45:14](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2714): Do not judge influencer marketing against UA on a 7-30 day window. Attributed installs are only part of the result, so the comparison is unfair.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [39:03](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2343): Evaluate verticals on CPM, view-to-install conversion, LTV and retention; identify which metric is failing to know what to fix.
- 2026-06-01 MoseyTube [2:41](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=161): Target roughly 2.5% CTR or better and about $0.008 cost per play or better on day one.
- 2026-06-01 MoseyTube [3:56](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=236): From day 5, if retention is poor, stop ads and fix core gameplay; if stats are decent and home-recommendation impressions appear, keep running.
- 2026-06-06 janisjanis01 [12:09](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=729): Track day-7 retention; creator judged their D7 as weak even though overall stats looked good.
- 2026-06-09 rasdev [1:13](https://youtu.be/U9RGcuxt9m4?t=73): Judge success against your own history, not front-page games; 50 concurrent players from zero is a real win.
- 2026-06-10 Tizzy [4:43](https://youtu.be/HFcegpPPvpI?t=283): Treat every update as a hypothesis; form it as 'if I change X, stat Y will improve' before shipping.
- 2026-06-10 Tizzy [9:08](https://youtu.be/HFcegpPPvpI?t=548): Cross-reference qualitative data, quantitative stats and competitor research to strengthen each hypothesis before launching.
- 2026-06-10 Deconstructor of Fun [8:13](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=493): Measure organic uplift by comparing organic installs before, during and after a campaign; the lift is often much larger than the attributed installs.
- 2026-06-10 Deconstructor of Fun [9:36](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=576): Expect organic uplift to vary by campaign, audience and country; giveaway campaigns attribute more directly, so uplift is lower there (~30%) than for non-giveaway posts (up to ~100% of attributed traffic).
- 2026-06-10 Deconstructor of Fun [10:59](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=659): Use at least a 7-day click window for influencer attribution, not the 24-hour window typical of paid UA, because creator content is watched for weeks or months.
- 2026-06-10 Deconstructor of Fun [10:59](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=659): Model organic uplift with a proxy in each market, since attribution alone understates influencer impact and can hide a path to profitability.
- 2026-06-10 Deconstructor of Fun [12:21](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=741): Evaluate influencer campaigns on lifetime or 365-day installs, not day 30; one client saw 50% more installs at day 365 than at day 30.
- 2026-06-10 Deconstructor of Fun [10:18](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=618): Expect organic uplift to vary by campaign; giveaway links pull attributed traffic, so uplift can be ~30% there versus far higher share without giveaways.
- 2026-06-10 Deconstructor of Fun [10:59](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=659): Do not use a 24-hour click window for influencer attribution; use at least a 7-day window plus a before/during/after organic-volume proxy model.
- 2026-06-10 Deconstructor of Fun [12:21](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=741): Judge influencer performance on long horizons: one dataset showed installs at day 365 about 50% above day-30 counts, so day-30 CPI overstates cost.
- 2026-06-10 MoseyTube [4:27](https://youtu.be/esZQX5COI_E?t=267): Track favorites as a retention signal; the creator treats 2,140 favorites as stronger evidence of retention than clicks.
- 2026-06-15 rasdev [0:41](https://youtu.be/7VhmLFkhwPU?t=41): Find your single worst metric in analytics, fix only that, wait a week, then repeat. Don't change several things at once.
- 2026-06-15 rasdev [2:02](https://youtu.be/7VhmLFkhwPU?t=122): Judge retention and monetization against benchmarks for your own genre, not absolute numbers.
- 2026-06-15 Roblox Creators [1:22](https://youtu.be/K7jsk3bzmvE?t=82): Use the Creator Analytics dashboard to compare your game's engagement, retention, return visits, Robux spend and co-play against these signals and fix what lags.
- 2026-06-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/recommended-for-you-algorithm-improvements-that-better-value-long-term-retention/4684575): Check Creator Analytics > Acquisition > Home Recommendations to see the updated signals and their relative importance, and find where your game is weak.
- 2026-06-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/recommended-for-you-algorithm-improvements-that-better-value-long-term-retention/4684575): Use benchmark games only as a comparison point; they do not affect the recommendation algorithm, but low signals against them show opportunities.
- 2026-06-15 Tizzy [4:42](https://youtu.be/zoz3PSMqets?t=282): Track 7-day spend days per user (Acquisition > Home wrecks). Consumables raise it because players can buy again each day.
- 2026-06-15 Tizzy [8:15](https://youtu.be/zoz3PSMqets?t=495): Before adding a product, check how it affects other stats and your progression economy so it doesn't break long-term balance.
- 2026-06-15 Tizzy [14:12](https://youtu.be/zoz3PSMqets?t=852): After each change, check D1, D7, session time and first-time experience. Keep it if it helps; revert if it hurts playtime or retention.
- 2026-06-16 VuukStudios [18:12](https://youtu.be/catPKgFIZbQ?t=1092): Check Statistics > Analytics > Acquisition for a Home Recommendations bar; once it appears, turn off ads and let organic recommendations run.
- 2026-06-18 Roblox Creators [10:25](https://youtu.be/B3R1ybI3yzs?t=625): Check the Acquisition tab in Creator Analytics; bounce rate is a negative signal, so lower is better.
- 2026-06-18 Roblox Creators [16:52](https://youtu.be/B3R1ybI3yzs?t=1012): Use Creator Analytics experimentation to A/B test changes; it controls for competition and seasonality to measure causal impact.
- 2026-06-20 Tizzy [8:43](https://youtu.be/pu2o80prNV8?t=523): Treat the game as a funnel: impression, PTR, bounce, D1, D7, D28; find the biggest leak and fix that stage first.
- 2026-06-20 Tizzy [12:20](https://youtu.be/pu2o80prNV8?t=740): Filter funnels by device type and traffic source; a console-only tutorial bug dragged completion down (77% overall, ~30% on console earlier).
- 2026-06-23 Raul [1:40](https://youtu.be/kKavvUDtHOg?t=100): Check the Monetization overview and Retention/Engagement/Acquisition/Demographics tabs in Creator Dashboard; acquisition data may lag several days after release.
- 2026-06-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-shop-a-personalized-in-game-storefront/4701505): Preview the Shop in Roblox Studio and review it in Creator Hub before launch; if no changes are made, Roblox's suggested categories and listing settings auto-publish.
- 2026-07-05 janisjanis01 [2:45](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=165): Before buying, check income per CCU, not just concurrency. A 100-CCU game with horrible income is a poor buy.
- 2026-07-06 Tizzy [9:17](https://youtu.be/EZzztgpSaTU?t=557): When a stat is weak, focus on that single stat until it improves; the speaker's bounce rate drop from ~21% to ~15% roughly doubled impressions and lifted CCU.
- 2026-07-06 Tizzy [11:37](https://youtu.be/EZzztgpSaTU?t=697): Expect about 24 hours for most data to update, sometimes up to 3 days early in launch; don't judge an update on minutes of data.
- 2026-07-06 Tizzy [12:18](https://youtu.be/EZzztgpSaTU?t=738): Cross-reference multiple data sources (competitor research, onboarding funnel drop-off, engagement, first-session retention) before shipping an update.
- 2026-07-07 Generalist Programmer [1:51](https://youtu.be/Oj0nbY61qsY?t=111): Store coins and rebirths in a leaderstats folder under the player; Roblox renders them on the leaderboard automatically.
- 2026-07-08 rasdev [2:43](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=163): Pick one weak statistic (e.g. CTR or day-1 retention) and fix that specific metric, then update slowly bit by bit.
- 2026-07-08 rasdev [3:24](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=204): Wait at least a week or two after a change before judging results; don't expect a blow-up within a day.
- 2026-07-08 rasdev [4:48](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=288): Use data as a guide: don't ignore it, but don't obsess over it.
- 2026-07-09 LovJak [4:48](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=288): Read 7-day moving averages in ad dashboards; day-one snapshots were poor and improved on day two.
- 2026-07-13 Tizzy [0:42](https://youtu.be/nggNvUILQ1I?t=42): Track D1, average session length and retention cohorts early. Creator watched D1 by traffic source (search ~31%, friends ~27%) to judge intent.
- 2026-07-14 MoseyTube [5:11](https://youtu.be/-PoyiiGhoNo?t=311): Check that a new product doesn't hurt session time and retention before keeping it; remove products that don't work and test alternatives.
- 2026-07-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905): Use Scribe.GetStatus and OnIssue to watch health state (Healthy, Degraded, Outage) and error logs in production.
- 2026-07-17 VuukStudios [7:05](https://youtu.be/Oo3sCq-TQXs?t=425): The audience-reach counter updates about every 2 days, so expect 1-2 weeks to hit 500. Don't buy the fee if home recommendations haven't appeared by about day 5-8.
- 2026-07-17 VuukStudios [9:52](https://youtu.be/Oo3sCq-TQXs?t=592): Play-through rate of 2-3% is very good and 4% is amazing; the speaker's game was around 2.3%.
- 2026-07-23 Yann [1:40](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=100): Check retention against region: a low-spending, lower-income player base limits conversion and ARPPU, so set benchmarks by geography.
- 2026-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/leveling-up-ads-manager-with-new-features/4755120): Open a campaign to review Plays day by day as a time series to spot engagement trends across the campaign flight.
- 2026-07-26 Lnor Roblox [7:02](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=422): Track engaged players per ad dollar and average session length; here ~$117 yielded 80 engaged players with ~5 minute average play.
- 2026-07-28 Tizzy [8:24](https://youtu.be/FnsIL4zPCQg?t=504): Check Engagement > New user first session retention to diagnose onboarding; a healthy target in his example was ~95% at 30s, ~87% at 1 min, ~65% at 5 min.
- 2026-07-28 Tizzy [9:05](https://youtu.be/FnsIL4zPCQg?t=545): Track every micro-step in Funnels (not one chunked step) and slice by device; high tutorial completion can hide poor console or mobile friction.
- 2026-07-30 Roblox Creators [28:58](https://youtu.be/yv3GO5AHwJk?t=1738): Track the funnel: impressions, link clicks or game-name searches, game opens, first session, D1 return, D7 return; find where most players drop.
- 2026-07-31 LovJak [4:46](https://youtu.be/Y602HH_KdOc?t=286): Expect paid traffic to be cold: engagement, playtime and day-1 retention dropped during ads. Judge over a longer window, not daily swings.
- 2026-07-31 LovJak [5:28](https://youtu.be/Y602HH_KdOc?t=328): Track ARRPPU and PTR; he reports ARRPPU 83 and PTR 3.41 (about 80th percentile) as signs of healthy monetisation and discovery.
- 2026-08-02 janisjanis01 [6:47](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=407): Monitor six core stats; a mixed picture is normal early as the algorithm finds the right audience.
- 2026-08-05 Tizzy [5:17](https://youtu.be/TVdqb9O0RMw?t=317): Check Acquisition > Home Recs over the last 7 days and pick one metric (e.g. play days per user, bounce rate, playtime) to target per update.
- 2026-08-06 Raul [5:04](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=304): Expect benchmarks and dashboard plays to lag by a day after launch; wait for data before judging a campaign.
- 2026-08-06 Raul [4:20](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=260): Benchmark CTR on Roblox: 1% is bad, about 2% is average; track clicks against actual plays since they can diverge.
- 2026-08-06 VuukStudios [8:04](https://youtu.be/aw0PmBkakb4?t=484): When conversion is poor, first check whether pricing matches the demographic you actually attract, before blaming the game.
- 2026-08-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-by-building-games-people-want-to-play/4779042): Check Creator Analytics > Acquisition > Home Recommendations and the Overview page; use Benchmark signals to spot low signals as opportunities.
- 2026-08-07 LovJak [6:49](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=409): Check 5-minute retention early; he saw only 22% still playing after 5 minutes and suspected the onboarding or age-rating audience mix was the cause.
- 2026-08-07 RoyalDev [1:43](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=103): Track day-1 retention, average session time and percent still playing at 5 minutes; ignoring these analytics let the failures go unnoticed.
- 2026-08-07 RoyalDev [2:45](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=165): Join your own game and watch new players; the creator saw players walk around and leave, which the stats alone didn't explain.
- 2026-08-12 RoyalDev [3:25](https://youtu.be/LpqqLed1dBU?t=205): Track D1 retention and the share of players still playing after 5 minutes; target over 18% past 5 minutes.
- 2026-08-16 VuukStudios [13:25](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=805): Track average playtime: aim for 10+ minutes during ads and 20+ once on the algorithm. Low playtime predicts a flop.
- 2026-08-16 VuukStudios [14:09](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=849): Once on the algorithm, stats should improve. If they stay flat versus the ad period, change something in the game.
- 2026-08-16 VuukStudios [14:50](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=890): Benchmarks he aims for: D1 retention 12-15%, D7 2-3%, pay conversion and QPTR 2-3%.
- 2026-08-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/actioning-against-bot-accounts-on-roblox/4820656): Expect Creator Hub > Analytics visits and players to drop for affected periods as bots are removed; this is an accuracy correction, not a real engagement loss.
- 2026-08-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/actioning-against-bot-accounts-on-roblox/4820656): Judge engagement against real-user trends over weeks, since concurrent player counts may fluctuate for several weeks during the cleanup.
- 2026-08-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/actioning-against-bot-accounts-on-roblox/4820656): If you see issues or questions about this enforcement, contact Roblox Support rather than attempting to fix it yourself.
- 2026-08-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4828676): Use the Analytics Query API to pull DailyActiveUsers with breakdowns like IsNewUser instead of manually downloading CSVs. Queries are async: submit, then poll for results.
- 2026-08-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/evolving-experiments-and-analytics-segmentation-experiment-targeting/4828462): Break down analytics by new segment dimensions: in-experience activity status, engagement level, platform activity status, new vs. returning, acquisition source.
- 2026-08-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/evolving-experiments-and-analytics-segmentation-experiment-targeting/4828462): Segment experiment results by player traits (country, platform, acquisition source, tenure, U18 vs 18+) to find audiences a change helps or harms.
- 2026-08-25 RoyalDev [1:22](https://youtu.be/6CefFrCFqcI?t=82): Check day-one retention, analytics and session length before spending on ads; poor numbers mean the game loop needs fixing first.
- 2026-08-25 MoseyTube [5:11](https://youtu.be/xIBc5He_sy4?t=311): Check the acquisition tab for friend joins and search clicks; flat numbers signal no word of mouth, which caps algorithm promotion.
- 2026-08-26 VuukStudios [5:28](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=328): Watch where users quit in your game and log a board of likely pain points to fix.
- 2026-08-26 VuukStudios [7:30](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=450): Check Create > Analytics > Acquisition > Home recs. If no home recommendations after the ad run, cut the game and pivot, and stop ads.
- 2026-08-26 VuukStudios [8:52](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=532): After getting some home recs, ship one major targeted update (e.g. retention fix or better 5-min tutorial) and check whether home recs grow exponentially.
- 2026-08-28 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4836039): Use MessagingService dashboard topic breakdowns and filters to debug published and received messages live.
- 2026-09-00 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208): Journey analytics (Late 2026) uses Sankey diagrams with custom non-linear funnels to compare conversion across branches and find drop-offs.
- 2026-09-00 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208): Analytics agent (Late 2026) answers plain-language questions about your game metrics with recommended next steps.
- 2026-09-01 VuukStudios [0:40](https://youtu.be/2Q4NqQikzsY?t=40): Track hourly spend and peak Robux on the stats page, and use revenue sources and DevEx figures to judge performance.
- 2026-09-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-custom-dashboards-to-save-organize-and-share-custom-charts/4844585): Build a custom dashboard per game in Analytics > Custom Dashboards to keep key metrics in one view instead of rebuilding charts each login.
- 2026-09-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-custom-dashboards-to-save-organize-and-share-custom-charts/4844585): Publish dashboards to save them; unpublished changes are lost when you leave the page.
- 2026-09-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-custom-dashboards-to-save-organize-and-share-custom-charts/4844585): Use summary cards for top-of-dashboard KPIs such as daily revenue, D1 retention and peak CCU, choosing average, most recent or cumulative summarization.
- 2026-09-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-custom-dashboards-to-save-organize-and-share-custom-charts/4844585): Add charts directly from Explore via Add to custom dashboard, so exploratory analysis can be kept for daily monitoring.
- 2026-09-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-custom-dashboards-to-save-organize-and-share-custom-charts/4844585): Pin the dashboards you check daily to the left nav for one-click access.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880): Use the Observability Platform (late 2026) to trace client sessions and logs and get troubleshooting insights from Studio Build.
- 2026-09-12 LovJak [4:48](https://youtu.be/VHdyHvHOId8?t=288): Remove features that worsen stats: the creator removed an ASMR update after retention and engagement numbers got worse.
- 2026-09-13 VuukStudios [8:04](https://youtu.be/eS0Vc7h0VW0?t=484): Use early home-recommendation traffic and peak CCU during ad runs to estimate your likely CCU cap (e.g., ~5–6 CCU peak implies ~10–15 peak).
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [43:09](https://youtu.be/3a2RbLOAl4A?t=2589): Use the Build stats and analytics agent to view DAU, playtime and retention and drill into metrics from mobile.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [17:19](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=1039): Use custom dashboards to define your own metrics and share one source of truth across your team (early access sign-up at community booths).
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [18:54](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=1134): Ask the upcoming Creator Hub analytics agent questions in plain language for expert advice; agents will also automate config, metadata and event setup.
- 2026-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208): Plan to use the analytics agent (plain-language questions) and journey analytics Sankey funnels to find drop-off at choice points (Late 2026).
- 2026-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208): Expect Creator Hub analytics alerts and custom analytics dashboards; age group analytics will switch to age-checked groupings from self-reported data.
- 2026-09-20 Possible Panda [5:58](https://youtu.be/KVmPyoOPmRI?t=358): Pass an economy analytics transaction type and item SKU when changing currency so economy flows get logged automatically.
- 2026-09-21 RoyalDev [0:42](https://youtu.be/e953Nvhpdjo?t=42): Use Creator Hub Analytics > Funnels to see exactly which step players drop off at, instead of guessing why the game is failing.
- 2026-09-21 RoyalDev [2:05](https://youtu.be/e953Nvhpdjo?t=125): Define funnel steps for linear flows like tutorials; log each step so you can spot where players stop (e.g. step 4 after steps 1-3 succeed).
- 2026-09-21 RoyalDev [4:08](https://youtu.be/e953Nvhpdjo?t=248): Check the Output window while testing; it prints when each funnel event fires to confirm the step is logged correctly.
- 2026-09-24 RoyalDev [0:01](https://youtu.be/Nqcba6pLfXY?t=1): Use funnels on tutorials and starting systems to find where players drop off and which steps they skip.
- 2026-09-24 RoyalDev [1:22](https://youtu.be/Nqcba6pLfXY?t=82): Call AnalyticsService:LogOnboardingFunnelStepEvent(player, stepNumber, stepName) with the same step names across calls so the funnel graph lines up.
- 2026-09-24 RoyalDev [1:22](https://youtu.be/Nqcba6pLfXY?t=82): Log step 1 on Players.PlayerAdded to measure how many players join versus reach later steps.
- 2026-09-24 RoyalDev [2:44](https://youtu.be/Nqcba6pLfXY?t=164): Each funnel step only logs once per player, so repeated touches do not inflate counts.
- 2026-09-24 RoyalDev [3:28](https://youtu.be/Nqcba6pLfXY?t=208): If a player skips a step, the funnel assumes earlier steps were completed; drop-off is only recorded when a later step never fires.
- 2026-09-26 Seibo [4:54](https://youtu.be/NqW98hvXUhc?t=294): Benchmark first-session retention against your genre; creator's 71% was judged excellent by Gemini, but verify against Roblox benchmarks.
- 2026-09-27 janisjanis01 [10:02](https://youtu.be/WJpNCB5J9bg?t=602): Judge a game by player count stability and retention over a week, not by one-off spikes from ads.
- 2026-10-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/experiments-catch-experiment-regressions-faster-with-early-harm-detection/4907734): Check Creator Hub > Experiments > Metrics during the first 24 hours to see 5-minute updates for harm monitoring.
- 2026-10-02 rasdev [4:49](https://youtu.be/Weui21kVEIY?t=289): Use a game-earnings tracker (the speaker's Reblox tool) to see how much money competing games make before choosing an idea.
- 2026-10-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ads-manager-rdc-recap-earnings-roas-reporting-incrementality/4909790): Run an incrementality study (Plays objective only) by survey to measure true ad lift versus organic plays.
- 2026-10-09 Alex Builds Games [12:19](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=739): Run funnel-based revisions to improve early drop-off; he credits this with the recommendation jump.
- 2026-10-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ability-to-view-lifetime-ugc-sales/4922542): Track item-level sales per catalog in Creator Hub Marketplace Analytics; the dashboard only covers selected reporting periods, so export or log numbers regularly for long-term comparisons.
- 2026-10-11 rasdev [11:14](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=674): Ad data takes up to 48 hours and plays are credited up to 30 days later; do not judge a campaign on its first afternoon.
- 2026-10-11 rasdev [11:56](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=716): When players spike, identify the update or change responsible, then do more of what worked; the speaker suggests using an AI assistant to analyze it.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [10:05](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=605): Use Ctrl+Shift+F6 in Studio to open the server authority visualizer; check misprediction rate and predicted frames ahead.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [6:50](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=410): Check the abuse report analytics dashboard for daily takedown counts, violation reasons, and comparison with similar games.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [16:28](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=988): Use the single-player Play Test Agent (beta) to describe test cases in text and let an agent run them, instead of manually replaying the same flow.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [1:55](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=115): Set real-time alerts on client frame rate, crash rate and play time with static thresholds or period-over-period change detection, posted to your team's chat channel.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [3:17](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=197): After each release, check error reports filtered to the newest version; every error shows first-seen timestamp and version to identify what the update introduced.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [4:39](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=279): Test risky changes on a small segment (e.g. new players only) and compare session length and D1 retention against other users before a full launch.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [6:01](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=361): Use calculated metrics across multiple custom events (e.g. wins divided by match starts for win rate) and save them to shared custom dashboards.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [6:41](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=401): Revisit custom dashboards periodically; metrics tracked six months ago may no longer answer current questions as audience and game change.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [17:53](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=1073): When a metric drops, slice by device tier until you find the smallest affected segment; a drop only on low-memory Android points to a resource problem, not game design.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [18:33](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=1113): Check timing first: a drop that starts exactly when you publish is likely tied to that update, so inspect that update's diff.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [19:15](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=1155): Do not trust total aggregate numbers alone; slice and dice to narrow down the cause of a metric change.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [9:06](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=546): Check your weakest top-group signal in Creator Dashboard home recommendations tab and fix it first; benchmarks compare to similar games.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [14:29](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=869): Track funnels, economy events and custom events, not just D1 retention and playtime, to find where onboarding loses players.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-simpler-path-to-publish-plus-invite-early-testers/4930524): Track Highly Engaged Player progress in Creator Hub's Audience Reach tab; counts now update hourly, so a suddenly popular game can reach Kids/Select promotion within hours.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-discovery-measurement-in-creator-analytics/4930122): Don't compare metrics across the Oct 12, 2026 boundary; compare periods before and after separately when reading trends.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-discovery-measurement-in-creator-analytics/4930122): Expect PTR to dip slightly after the change because impressions rise more than plays; this is measurement, not player behaviour.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [8:17](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=497): Check first-10-minute drop-off for simple blockers (missing button, loading screen), then drop-off per world; a late big drop-off signals a content problem.
- 2026-10-14 Tizzy [8:37](https://youtu.be/_JyiKfNNqls?t=517): Build progression funnels for milestones like first treadmill and first plot upgrade. Here only ~60% bought treadmill 1 and 26% upgraded plot, signalling price or timing issues.
- 2026-10-14 MoseyTube [4:22](https://youtu.be/tao925_ypVg?t=262): Compare click-through rate between thumbnail variants; CTR is the metric to decide the winner.
- 2026-10-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-october-12-18-2026/4938638): Expect higher impressions and plays and a small PTR decrease from the Oct 12, 2026 discovery measurement update; historical data is unchanged, no action needed.
