- 2026-10-17 rasdev [5:19](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=319): After an update, watch the baseline a week later, not the spike, and try to keep that baseline as high as possible.

- 2026-10-20 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/home-thumbnail-a-b-test/4947121): Cross-check platform-reported thumbnail metrics against an independent funnel (e.g., Home Recommendation qualified users) to detect data drops that aren't caused by your own changes.
- 2026-10-23 tef [14:28](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=868): Track visits against a daily goal (speaker used 500 visits/day) and active players; the speaker cited 250 active players as the threshold for targeting under-16 audiences.
- 2026-10-26 Deconstructor of Fun [42:37](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2557): Attribution is probabilistic now; combine MMP data (AppsFlyer, Adjust, Singular) with platform reporting rather than relying on last-click.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/error-reporting): Get error message hashes by querying the ErrorCount metric broken down by the MessageHash dimension before calling log-details.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails): Judge thumbnails by Qualified Play Through Rate (qualified plays ÷ home page impressions), not impressions alone, and check Average Playtime per qualified play.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-events): Read update stats: Views, Visit rate (visits from notification), Unfollow rate. Watch unfollow rate as a negative signal.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-events): Read event attribution: check GameJoinContext EventId in PlayerAdded to identify event participants and personalize their experience.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types): Log economy events only after a successful operation (e.g. after a purchase succeeds, not when attempted), since failed attempts create misleading data.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types): Use custom fields to break down values instead of creating separate currencies, e.g. one XP currency with class as Custom Field 1, to conserve currency cardinality.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types): Use the View Events tool on Economy, Funnel, or Custom pages to check near real-time event lists while charts take about 24 hours to appear.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types): Check your game's error report to catch errors in event tracking.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types): Plan for rate limits: AnalyticsService allows 120 + (20 × CCU) requests per minute globally, so scale logging with server population.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads): Check Monetization > Ads metrics after 48 hours of ads running to see views, impressions, teleports, and per-ad-unit Robux earned.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads): Name ad units distinctly in Studio; metrics graphs per ad unit use the Studio instance name.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/monetization): Open Creator Dashboard > Creations > your game > Monetization > Overview to see revenue, conversion, paying users, ARPPU and ARPDAU.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/first-experience): Use the Creator Dashboard analytics for player activity and retention to decide which iterations to make after publishing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics): Set alerts on client crash rate (ClientCrashRate15m) and unexpected OOM exits (OomUnexpectedExits) to catch stability regressions after releases.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics): Use percentile metrics (P10/P50/P90) for client FPS and memory, not just averages, to see how low-end devices perform.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics): Alert on DataStore quota consumption (read/write/list/remove) per DataStoreRequestSource to spot runaway save loops before hitting limits.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics): Filter alerts by Place to scope them to a single experience; Place is filter-only and cannot be used as a split dimension.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics): Use Session time percentiles (P10/P50/P90) alongside peak concurrent players to read engagement by platform and OS.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics): Create an API key with universe.analytics:read on universe-analytics and add your experience under Access Permissions; include it as x-api-key on every request.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics): Standard metrics (engagement, monetization, acquisition, retention) have up to 4 years of history; performance metrics only 28 days.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics): Date ranges over two years only support OneWeek, OneMonth, or None granularity.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics): Use granularity None with breakdown and limit to rank top-N series, then run a second query filtered to those values for a display metric.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics): Funnel steps only appear on days when a user reached them; use a 90-day lookback to discover all steps.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics): Weekly and monthly buckets count distinct users across the period, not a sum of daily values.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics): Check data point status: Projected values may change; NotStatisticallySignificant values are unreliable (e.g. crash rate in small countries).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics): On 429 (data point budget exceeded), shorten the date range, use coarser granularity, or reduce breakdown series with limit.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics): Buckets are UTC-aligned; convert your reporting day to UTC when comparing against dashboard figures.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/learn-explore-sort): Maintain a like ratio above 50% and at least 15 concurrent users to be eligible.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications): Use a new notification string per variant to A/B test; the Analytics tab tracks each string as a separate row.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications): Stats show only after 100 aggregate impressions; per-notification rows appear once they reach 100 impressions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/share-links): Use the Acquisition page to see new users arriving at the game details page via share links and which channel brings the most engaged users.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard): Dashboard KPIs unlock only after the game has more than 10 DAU and 10 play hours for 7 consecutive days.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard): Use Filter By cohorts (in-experience activity status, user engagement) to separate Early, Active, Lapsed and Reactivated players when reading charts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard): Use period-over-period overlays (e.g. 28-day offset) and 7-day moving averages to remove weekly seasonality before judging trends.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard): Compare KPIs against benchmarks; read the 50th-90th percentile range as the spread of similar games, not a single target.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard): Create calculated metrics (e.g. RoundWins / RoundsPlayed, CurrencySources - CurrencySinks) to track difficulty, inflation and feature adoption.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability): Open the Memory Stores dashboard via Creations > game > Monitoring > Memory Stores; you need game owner or analytics group permission.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability): Act on a yellow warning once memory passes 70% of quota within an hour, before it turns into a critical overage.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability): Use the Request Count by Status and Request by API x Status charts to find the ratio of Success to error responses when troubleshooting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability): Dashboard data covers only the past 30 days; earlier custom ranges return a Request Failed error, so export or record older data in time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks): Use the Benchmarks API to compare your metrics against genre, similarity, or top-experience groups rather than judging numbers in isolation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks): Set endTime one day later in Analytics Query API than in Benchmarks API, since Benchmarks endTime is inclusive and Analytics endTime is exclusive.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks): Request BENCHMARK_TYPE_SIMILARITY explicitly for metrics that only have similarity benchmarks, otherwise the series come back empty.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks): If the requested benchmark type has no data, read availableTypes in the response and retry with a supported type.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks): Start and end times must be RFC 3339 midnight UTC; a start time later in the day excludes that day's benchmark value.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks): Benchmarks API returns 400 for benchmarkVariantId on metrics that don't support l7_average; omit it for those metrics.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks): Top-experience benchmarks ignore the percentiles field and return one percentile chosen from your 30-day average value.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks): Keep date ranges within 400 days and at most 10 percentiles per request to avoid 400 errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks): On 429 rate-limit responses, wait and retry at a lower request rate.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks): Generate the API key with your experience in Access Permissions and grant universe.analytics:read on universe-analytics, then send it in the x-api-key header.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/passes): Use pass analytics to view top passes, sales and net revenue trends, and how many buyers came via the Buy Robux page and joined the game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/avatar-items): Use Monetization > Avatar Items > Analytics to compare top selling and top grossing items, net revenue, and buyer age, gender, location and platform.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-planning): Track currency and resource sources and sinks and instrument features so you can see how players interact with them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-planning): Monitor event data hourly and same-day to catch too many prizes or events players can't complete.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-planning): Compare event-period data with the week before and the week after to see whether the event had a lasting effect.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-planning): Judge event success by retention during and after the event and by item and currency sales during and after.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/engagement): Use the New User First Session Retention chart (percent still playing after X minutes, week-on-week) to catch onboarding issues.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/engagement): After a drop in new-user retention, check recent updates for new onboarding steps or changed intro messaging, and check error/performance reports.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-fields): Use custom fields to slice economy, funnel and custom events by game-specific dimensions such as level, class or weapon type.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-fields): Pass custom field values as strings; convert booleans and numbers to string format before logging.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-fields): Only Enum.AnalyticsCustomFieldKeys.CustomField01/02/03 keys are read; any other keys are silently ignored.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-fields): Prefix values with the dimension name (e.g. "Level - 10") to make charts easier to read later.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-fields): Keep combined custom field values under 8,000 unique combinations across the three fields to avoid unwieldy data.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/developer-console): Use the Developer Console's Network tab to inspect web calls, including DataStore and HttpService requests, with status codes, timing and failures.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/feedback): Use the Feedback chart view to spot sudden downvote spikes after an update, then compare sentiment before and after changes to judge their effect.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/feedback): Use the Date Range selector (7/30/60/90 days or custom) and check the relative percentage, which compares the range against the prior equal-length period.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/feedback): Export feedback to CSV from the export button for processing in external tools.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report): Check the Error Report before and after each update to catch regressions early.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report): When a spike appears on the chart, use the error table to troubleshoot, then expand rows for stack traces.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report): Use the 'New errors since' filter to isolate errors introduced by your most recent version.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report): Use platform and OS breakdowns to see whether an error is specific to Desktop, Mobile, Console, or a given OS.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events): Track custom events for metrics default analytics miss, such as UI adoption, ability usage per map, or kill/death ratios per weapon.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events): Send custom events only from the server via AnalyticsService:LogCustomEvent; events can't be sent from the client or Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events): Use custom fields instead of many event names (e.g. one PlantSeed event with plant-type field) because event name cardinality is much tighter.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events): Batch events with values (e.g. 10 zombies killed as one event) to stay under rate limits.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events): Instrument the full core loop (seeds planted, plants watered, shop visits), not just the final action, to find where players drop off or prefer options.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events): Expect up to 24 hours before Explore page charts populate, since events are aggregated daily.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started): Low D1 retention usually signals poor onboarding: players are confused, frustrated, or not reaching the fun fast enough.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started): Low D7 retention often means weak progression; players lack a tangible reason to return a week later.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started): Low D30 retention often indicates missing end-game content or end-game goals for seasoned players.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started): Conversion rate (share of users who ever spend) is one of the most important monetization metrics, since initial purchasers tend to buy again.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started): Low ARPPU often means purchasable item value is not optimized; top payers typically spend several times more than other segments.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started): Compare average session length against your content length after updates or events to gauge how new content was received.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started): Segment by Active Payer Status: if the top 15% of payers contribute much less than ~50% of revenue, improve retention/monetization for top spenders.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started): Segment by When user first played: in mature games, older cohorts should make up a growing share of DAU; declining old cohorts call for new content or events.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started): Follow the loop: analyze metrics, make a targeted change via configs or experiments, monitor results, then repeat; change incrementally.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events): Start funnel tracking with the onboarding flow; it is likely where most users are lost. Log a step after each player completes it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events): Be consistent about when you call LogFunnelStepEvent and use easy-to-interpret step names; call after the player completes the step.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events): Funnel events can only be sent from the server in published games; they cannot be sent from the client or Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events): Use a GUID funnelSessionId for recurring funnels like shop opens within one session; use a item-based key for longer upgrade-path funnels.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events): Validate step numbers received from clients on the server against max step before logging, to keep funnel data clean from exploiters.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events): After editing funnel steps, set the dashboard date range to start at the edit date to see the updated funnel; mixed ranges show a warning.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events): Use custom fields on funnels to compare segments, e.g. starter car choice or map, to find which variant gives better progression.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/slim): Use Developer Console DebugVisualizations > SlimTintMode (LOD or DataModelState) to verify SLIM status; Studio also offers TranscoderStatus while editing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/subscriptions): Track new, renewed, resurrected subscribers and cancellations in the Creator Dashboard Analytics tab; use webhooks for real-time cancelled, purchased, refunded and renewed events.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/analytics): Use the Analytics tab date range (previous day to last 90 days) to compare matchmaking performance across periods.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/analytics): For metrics where lower is better (e.g. Estimate Ping, Age Difference), read P10 as best case and P90 as worst case.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/analytics): Switch the Filter By menu between average, P10, P50 and P90 to avoid being misled by averages skewed by outliers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition): Open Creator Dashboard > your game > Analytics > Acquisition to see new vs returning users by source and the cumulative new-user funnel.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition): Create share links per channel and compare Users with qualified plays, 7D playtime, D7 retention and 30D revenue per user in the New users funnel table.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition): Run an acquisition funnel to find sources that drive the most new users and sources with the best end-to-end conversion rate.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/extended-services): Watch for email alerts at 80% and 100% of budget; decreasing budget below month-to-date usage throttles the service.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing): Read ad performance at three levels: campaign for budget allocation, ad set for targeting and budget effectiveness, individual ad for creative impact.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing): Track CPP (total cost divided by plays) alongside CTR and play rate; play rate is plays divided by impressions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing): Use 7D playtime and 30D Robux earnings per converted player to judge ad traffic quality beyond raw clicks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/obby): Track opt-in rate (eligible users who engage with the ad, DUV/DAU) and daily frequency (views per user) as the two key earnings metrics.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/tools): Track engagement, retention, and monetization KPIs in the Creator Dashboard, which is free of charge.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/modes): Pin Counters mode graphs to watch min and max values of instance count and memory usage over time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques): Track whether players who haven't used a feature (e.g. trade) after two sessions need a timed hint to surface it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques): A/B test hint timing with Experiments (e.g. 5s vs 10s) measuring funnel completion, then push the winner globally via Configs without restarting servers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments): Read operations need universe:read scope; write operations need universe:write, so issue keys with least privilege.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox): Use built-in Roblox analytics for retention, engagement and monetization KPIs; they are free and need no setup.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/shooter): Tag each rewarded video surface with a Placement ID to see which placement drives the most views.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization): Run the cycle: identify issues with built-in tools, improve, then monitor impact with analytics tools.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking): Use matchmaking analytics to track how well each configuration performs and to find optimisation opportunities.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/identify): Use the Performance Dashboard to track client/server memory, client FPS, server heartbeat and crash rates over time in live sessions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/overview): Use Attributes in the Properties window to let team members tweak values at runtime without touching code.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/record-and-display-player-data): Create leaderstats as a Folder under each Player with IntValue/NumberValue/StringValue children so Roblox automatically renders the default leaderboard UI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/best-practices): Monitor quota usage, request volume and response statuses in the Memory Store Observability Dashboard; set custom alerts and use the Error Report.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): Compute minimum detectable effect (MDE) before running; if MDE exceeds ~100% for your goal metric, significance is unlikely. Games under 1,000 DAU may struggle to get useful data.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): Let experiments run their full 14-60 day duration; ending early risks acting on novelty-effect spikes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): Only act on statistically significant results, i.e. where the confidence interval for percent change does not overlap 0%. Ignore non-significant changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): Write a cause-and-effect hypothesis (change, expected effect, reason) before each experiment and keep them with results.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): Stop and restart an experiment if a Sample Ratio Mismatch banner appears; SRM can invalidate results.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): Do not use first-24-hour early-harm metrics for launch decisions; use them only to guide early stopping.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): Use confidence intervals for deep dives and borderline significance; very wide intervals may never reach significance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments): For matchmaking experiments, use 100% rollout; it minimizes risk of isolating players and gives faster, more reliable results.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/video-service): Check the Video Service dashboard (Creator Dashboard > Monitoring) to compare efficient vs inefficient video usage after adding or changing videos.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/observability): Open Creations > Monitoring > Data Stores in Creator Hub to watch DataStore request counts and quota usage; you need owner or analytics group permission.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/observability): Use Request Count by Status and Request by API x Status to find which DataStore API methods are returning errors before players notice data issues.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/observability): Read Write List Remove Quota Usage charts show usage against future per-category limits; check them before launch and growth to avoid throttling.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/observability): Dashboard data from the last 3 minutes may be incomplete, so ignore the drop at the chart end.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/device-trust): Before enforcing a policy, check the Anti-cheat page under Safety in Creator Hub. It estimates DAU, playtime and revenue by trust level on supported platforms.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation): Pass the itemId returned by RegisterItemAsync or GenerateItemListAsync to LogImpressionEvent, not a raw referenceId string.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation): For static cards without video, set impression Duration to 1 to get clean 'user viewed this item' data.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation): Only log play actions when a user passes a satisfaction threshold (e.g. over 60 seconds), not on every Play click.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation): Use Enum.RecommendationActionType.Play for play-session actions; other enums won't register correctly with MaximizePlays.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation): Never log plays from Search, random picks, or past plays as recommendation actions, and never use dummy tracing IDs; the model expects realistic impression-to-play ratios.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation): Track Total Actions, Total Unique Users, Average Items Impressed per User, and Average Time Spent per User in Creator Hub Recommendation Service analytics.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/RTBF-and-creators): Check your inbox daily for Roblox RTBF messages, which list pending requests requiring action and the affected games.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing): Use the Sales page's Top Countries/Regions list to see adjusted regional prices per pass or product.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items): Track CTR (clicks/impressions), CPC, conversion rate (attributed purchases/impressions) and CPP (Robux spent/attributed purchases) to judge sponsored item performance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/optimization/scriptprofiler): Export profiling data as JSON to compare sessions or analyze node and function TotalDuration values outside Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention): Read retention by cohort: x-axis is each user's first play date, so changes made on a given day show up in that day's new-user cohort.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention): Expect D7 and D30 data to be empty for recent dates; D7 appears after 7 days and D30 after 30 days.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention): Use daily and weekly cohort tables to compare users acquired during events or campaigns against earlier users, including 7D playtime, 7D conversion, and 7D/30D revenue per user.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention): Measure progression by granting a special item at each level to see how long players take to reach the next level, then adjust task difficulty.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/leaderboards): Set stat values through .Value (e.g. gold.Value), not by assigning the object directly; stats must be IntValue/NumberValue/StringValue children of leaderstats.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alert/metrics): Set alerts on client crash rate (ClientCrashRate15m) and OOM exits by platform and memory group to catch device-specific stability problems early.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alert/metrics): Use percentile metrics (P10/P50/P90) for frame rate and memory, not just averages, to see how low-end players experience the game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alert/metrics): Use OneMinute granularity for live incident alerts; the longer granularities suit trend monitoring. Data is kept for 28 days only.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq): Check Creator Analytics > Overview and Acquisition > Home Recommendations to see where your game is strong and where to focus.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics): Query retention by segment dimensions (Platform, Country, AcquisitionSource, UserSegmentationPayerStatus) rather than only the blended D1/D7/D30 number.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics): Export performance metrics within 28 days; client and server performance data is only retained for 28 days.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics): Export thumbnail metrics within 30 days; thumbnail data is only retained for 30 days.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics): Use ClientFpsP10 rather than the average to see the experience of your slowest players; P10 captures low-end performance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics): Segment by IsNewUser when reading engagement metrics so new-player behaviour is not diluted by returning players.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance): Monitor the Performance dashboard before and after every update to catch regressions early.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance): Dashboard charts require at least 100 daily active users (DAU) to display.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance): Use Breakdown by Place Version to compare the latest release against earlier versions for lower FPS or more out-of-memory exits.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/users): Group in-game checks (IsInGroupAsync, GetRankInGroupAsync, GetRoleInGroupAsync, PromptJoinAsync) need no user ID handling; Open Cloud group APIs use the group's domain IDs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-essentials): Use analytics and the creator dashboard's performance data and suggestions to find issues and opportunities to target with updates.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts): Use PeriodOverPeriod with periodOffsetMultiplier 7 on OneDay interval to compare each day with the same day last week.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts): Any dimension used in a breakdown must also appear in filter; only one breakdown dimension is allowed per alert.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts): Query incident history with startTime within last 30 days; incidents are retained about 35 days.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/test-on-hardware): Use the Performance Dashboard to track FPS, memory and crash rates by device category after publish; a crash spike after an update suggests a low-end memory regression.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/alerts): Establish a baseline first: review analytics charts over the last 7 to 14 days before setting thresholds, so normal fluctuation does not trigger false alarms.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/alerts): Set alert thresholds above normal levels. Example: if normal crash rate is about 1.2%, set the alert at 2.5% or 3%.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/alerts): Use a duration of 5 (not 1) for volatile metrics like session time; sudden player influxes or asset loads can cause temporary spikes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/alerts): Use the Breakdown feature in one alert (e.g. by platform: Android, iOS, PC) instead of separate alerts per platform; the payload shows where the issue occurs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/alerts): Name alerts with a structured formula like 'metric - context' (e.g. 'High client crash rate - mobile') so issues can be triaged at a glance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads): Estimate earnings with the Eligibility calculator: eligible users (7-day) x ads per user x projected EPM. Daily ad views per user is the main lever you control.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads): Use experiments to roll a new ad placement out to a percentage of users first and measure engagement and retention before full rollout.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/engagement-based-payouts): Use Premium Playtime Score (daily Premium engagement time) for immediate feedback on new features; Premium Playtime Robux Earned aggregates 28 days, so it lags.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/analytics-essentials): Start with the three KPIs (engagement, retention, monetization) to find the highest-level areas for improvement in your game's health.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/insights): Use the 'Explore this metric' button on an overview insight to slice revenue or other metrics by platform, country, and more.
